================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================
<div align="center">

# Abstra

A modern, offline-first Android music player built with Jetpack Compose.

[![Release](https://img.shields.io/badge/release-v2.0.0-blue)](https://github.com/dotHP-harshu/abstra/releases/tag/v2.0.0)
[![Min SDK 24](https://img.shields.io/badge/minSDK-24-green)]()
[![Kotlin 2.2.10](https://img.shields.io/badge/kotlin-2.2.10-7F52FF)]()

</div>

## Features

- **Local library** — browse songs, artists, albums, and folders from on-device storage
- **Swipeable library tabs** — swipe between Songs, Playlists, and Folders with animated indicator
- **Full-screen player** — now playing view with palette-generated album art backgrounds
- **Equalizer & Virtualizer** — live audio effects with adjustable strength
- **Playlists** — create and manage your own collections
- **"Play Next" queue** — long-press any song to play it next, before the current playlist
- **Double-tap previous** — tap previous twice quickly to skip, once to restart track
- **Playback stats** — monthly listening activity charts
- **Foreground playback** — media session support with lockscreen and notification controls
- **Smooth fade on disconnect** — headphones/Bluetooth disconnect fades out over 200ms
- **Built-in procedural tracks** — real-time generated Pookie & Trap Mix audio for when your library is empty
- **Material 3 UI** — adaptive themes and edge-to-edge design

## What's New in v2.0.0 — Major Update [(full changelog)](CHANGELOG.md)

This release introduces **swipeable library tabs**, **palette-based album art theming**, **explicit "Play Next" queue**, and **battery-optimized playback** — all discovered and refined through on-device testing.

### Highlights

- **Swipe between tabs** — Songs, Playlists, Folders now in a horizontal pager with animated indicator
- **Album art colors drive UI** — Full-player background extracts vibrant/muted palette from artwork
- **"Play Next" queue** — Long-press any track → "Play Next" queues it before current context
- **Smart previous button** — Double-tap (1.2s) skips track; single tap restarts if >3s played
- **Smooth audio disconnect** — Bluetooth/headphone unplug fades out 200ms then pauses
- **Marquee text everywhere** — Long titles scroll in mini-player, full-player, playlist detail
- **Instant notification sync** — Play/pause button in notification updates immediately
- **Theme-aware red** — Delete dialogs use each theme's accent color (not hardcoded red)
- **Actual file deletion** — Remove from library now deletes the file from storage

### Build / Toolchain

- `AGP 8.7.3`, `KSP 2.2.10-2.0.2`, `core-ktx 1.13.1`, `compileSdk 35`, `jvmToolchain 17`
- New dependency: `androidx.palette:palette-ktx:1.0.0` for album art color extraction

### Data Safety

- `Room v1→v2` migration with `FK CASCADE` on `playlist_songs`, proper `deletePlaylistWithSongs` transaction
- `refreshLibrary` only deletes stale locals when `cursor != null` (permission denied no longer wipes library)

### Playback Engine

- Dual-tier queue: explicit user queue ("Play Next") + context queue (album/playlist/folder)
- `baseQueue` sync fixed: songs added during shuffle persist when toggling shuffle off
- `@Volatile mediaPlayer`, progress tracker on `Dispatchers.Main` for memory visibility
- `requestAudioFocus` ignores spurious `LOSS` within 700ms of `resume()` (palette/theme bug fix)
- Foreground polling 200ms, background 1000ms (battery optimization)
- `fadeOutAndPause()` on `AUDIO_BECOMING_NOISY` (smooth headphone disconnect)

### Service & MediaSession

- Immediate playback state callback (`onPlaybackStateChange`) for notification button sync
- `MediaButtonReceiver` handles `ACTION_UP` (fixes double-trigger on Bluetooth buttons)
- Art cache: 64 entries, `Semaphore(3)`, `inSampleSize=2`, max 512px, `finally release()`

### ViewModel / UI

- `visibleSongs` debounced 220ms, `HorizontalPager` with `rememberPagerState`
- `ThematicAlbumArt` uses `Palette` API (vibrant/lightVibrant/darkVibrant gradient)
- `VinylRecordCenterpiece` uses `graphicsLayer { rotationZ }` (GPU, no recomposition)
- `basicMarquee` in MiniPlayer, FullPlayer, PlaylistDetail for scrolling titles
- `AnimatedTabRow` + `AnimatedIndicator` with scaleX transform origin center
- Destructive color from `AbstraColors.destructive` per theme (was hardcoded #E53935)
- `deleteSongFile()` removes actual file from storage on delete confirmation

## Tech Stack

| Layer      | Technology                                        |
| ---------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| UI         | Jetpack Compose (BOM 2024.09.00), Material 3, Material Icons |
| Architecture | MVVM with ViewModel + StateFlow                 |
| Data       | Room 2.7.0 (v2), MediaStore                       |
| Media      | AndroidX Media 1.7.0, MediaSession, AudioEffect   |
| Language   | Kotlin 2.2.10, Coroutines 1.10.2, KSP 2.2.10-2.0.2  |
| Build      | AGP 8.7.3, compileSdk 35, targetSdk 35, JDK 17     |
| Testing    | JUnit, Robolectric                                |

## Project Structure

```
app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/
├── data/          # Room database, models, repository
├── player/        # Audio engine and procedural audio generator
├── service/       # Foreground playback and media session
├── ui/
│   ├── components # Shared Compose components
│   ├── screens    # Library, player, equalizer, settings, stats, etc.
│   ├── theme      # Material 3 theme
│   └── viewmodel  # MusicViewModel and screen state
└── util/          # Permission helpers
```

## Requirements

- [Android Studio](https://developer.android.com/studio) (or JDK 17+ and the Android SDK)
- Android device or emulator running Android 7.0 (API 24) or newer

## Building

Open the project in Android Studio and run, or build from the command line:

```bash
./gradlew assembleDebug
```

The debug APK is written to `app/build/outputs/apk/debug/` (~18 MB, debuggable, with `AbstraAudio` logs).

```bash
./gradlew assembleRelease -x lintVitalAnalyzeRelease
```
Release at `app/build/outputs/apk/release/` (~12 MB). Provide signing via env or `my-upload-key.jks`:

| Variable         | Description                          |
| ---------------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `KEYSTORE_PATH`  | Path to the keystore file (optional) |
| `STORE_PASSWORD` | Keystore password                    |
| `KEY_PASSWORD`   | Key password                         |

```bash
./gradlew assembleRelease
```

If no keystore is configured, `release` falls back to `debug` keystore for local testing.

## Running

1. `adb uninstall dev.abstra.player` (required after DB v1→v2)
2. `adb install -r app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk`
3. Grant storage permission when prompted — `Open Settings` CTA handles denied case
4. Pull down to refresh to scan device audio

## Debugging

Debug builds log `AbstraAudio:D`:
```powershell
adb logcat -s AbstraAudio:D    # streaming
adb logcat -d *:E > crash.log  # full FATAL
```

[MIT](LICENSE)

  alias(libs.plugins.android.application) apply false

  alias(libs.plugins.kotlin.compose) apply false
  alias(libs.plugins.google.devtools.ksp) apply false
}



================================================
FILE: CHANGELOG.md
================================================
# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

## [2.0.0] - 2026-09-08

### ✨ New Features

- **Swipeable Library Tabs** — Swipe horizontally between Songs, Playlists, and Folders with a smooth animated indicator
- **Palette-Based Album Art** — Full-player background now extracts vibrant colors from album artwork (previously hash-based)
- **"Play Next" Queue** — Long-press → "Play Next" adds to an explicit queue that plays before the current playlist/album
- **Double-Tap Previous** — Tap previous twice within 1.2s to skip track; single tap restarts current song (if >3s played)
- **Smooth Fade on Disconnect** — Headphones/Bluetooth disconnect now fades out over 200ms instead of abrupt pause
- **Marquee Scrolling Text** — Long song/artist titles now scroll in mini-player, full-player, and playlist detail

### 🐛 Bug Fixes

- **Shuffle Queue Sync Fixed** — Songs added during shuffle no longer vanish when toggling shuffle off
- **Notification Play/Pause Sync** — Media notification buttons now update instantly (was 180ms delayed)
- **Media Button Handling** — Bluetooth/headset buttons now respond on press release (ACTION_UP), fixing double-trigger
- **Memory Visibility** — Fixed rare crashes from stale MediaPlayer state across threads

### ⚡ Performance & Battery

- **Foreground/Background Polling** — Progress updates throttle to 1s when app backgrounded (was 200ms always)
- **GPU-Accelerated Vinyl** — Spinning record now runs on GPU via `graphicsLayer` (zero recomposition, ~60fps)
- **Art Cache Optimized** — Semaphore-limited concurrent decodes, 512px max, 64-entry LRU cache

### 🎨 Visual Polish

- **Theme-Aware Destructive Red** — Delete dialogs and folder rules now use each theme's accent red (was hardcoded #E53935)
- **Actual File Deletion** — "Delete from library" now removes the file from device storage (was only hiding in DB)

### 🔧 Under the Hood

- Added `androidx.palette:palette-ktx` for album art color extraction
- Room migration safety: removed `fallbackToDestructiveMigration`, schema changes now throw (data-safe)
- Dual-tier queue architecture: explicit user queue + context queue (playlist/album/folder)

## [1.0.1] - 2026-09-04

### Fixed — Crashes / ANR (device RMX3782)

- **Empty dex ClassNotFound** `dev.abstra.MainActivity` — missing `kotlin-android` plugin meant `compileDebugKotlin` never ran; added plugin, `jvmToolchain 17` (Java 11→17), `AGP 9.1.1→8.7.3`, `KSP 2.2.10-2.0.2`, `core-ktx 1.18→1.13.1`, `useAndroidX`
- **IllegalStateException** `MediaPlayer.isPlaying` in `AudioEngine.kt:577` `DefaultDispatcher-worker-2` — wrapped `isPlaying`/`currentPosition` in try/catch, `stopCurrentPlayers` nulls listeners + `reset()` before `release()` to avoid `prepareAsync` race on rapid track switches, `resume` snapshots `mediaPlayer` reference
- **Can't parcel a recycled bitmap** `MediaSessionManager.kt:122` — `MusicPlaybackService` recycled bitmap still in `ArtCache`; removed recycle, added `!isRecycled` guard and fallback metadata retry
- **1 ms play → auto-pause after palette/theme change** — spurious `AUDIOFOCUS_LOSS -1` 7 ms after `requestAudioFocus`; now ignores loss within `700 ms` of `resume()`, added `lastResumeTimeMs`, `setWillPauseWhenDucked(true)` and `LOSS_TRANSIENT` duck handling
- **DB close crash** — `fallbackToDestructiveMigrationOnDowngrade(dropAllTables=false)` deprecated; bumped `v1→v2` with `FK CASCADE` on `playlist_songs`, proper `deletePlaylistWithSongs` transaction

### Changed — Performance / Correctness

- `visibleSongs` debounced `220 ms`, `MediaSession` throttled `queue hash + 180 ms position`, art cache `128→64` with `Semaphore(3)` and sampled `512/300` decode
- `ProceduralAudioGenerator` throttled `onProgressUpdate` 43 Hz → 5 Hz, `AudioEngine` tracker moved `Main→Default`
- `ConfettiBackground` `infiniteTransition` only when enabled, `FullPlayerScreen` vinyl busy loop `withFrameNanos` → `delay(16)`
- `bulkDelete/Add/Share` no longer falls back to whole library when `selectedIds` empty, `settingsMutex` `withLock`, `share` grants all 10 URIs via `ClipData`
- `topPlayedSongs` now correct hidden count, `StatsScreen` max from visible 6 months

### Changed — UX

- Permission split `READ_MEDIA_AUDIO` + `POST_NOTIFICATIONS` with separate rationale, `BackHandler` respects selection mode, `LibraryScreen` empty states with `Open Settings` CTA, `FolderDetailScreen` hoisted state, `Settings` backup honest `Soon`, `HeartScrubber` semantics, `LibrarySearch` debounce

## [1.0.0] - 2026-08-19

### Added

- Local music library with storage permission handling
- Full-screen player with generated album art
- Equalizer and Virtualizer audio effects
- Playlist creation and management
- Folder browsing
- Monthly playback statistics
- Foreground media playback service with media session
- Procedural built-in tracks (Pookie, Trap Mix)
- Material 3 theming with adaptive light/dark themes

# Specifies the JVM arguments used for the daemon process.
# The setting is particularly useful for tweaking memory settings.
org.gradle.jvmargs=-Xmx4g -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
# When configured, Gradle will run in incubating parallel mode.
# This option should only be used with decoupled projects. For more details, visit
# https://developer.android.com/r/tools/gradle-multi-project-decoupled-projects
org.gradle.parallel=true
# Kotlin code style for this project: "official" or "obsolete":
kotlin.code.style=official
# Enables namespacing of each library's R class so that its R class includes only the
# resources declared in the library itself and none from the library's dependencies,
# thereby reducing the size of the R class for that library
android.nonTransitiveRClass=true
android.useAndroidX=true
android.enableJetifier=false
org.gradle.caching=true
org.gradle.configuration-cache=true
# Set the maximum number of workers to 4 to avoid overloading the machine.
org.gradle.workers.max=4
# Set the Kotlin compiler execution strategy to in-process to avoid "Could not
# connect to Kotlin compile daemon" error.
kotlin.compiler.execution.strategy=in-process

@rem
@rem Copyright 2015 the original author or authors.
@rem
@rem Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
@rem you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
@rem You may obtain a copy of the License at
@rem
@rem      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
@rem
@rem Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
@rem distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
@rem WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
@rem See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
@rem limitations under the License.
@rem
@rem SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
@rem

@if "%DEBUG%"=="" @echo off
@rem ##########################################################################
@rem
@rem  Gradle startup script for Windows
@rem
@rem ##########################################################################

@rem Set local scope for the variables, and ensure extensions are enabled
setlocal EnableExtensions

set DIRNAME=%~dp0
if "%DIRNAME%"=="" set DIRNAME=.
@rem This is normally unused
set APP_BASE_NAME=%~n0
set APP_HOME=%DIRNAME%

@rem Resolve any "." and ".." in APP_HOME to make it shorter.
for %%i in ("%APP_HOME%") do set APP_HOME=%%~fi

@rem Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
set DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS="-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m"

@rem Find java.exe
if defined JAVA_HOME goto findJavaFromJavaHome

set JAVA_EXE=java.exe
%JAVA_EXE% -version >NUL 2>&1
if %ERRORLEVEL% equ 0 goto execute

echo. 1>&2
echo ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH. 1>&2
echo. 1>&2
echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the 1>&2
echo location of your Java installation. 1>&2

"%COMSPEC%" /c exit 1

:findJavaFromJavaHome
set JAVA_HOME=%JAVA_HOME:"=%
set JAVA_EXE=%JAVA_HOME%/bin/java.exe

if exist "%JAVA_EXE%" goto execute

echo. 1>&2
echo ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: %JAVA_HOME% 1>&2
echo. 1>&2
echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the 1>&2
echo location of your Java installation. 1>&2

"%COMSPEC%" /c exit 1

:execute
@rem Setup the command line



@rem Execute Gradle
@rem endlocal doesn't take effect until after the line is parsed and variables are expanded
@rem which allows us to clear the local environment before executing the java command
endlocal & "%JAVA_EXE%" %DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS% %JAVA_OPTS% %GRADLE_OPTS% "-Dorg.gradle.appname=%APP_BASE_NAME%" -jar "%APP_HOME%\gradle\wrapper\gradle-wrapper.jar" %* & call :exitWithErrorLevel

:exitWithErrorLevel
@rem Use "%COMSPEC%" /c exit to allow operators to work properly in scripts
"%COMSPEC%" /c exit %ERRORLEVEL%

================================================
FILE: app/build.gradle.kts
================================================
import java.io.File
import java.util.Locale

plugins {
  alias(libs.plugins.android.application)
  alias(libs.plugins.kotlin.android)
  alias(libs.plugins.kotlin.compose)
  alias(libs.plugins.google.devtools.ksp)
}

android {
  namespace = "dev.abstra"
  compileSdk = 35

  defaultConfig {
    applicationId = "dev.abstra.player"
    minSdk = 24
    targetSdk = 35
    versionCode = 2
    versionName = "2.0.0"

    testInstrumentationRunner = "androidx.test.runner.AndroidJUnitRunner"
  }

  signingConfigs {
    create("release") {
      val keystorePath = System.getenv("KEYSTORE_PATH") ?: "${rootDir}/my-upload-key.jks"
      val ksFile = file(keystorePath)
      // Only configure release signing if keystore exists and passwords are provided
      if (ksFile.exists() && !System.getenv("STORE_PASSWORD").isNullOrBlank() && !System.getenv("KEY_PASSWORD").isNullOrBlank()) {
        storeFile = ksFile
        storePassword = System.getenv("STORE_PASSWORD")
        keyAlias = "upload"
        keyPassword = System.getenv("KEY_PASSWORD")
      }
    }
  }

  buildTypes {
    release {
      isCrunchPngs = false
      isMinifyEnabled = false
      proguardFiles(getDefaultProguardFile("proguard-android-optimize.txt"), "proguard-rules.pro")
      // Use release signing only if configured, otherwise Gradle will use default debug signing
      val releaseKsExists = file(System.getenv("KEYSTORE_PATH") ?: "${rootDir}/my-upload-key.jks").exists()
      val hasPasswords = !System.getenv("STORE_PASSWORD").isNullOrBlank() && !System.getenv("KEY_PASSWORD").isNullOrBlank()
      if (releaseKsExists && hasPasswords) {
        signingConfig = signingConfigs.getByName("release")
      }
    }
    debug {  }
  }
  compileOptions {
    sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
    targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
  }
  kotlin {
    jvmToolchain(17)
  }
  buildFeatures {
    compose = true
  }
  testOptions {
    unitTests {
      isIncludeAndroidResources = true
      all {
        // Robolectric's Maven resolver URL-encodes spaces in the local repo path
        // (https://github.com/robolectric/robolectric/issues/7784), which breaks on
        // Windows user profiles containing spaces. Use the 8.3 short path as a workaround.
        it.doFirst {
          val home = System.getProperty("user.home")
          val isWindows = System.getProperty("os.name", "").lowercase(Locale.ROOT).contains("win")
          val base = if (isWindows) {
            runCatching {
              ProcessBuilder("cmd", "/c", "for %I in (\"$home\") do @echo %~sI")
                .redirectErrorStream(true)
                .start()
                .inputStream.bufferedReader().readText().trim()
            }.getOrNull()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: home
          } else {
            home
          }
          it.systemProperty("maven.repo.local", File(base, ".m2/repository").absolutePath)
        }
      }
    }
  }
  dependenciesInfo {
    includeInApk = false
    includeInBundle = true
  }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/MainActivity.kt
================================================
package dev.abstra

import android.Manifest
import android.content.ComponentName
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.ServiceConnection
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.IBinder
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler
import androidx.activity.compose.rememberLauncherForActivityResult
import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.activity.viewModels
import androidx.compose.animation.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.rememberLazyListState
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalLifecycleOwner
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.lifecycle.Lifecycle
import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleEventObserver
import dev.abstra.service.MusicPlaybackService
import dev.abstra.ui.screens.*
import dev.abstra.ui.theme.AbstraAppTheme
import dev.abstra.ui.viewmodel.ActiveScreen
import dev.abstra.ui.viewmodel.MusicViewModel
import dev.abstra.util.PermissionUtils

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    private val viewModel: MusicViewModel by viewModels()
    private var playbackService: MusicPlaybackService? = null
    private var isBound = false

    private val serviceConnection = object : ServiceConnection {
        override fun onServiceConnected(name: ComponentName?, service: IBinder?) {
            val binder = service as? MusicPlaybackService.LocalBinder
            playbackService = binder?.getService()
            playbackService?.initSession(viewModel.audioEngine)
            isBound = true
        }

        override fun onServiceDisconnected(name: ComponentName?) {
            playbackService = null
            isBound = false
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        enableEdgeToEdge()

        val serviceIntent = Intent(this, MusicPlaybackService::class.java)
        try {
            startForegroundService(serviceIntent)
            bindService(serviceIntent, serviceConnection, Context.BIND_AUTO_CREATE)
        } catch (_: Exception) {}

        setContent {
            val activeScreen by viewModel.activeScreen.collectAsState()
            val selectedPlaylist by viewModel.selectedPlaylist.collectAsState()
            val selectedFolder by viewModel.selectedFolder.collectAsState()
            val appTheme by viewModel.appTheme.collectAsState()

            // Dynamic Runtime Storage Permissions Request
            val permissionLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
                contract = ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()
            ) { _ ->
                viewModel.checkPermissionAndRefresh()
            }

            val lifecycleOwner = LocalLifecycleOwner.current
            DisposableEffect(lifecycleOwner) {
                val observer = LifecycleEventObserver { _, event ->
                    when (event) {
                        Lifecycle.Event.ON_RESUME -> {
                            viewModel.checkPermissionAndRefresh(force = false)
                            viewModel.audioEngine.setForegroundState(true)
                        }
                        Lifecycle.Event.ON_PAUSE -> {
                            viewModel.audioEngine.setForegroundState(false)
                        }
                        else -> {}
                    }
                }
                lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.addObserver(observer)
                onDispose {
                    lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.removeObserver(observer)
                }
            }

            // Notify permission request handled separately with rationale
            val notifPermission = PermissionUtils.getNotificationPermission()
            val notifLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
                ActivityResultContracts.RequestPermission()
            ) { /* no-op, notification optional */ }

            LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
                val storageNeeded = PermissionUtils.getRequiredStoragePermissions().filter {
                    ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this@MainActivity, it) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
                }
                if (storageNeeded.isNotEmpty()) {
                    permissionLauncher.launch(storageNeeded.toTypedArray())
                } else if (notifPermission != null && ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this@MainActivity, notifPermission) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
                    // Ask notification after storage granted
                    notifLauncher.launch(notifPermission)
                }
            }

            // Hoisted list states — survive AnimatedContent recomposition so song list keeps scroll position
            val songsListState = rememberLazyListState()
            val playlistsListState = rememberLazyListState()
            val foldersListState = rememberLazyListState()

            val isSelectionMode by viewModel.isSelectionMode.collectAsState()
            AbstraAppTheme(theme = appTheme) {
                // Back navigation: selection mode first, then screen stack
                BackHandler(enabled = isSelectionMode || activeScreen != ActiveScreen.LIBRARY) {
                    if (isSelectionMode) {
                        viewModel.exitSelectionMode()
                    } else when (activeScreen) {
                        ActiveScreen.FULL_PLAYER -> viewModel.closeFullPlayer()
                        ActiveScreen.EQUALIZER,
                        ActiveScreen.SETTINGS,
                        ActiveScreen.STATS,
                        ActiveScreen.PLAYLIST_DETAIL,
                        ActiveScreen.FOLDER_DETAIL -> viewModel.navigateTo(ActiveScreen.LIBRARY)
                        ActiveScreen.LIBRARY -> {}
                    }
                }

                AnimatedContent(
                    targetState = activeScreen,
                    transitionSpec = {
                        if (targetState == ActiveScreen.FULL_PLAYER) {
                            slideInVertically { height -> height } + fadeIn() togetherWith
                            slideOutVertically { height -> height } + fadeOut()
                        } else {
                            fadeIn() togetherWith fadeOut()
                        }
                    },
                    label = "screen_transition",
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                ) { screen ->
                    when (screen) {
                        ActiveScreen.LIBRARY -> {
                            LibraryScreen(
                                viewModel = viewModel,
                                songsListState = songsListState,
                                playlistsListState = playlistsListState,
                                foldersListState = foldersListState
                            )
                        }
                        ActiveScreen.FULL_PLAYER -> {
                            FullPlayerScreen(
                                viewModel = viewModel,
                                onClose = { viewModel.closeFullPlayer() }
                            )
                        }
                        ActiveScreen.EQUALIZER -> {
                            EqualizerScreen(
                                viewModel = viewModel,
                                onBack = { viewModel.navigateTo(ActiveScreen.LIBRARY) }
                            )
                        }
                        ActiveScreen.SETTINGS -> {
                            SettingsScreen(
                                viewModel = viewModel,
                                onBack = { viewModel.navigateTo(ActiveScreen.LIBRARY) }
                            )
                        }
                        ActiveScreen.STATS -> {
                            StatsScreen(
                                viewModel = viewModel,
                                onBack = { viewModel.navigateTo(ActiveScreen.LIBRARY) }
                            )
                        }
                        ActiveScreen.PLAYLIST_DETAIL -> {
                            selectedPlaylist?.let {
                                PlaylistDetailScreen(
                                    viewModel = viewModel,
                                    onBack = { viewModel.navigateTo(ActiveScreen.LIBRARY) }
                                )
                            } ?: LibraryScreen(viewModel = viewModel)
                        }
                        ActiveScreen.FOLDER_DETAIL -> {
                            selectedFolder?.let {
                                FolderDetailScreen(
                                    viewModel = viewModel,
                                    onBack = { viewModel.navigateTo(ActiveScreen.LIBRARY) }
                                )
                            } ?: LibraryScreen(viewModel = viewModel)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        try {
            if (isBound) {
                unbindService(serviceConnection)
                isBound = false
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            isBound = false
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/data/db/AppDatabase.kt
================================================
package dev.abstra.data.db

import android.content.Context
import androidx.room.Database
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase

@Database(
    entities = [
        SongEntity::class,
        PlaylistEntity::class,
        PlaylistSongCrossRef::class,
        FolderRuleEntity::class,
        PlayLogEntity::class,
        EqPresetEntity::class,
        UserSettingsEntity::class
    ],
    version = 2,
    exportSchema = false
)
abstract class AppDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
    abstract fun musicDao(): MusicDao

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var INSTANCE: AppDatabase? = null

        fun getDatabase(context: Context): AppDatabase {
            return INSTANCE ?: synchronized(this) {
                val inst = INSTANCE
                if (inst != null) return inst
                val instance = Room.databaseBuilder(
                    context.applicationContext,
                    AppDatabase::class.java,
                    "pookie_music_database"
                )
                    .build()
                INSTANCE = instance
                instance
            }
        }

        fun clearInstanceForTest() {
            INSTANCE = null
        }
    }
}

package dev.abstra.data.db

import androidx.room.Entity
import androidx.room.ForeignKey
import androidx.room.Index
import androidx.room.PrimaryKey

@Entity(tableName = "songs")
data class SongEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val id: String,
    val title: String,
    val artist: String,
    val album: String,
    val durationMs: Long,
    val path: String,
    val uriString: String,
    val tag: String,
    val folderName: String,
    val isFavorite: Boolean = false,
    val isHidden: Boolean = false,
    val playCount: Int = 0,
    val dateAdded: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val lyrics: String? = null,
    val isBuiltIn: Boolean = false
)

@Entity(tableName = "playlists")
data class PlaylistEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val name: String,
    val description: String = "",
    val createdAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
)

@Entity(
    tableName = "playlist_songs",
    foreignKeys = [
        ForeignKey(entity = PlaylistEntity::class, parentColumns = ["id"], childColumns = ["playlistId"], onDelete = ForeignKey.CASCADE),
        ForeignKey(entity = SongEntity::class, parentColumns = ["id"], childColumns = ["songId"], onDelete = ForeignKey.CASCADE)
    ],
    indices = [Index(value = ["playlistId", "songId", "position"], unique = true)]
)
data class PlaylistSongCrossRef(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val playlistId: Long,
    val songId: String,
    val position: Int,
    val addedAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
)

@Entity(tableName = "folder_rules")
data class FolderRuleEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val folderName: String,
    val isBlacklisted: Boolean = false,
    val isWhitelisted: Boolean = false
)

@Entity(tableName = "play_logs")
data class PlayLogEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val songId: String,
    val playedMs: Long,
    val timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val yearMonth: String // Format: YYYY-MM
)

@Entity(tableName = "eq_presets")
data class EqPresetEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val name: String,
    val isCustom: Boolean = true,
    val band0: Int = 0,
    val band1: Int = 0,
    val band2: Int = 0,
    val band3: Int = 0,
    val band4: Int = 0,
    val bassBoost: Int = 0,
    val virtualizer: Int = 0
)

@Entity(tableName = "user_settings")
data class UserSettingsEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val id: Int = 1,
    val activeTheme: String = "PINK_POOKIE",
    val uiDensity: String = "MEDIUM",
    val isEqEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val gaplessEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val crossfadeSeconds: Int = 2,
    val playTogether: Boolean = false,
    val playbackSpeed: Float = 1.0f,
    val isWhitelistMode: Boolean = false,
    val playerDesignStyle: String = "ROTATING_CD"
)

data class MonthlyStatTuple(
    val yearMonth: String,
    val totalPlayMs: Long,
    val totalPlays: Int
)

data class SongPlayCountTuple(
    val songId: String,
    val playCount: Long,
    val totalMs: Long
)

FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/data/db/MusicDao.kt
================================================
package dev.abstra.data.db

import androidx.room.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

@Dao
interface MusicDao {
    // Songs
    @Query("SELECT * FROM songs WHERE isHidden = 0 ORDER BY title ASC")
    fun getAllVisibleSongs(): Flow<List<SongEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM songs WHERE id = :id LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getSongById(id: String): SongEntity?

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertSongs(songs: List<SongEntity>)

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertSong(song: SongEntity)

    @Update
    suspend fun updateSong(song: SongEntity)

    @Query("UPDATE songs SET isFavorite = :isFavorite WHERE id = :id")
    suspend fun setFavorite(id: String, isFavorite: Boolean)

    @Query("UPDATE songs SET title = :title, artist = :artist WHERE id = :id")
    suspend fun renameSong(id: String, title: String, artist: String)

    @Query("UPDATE songs SET isHidden = 1 WHERE id = :id")
    suspend fun hideSong(id: String)

    @Query("DELETE FROM songs WHERE id = :id")
    suspend fun deleteSong(id: String)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM songs")
    suspend fun getAllSongsOnce(): List<SongEntity>

    @Query("DELETE FROM songs WHERE id IN (:ids)")
    suspend fun deleteSongsByIds(ids: List<String>)

    // Playlists
    @Query("SELECT * FROM playlists ORDER BY name ASC")
    fun getAllPlaylists(): Flow<List<PlaylistEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertPlaylist(playlist: PlaylistEntity): Long

    @Query("DELETE FROM playlist_songs WHERE playlistId = :playlistId")
    suspend fun clearPlaylistSongs(playlistId: Long)

    @Transaction
    suspend fun deletePlaylistWithSongs(playlistId: Long) {
        clearPlaylistSongs(playlistId)
        deletePlaylist(playlistId)
    }

    @Query("DELETE FROM playlists WHERE id = :playlistId")
    suspend fun deletePlaylist(playlistId: Long)

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
    suspend fun insertPlaylistSong(crossRef: PlaylistSongCrossRef)

    @Query("DELETE FROM playlist_songs WHERE playlistId = :playlistId AND songId = :songId")
    suspend fun removeSongFromPlaylist(playlistId: Long, songId: String)

    @Query("""
        SELECT s.* FROM songs s
        INNER JOIN playlist_songs ps ON s.id = ps.songId
        WHERE ps.playlistId = :playlistId AND s.isHidden = 0
        ORDER BY ps.position ASC
    """)
    fun getSongsForPlaylist(playlistId: Long): Flow<List<SongEntity>>

    @Query("""
        SELECT ps.position as position 
        FROM playlist_songs ps 
        WHERE ps.playlistId = :playlistId
        ORDER BY ps.position DESC
        LIMIT 1
    """)
    suspend fun getMaxPlaylistSongPosition(playlistId: Long): Int?

    @Query("SELECT playlistId, COUNT(s.id) as count FROM playlists p LEFT JOIN playlist_songs ps ON p.id = ps.playlistId LEFT JOIN songs s ON s.id = ps.songId AND s.isHidden = 0 GROUP BY playlistId")
    fun getPlaylistSongCounts(): Flow<List<PlaylistSongCountTuple>>

    // Folder Rules
    @Query("SELECT * FROM folder_rules")
    fun getAllFolderRules(): Flow<List<FolderRuleEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertFolderRule(rule: FolderRuleEntity)

    @Query("DELETE FROM folder_rules WHERE folderName = :folderName")
    suspend fun deleteFolderRule(folderName: String)

    // Play Logs & Stats
    @Insert
    suspend fun insertPlayLog(log: PlayLogEntity)

    @Query("SELECT SUM(playedMs) FROM play_logs")
    fun getTotalLifetimePlaytime(): Flow<Long?>

    @Query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM play_logs")
    fun getTotalPlayCount(): Flow<Int>

    @Query("""
        SELECT yearMonth, SUM(playedMs) as totalPlayMs, COUNT(*) as totalPlays
        FROM play_logs
        GROUP BY yearMonth
        ORDER BY yearMonth DESC
        LIMIT 12
    """)
    fun getMonthlyPlayStats(): Flow<List<MonthlyStatTuple>>

    @Query("""
        SELECT songId, COUNT(*) as playCount, SUM(playedMs) as totalMs
        FROM play_logs
        GROUP BY songId
        ORDER BY playCount DESC
        LIMIT 10
    """)
    fun getTopPlayedSongTuples(): Flow<List<SongPlayCountTuple>>

    // EQ Presets
    @Query("SELECT * FROM eq_presets ORDER BY name ASC")
    fun getAllEqPresets(): Flow<List<EqPresetEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertEqPreset(preset: EqPresetEntity): Long

    @Query("DELETE FROM eq_presets WHERE id = :id")
    suspend fun deleteEqPreset(id: Long)

    // User Settings
    @Query("SELECT * FROM user_settings WHERE id = 1 LIMIT 1")
    fun getUserSettings(): Flow<UserSettingsEntity?>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun saveUserSettings(settings: UserSettingsEntity)
}

data class PlaylistSongCountTuple(
    val playlistId: Long,
    val count: Int
)

package dev.abstra.data.model

data class Song(
    val id: String,
    val title: String,
    val artist: String,
    val album: String = "Unknown Album",
    val durationMs: Long = 180000L,
    val path: String = "",
    val uriString: String = "",
    val tag: String = "Pookie",
    val folderName: String = "Music",
    val isFavorite: Boolean = false,
    val isHidden: Boolean = false,
    val playCount: Int = 0,
    val dateAdded: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val lyrics: String? = null,
    val isBuiltIn: Boolean = false
) {
    val durationFormatted: String
        get() {
            val totalSeconds = (durationMs / 1000).coerceAtLeast(0)
            val minutes = totalSeconds / 60
            val seconds = totalSeconds % 60
            return "%02d:%02d".format(minutes, seconds)
        }
}

data class Playlist(
    val id: Long = 0,
    val name: String,
    val description: String = "",
    val songCount: Int = 0,
    val isSmartFavorites: Boolean = false,
    val createdAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
)

data class Folder(
    val name: String,
    val path: String,
    val songCount: Int,
    val isBlacklisted: Boolean = false,
    val isWhitelisted: Boolean = false
)

data class TopSong(
    val song: Song,
    val playCount: Long,
    val totalMs: Long
)

enum class SongSortOption(val displayName: String) {
    TITLE_ASC("Title (A-Z)"),
    TITLE_DESC("Title (Z-A)"),
    ARTIST_ASC("Artist (A-Z)"),
    DURATION_DESC("Duration (Longest)"),
    DURATION_ASC("Duration (Shortest)"),
    RECENTLY_ADDED("Recently Added");

    val label: String get() = displayName
}

enum class SongFilterCategory(val displayName: String) {
    ALL("All Songs"),
    FAVORITES("Favorites"),
    BUILT_IN("Pookie Mixes"),
    LOCAL("Device Audio"),
    POP("Pastel Pop"),
    LOFI("Dreamy Lo-Fi");

    val label: String get() = displayName
}

enum class PlayerDesignStyle(
    val displayName: String,
    val description: String,
    val presetNumber: String
) {
    ROTATING_CD("01. Rotating CD", "Shiny pink holographic CD with ribbon bow & cassette sticker", "01"),
    POLAROID_FRAME("02. Polaroid Frame", "Vintage photo frame with paperclip, heart pin & washi tape", "02"),
    HEART_WINDOW("03. Heart Window", "Puffy pastel layered heart window with sparkles & gem", "03"),
    VINYL_RECORD("04. Vinyl Record", "Classic black vinyl LP with turntable tonearm & plush bear", "04"),
    TV_SCREEN("05. TV Screen", "Vintage 90s pastel pink CRT TV with dials & plush bear", "05"),
    CLOUD_FRAME("06. Cloud Frame", "Dreamy cloud frame with golden moon & sleeping teddy bear", "06");
}

enum class AppTheme(val displayName: String, val description: String) {
    COTTON_CANDY("Cotton Candy", "Soft, dreamy airy pastel pink with bows & hearts"),
    PEACH_BLOSSOM("Peach Blossom", "Warm, soft, fresh coral-peach tones"),
    MILKY_PINK("Milky Pink", "Clean, minimal, cozy milky pink"),
    NIGHT_BUBBLE("Night Bubble", "Cozy chill night-cafe purples & pinks"),
    VINTAGE_TAPE("Vintage Tape", "Retro nostalgic warm browns & cream"),
    MIDNIGHT_ROOM("Midnight Room", "Intimate soft bedroomcore plum & blush");

    companion object {
        fun fromStored(value: String): AppTheme = when (value) {
            "PINK_POOKIE", "COTTON_CANDY" -> COTTON_CANDY
            else -> runCatching { valueOf(value) }.getOrDefault(COTTON_CANDY)
        }
    }
}

enum class UiDensity(val displayName: String, val rowHeightDp: Int) {
    COMPACT("Compact", 56),
    MEDIUM("Medium", 68),
    COMFORTABLE("Comfortable", 80)
}

enum class RepeatMode {
    ONE_TIME,
    REPEAT_ALL,
    REPEAT_ONE
}

enum class EqPreset(
    val displayName: String,
    val bandLevels: List<Int>,
    val bassBoost: Int,
    val virtualizer: Int
) {
    FLAT("Flat", listOf(0, 0, 0, 0, 0), 0, 0),
    POP("Pop", listOf(2, 4, 7, 3, 1), 20, 15),
    BASS_BOOST("Bass Boost", listOf(9, 6, 2, 0, 0), 65, 20),
    ROCK("Rock", listOf(5, 3, -1, 3, 6), 30, 25),
    VOCAL("Vocal", listOf(-2, 1, 6, 4, -1), 10, 10),
    ELECTRONIC("Electronic", listOf(6, 4, 0, 4, 7), 50, 40),
    KAWAII("Kawaii Sparkle", listOf(3, 5, 8, 9, 6), 35, 50),
    CUSTOM("Custom", listOf(0, 0, 0, 0, 0), 0, 0)
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/data/repository/MusicRepository.kt

package dev.abstra.data.repository

import android.content.ContentUris
import android.content.Context
import android.provider.MediaStore
import dev.abstra.data.db.*
import dev.abstra.data.model.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.File

class MusicRepository(
    private val context: Context,
    private val musicDao: MusicDao
) {
    val allSongs: Flow<List<Song>> = musicDao.getAllVisibleSongs().map { entities ->
        entities.map { it.toSong() }
    }

    val playlists: Flow<List<PlaylistEntity>> = musicDao.getAllPlaylists()
    val folderRules: Flow<List<FolderRuleEntity>> = musicDao.getAllFolderRules()
    val settings: Flow<UserSettingsEntity?> = musicDao.getUserSettings()

    val playlistSongCounts: Flow<Map<Long, Int>> = musicDao.getPlaylistSongCounts().map { list ->
        list.associate { it.playlistId to it.count }
    }

    val totalLifetimePlaytime: Flow<Long> = musicDao.getTotalLifetimePlaytime().map { it ?: 0L }
    val totalPlayCount: Flow<Int> = musicDao.getTotalPlayCount()
    val monthlyPlaytimeStats: Flow<List<MonthlyStatTuple>> = musicDao.getMonthlyPlayStats()

    val topPlayedSongs: Flow<List<TopSong>> = combine(
        musicDao.getTopPlayedSongTuples(),
        allSongs
    ) { tuples, songs ->
        val songMap = songs.associateBy { it.id }
        tuples.mapNotNull { tuple ->
            songMap[tuple.songId]?.let { song ->
                TopSong(
                    song = song,
                    playCount = tuple.playCount,
                    totalMs = tuple.totalMs
                )
            }
        }
    }

    suspend fun refreshLibrary() = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        // Caller should have checked permission; still guard via try
        val projection = arrayOf(
            MediaStore.Audio.Media._ID,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.TITLE,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.ARTIST,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.ALBUM,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.DURATION,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATA,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATE_ADDED,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.RELATIVE_PATH
        )

        val selection = "${MediaStore.Audio.Media.IS_MUSIC} != 0 AND ${MediaStore.Audio.Media.DURATION} > 5000"
        val cursor = context.contentResolver.query(
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI,
            projection,
            selection,
            null,
            "${MediaStore.Audio.Media.TITLE} ASC"
        )

        val localSongs = mutableListOf<SongEntity>()
        // Batch fetch existing entities to preserve user edits without N queries
        val existingMap = try { musicDao.getAllSongsOnce().associateBy { it.id } } catch (_: Exception) { emptyMap() }

        cursor?.use { c ->
            val idCol = c.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media._ID)
            val titleCol = c.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.TITLE)
            val artistCol = c.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.ARTIST)
            val albumCol = c.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.ALBUM)
            val durCol = c.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.DURATION)
            val dataCol = c.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATA)
            val relativePathCol = c.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.RELATIVE_PATH)
            val dateCol = c.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATE_ADDED)

            while (c.moveToNext()) {
                val id = c.getLong(idCol)
                val title = c.getString(titleCol) ?: "Unknown Track"
                val artist = c.getString(artistCol) ?: "Unknown Artist"
                val album = c.getString(albumCol) ?: "Unknown Album"
                val duration = c.getLong(durCol)
                val path = if (dataCol != -1) c.getString(dataCol) ?: "" else ""
                val relativePath = if (relativePathCol != -1) c.getString(relativePathCol) ?: "" else ""
                val dateAdded = try { c.getLong(dateCol) * 1000 } catch (_: Exception) { System.currentTimeMillis() }

                val uri = ContentUris.withAppendedId(
                    MediaStore.Audio.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI,
                    id
                ).toString()

                val folderName = when {
                    path.isNotBlank() -> File(path).parentFile?.name ?: "Device Audio"
                    relativePath.isNotBlank() -> relativePath.trimEnd('/').substringAfterLast('/', "Device Audio").ifBlank { "Device Audio" }
                    else -> "Device Audio"
                }

                val existing = existingMap["local_$id"]
                // If existing was hidden but file still exists, keep hidden (user explicitly hid it). Do not auto-unhide.
                // Preserve user-renamed title/artist only if existing values are non-blank and not the default Unknown.
                val resolvedTitle = existing?.title?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: title
                val resolvedArtist = when {
                    existing?.artist != null && existing.artist.isNotBlank() && existing.artist != "Unknown Artist" && existing.artist != "<unknown>" -> existing.artist
                    artist == "<unknown>" -> "Unknown Artist"
                    else -> existing?.artist ?: artist
                }

                localSongs.add(
                    SongEntity(
                        id = "local_$id",
                        title = resolvedTitle,
                        artist = resolvedArtist,
                        album = album,
                        durationMs = duration,
                        path = path.ifBlank { relativePath },
                        uriString = uri,
                        tag = "Local",
                        folderName = folderName,
                        isFavorite = existing?.isFavorite ?: false,
                        isHidden = existing?.isHidden ?: false,
                        playCount = existing?.playCount ?: 0,
                        dateAdded = dateAdded,
                        lyrics = existing?.lyrics,
                        isBuiltIn = false
                    )
                )
            }
        }

        if (localSongs.isNotEmpty()) {
            musicDao.insertSongs(localSongs)
            val localIds = localSongs.map { it.id }.toSet()
            val staleIds = existingMap.keys.filter { it.startsWith("local_") && it !in localIds && existingMap[it]?.isBuiltIn == false }
            if (staleIds.isNotEmpty()) {
                staleIds.chunked(500).forEach { chunk ->
                    try { musicDao.deleteSongsByIds(chunk) } catch (_: Exception) {}
                }
            }
        } else {
            // No songs found: only delete stale if cursor succeeded (not null). If cursor is null = permission denied, keep existing.
            if (cursor != null) {
                val staleLocalIds = existingMap.keys.filter { it.startsWith("local_") && existingMap[it]?.isBuiltIn == false }
                if (staleLocalIds.isNotEmpty()) {
                    staleLocalIds.chunked(500).forEach { chunk ->
                        try { musicDao.deleteSongsByIds(chunk) } catch (_: Exception) {}
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    suspend fun toggleFavorite(songId: String, currentFavorite: Boolean) {
        musicDao.setFavorite(songId, !currentFavorite)
    }

    suspend fun renameSong(songId: String, title: String, artist: String) {
        musicDao.renameSong(songId, title, artist)
    }

    suspend fun deleteSong(songId: String) {
        musicDao.hideSong(songId)
        deleteSongFile(songId)
    }

    suspend fun deleteSongFile(songId: String) {
        val song = musicDao.getSongById(songId)?.let { it.toSong() }
        song?.let {
            val file = File(it.path)
            if (file.exists()) {
                file.delete()
            }
            // Also remove from MediaStore - let Room handle the isHidden
        }
    }

    suspend fun blacklistFolder(folderName: String) {
        musicDao.insertFolderRule(FolderRuleEntity(folderName = folderName, isBlacklisted = true, isWhitelisted = false))
    }

    suspend fun unblacklistFolder(folderName: String) {
        musicDao.deleteFolderRule(folderName)
    }

    suspend fun whitelistFolder(folderName: String) {
        musicDao.insertFolderRule(FolderRuleEntity(folderName = folderName, isBlacklisted = false, isWhitelisted = true))
    }

    suspend fun unwhitelistFolder(folderName: String) {
        musicDao.deleteFolderRule(folderName)
    }

    suspend fun setWhitelistMode(enabled: Boolean) {
        val current = musicDao.getUserSettings().firstOrNull() ?: UserSettingsEntity()
        musicDao.saveUserSettings(current.copy(isWhitelistMode = enabled))
    }

    suspend fun createPlaylist(name: String, description: String = ""): Long {
        return musicDao.insertPlaylist(PlaylistEntity(name = name, description = description))
    }

    suspend fun deletePlaylist(playlistId: Long) {
        musicDao.deletePlaylistWithSongs(playlistId)
    }

    suspend fun addSongToPlaylist(playlistId: Long, songId: String) {
        val maxPosition = musicDao.getMaxPlaylistSongPosition(playlistId) ?: 0
        val newPosition = maxPosition + 1
        musicDao.insertPlaylistSong(PlaylistSongCrossRef(
            id = 0, // Will be auto-generated
            playlistId = playlistId,
            songId = songId,
            position = newPosition
        ))
    }

    suspend fun removeSongFromPlaylist(playlistId: Long, songId: String) {
        musicDao.removeSongFromPlaylist(playlistId, songId)
    }

    fun getSongsForPlaylist(playlistId: Long): Flow<List<Song>> {
        return musicDao.getSongsForPlaylist(playlistId).map { entities ->
            entities.map { it.toSong() }
        }
    }

    suspend fun logPlaytime(songId: String, playedMs: Long) {
        val cal = java.util.Calendar.getInstance()
        val yearMonth = "%04d-%02d".format(cal.get(java.util.Calendar.YEAR), cal.get(java.util.Calendar.MONTH) + 1)
        musicDao.insertPlayLog(
            PlayLogEntity(
                songId = songId,
                playedMs = playedMs,
                yearMonth = yearMonth
            )
        )
    }

    suspend fun saveCustomEq(name: String, bandLevels: List<Int>, bass: Int, virt: Int) {
        musicDao.insertEqPreset(
            EqPresetEntity(
                name = name,
                isCustom = true,
                band0 = bandLevels.getOrElse(0) { 0 },
                band1 = bandLevels.getOrElse(1) { 0 },
                band2 = bandLevels.getOrElse(2) { 0 },
                band3 = bandLevels.getOrElse(3) { 0 },
                band4 = bandLevels.getOrElse(4) { 0 },
                bassBoost = bass,
                virtualizer = virt
            )
        )
    }

    suspend fun saveSettings(
        activeTheme: AppTheme,
        density: UiDensity,
        isEqEnabled: Boolean,
        gapless: Boolean,
        crossfade: Int,
        playTogether: Boolean,
        speed: Float,
        isWhitelistMode: Boolean,
        playerStyle: PlayerDesignStyle
    ) {
        musicDao.saveUserSettings(
            UserSettingsEntity(
                activeTheme = activeTheme.name,
                uiDensity = density.name,
                isEqEnabled = isEqEnabled,
                gaplessEnabled = gapless,
                crossfadeSeconds = crossfade,
                playTogether = playTogether,
                playbackSpeed = speed,
                isWhitelistMode = isWhitelistMode,
                playerDesignStyle = playerStyle.name
            )
        )
    }
}

private fun SongEntity.toSong(): Song = Song(
    id = id,
    title = title,
    artist = artist,
    album = album,
    durationMs = durationMs,
    path = path,
    uriString = uriString,
    tag = tag,
    folderName = folderName,
    isFavorite = isFavorite,
    isHidden = isHidden,
    playCount = playCount,
    dateAdded = dateAdded,
    lyrics = lyrics,
    isBuiltIn = isBuiltIn
)

FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/player/AudioEngine.kt
