package dev.abstra.player

import android.content.Context
import android.media.AudioAttributes
import android.media.AudioFocusRequest
import android.media.AudioManager
import android.media.MediaPlayer
import android.media.PlaybackParams
import android.media.audiofx.BassBoost
import android.media.audiofx.Equalizer
import android.media.audiofx.Virtualizer
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.util.Log
import dev.abstra.data.model.EqPreset
import dev.abstra.data.model.RepeatMode
import dev.abstra.data.model.Song
import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

class AudioEngine(private val context: Context) {
    @Volatile
    private var mediaPlayer: MediaPlayer? = null
    private val proceduralSynth = ProceduralAudioGenerator()
    private val audioManager = context.getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager

    private var equalizer: Equalizer? = null
    private var bassBoost: BassBoost? = null
    private var virtualizer: Virtualizer? = null

    // State flows
    private val _currentSong = MutableStateFlow<Song?>(null)
    val currentSong = _currentSong.asStateFlow()

    private val _isPlaying = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isPlaying = _isPlaying.asStateFlow()

    private val _currentPosition = MutableStateFlow(0L)
    val currentPosition = _currentPosition.asStateFlow()

    private val _duration = MutableStateFlow(180000L)
    val duration = _duration.asStateFlow()

    private val _queue = MutableStateFlow<List<Song>>(emptyList())
    val queue = _queue.asStateFlow()

    private val _currentIndex = MutableStateFlow(0)
    val currentIndex = _currentIndex.asStateFlow()

    private val _repeatMode = MutableStateFlow(RepeatMode.REPEAT_ALL)
    val repeatMode = _repeatMode.asStateFlow()

    private val _isShuffle = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isShuffle = _isShuffle.asStateFlow()

    // Base (un-shuffled) queue order, remembered so shuffle can be restored when toggled off.
    private var originalQueue: List<Song> = emptyList()

    private val _playbackSpeed = MutableStateFlow(1.0f)
    val playbackSpeed = _playbackSpeed.asStateFlow()

    // Equalizer enabled toggle - OFF by default to preserve original quality
    private val _isEqEnabled = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isEqEnabled = _isEqEnabled.asStateFlow()

    private val _currentPreset = MutableStateFlow(EqPreset.POP)
    val currentPreset = _currentPreset.asStateFlow()

    private val _crossfadeSeconds = MutableStateFlow(0)
    val crossfadeSeconds = _crossfadeSeconds.asStateFlow()

    private val _gaplessEnabled = MutableStateFlow(true)
    val gaplessEnabled = _gaplessEnabled.asStateFlow()

    private val _playTogether = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val playTogether = _playTogether.asStateFlow()

    private val _customBandLevels = MutableStateFlow(EqPreset.POP.bandLevels)
    val bandLevels: StateFlow<List<Int>> = _customBandLevels.asStateFlow()

    private val _customBassBoostLevel = MutableStateFlow(EqPreset.POP.bassBoost)
    val bassBoostLevel: StateFlow<Int> = _customBassBoostLevel.asStateFlow()

    private val _customVirtualizerLevel = MutableStateFlow(EqPreset.POP.virtualizer)
    val virtualizerLevel: StateFlow<Int> = _customVirtualizerLevel.asStateFlow()

    val activePreset: StateFlow<EqPreset> = _currentPreset.asStateFlow()

    private var progressJob: Job? = null
    private val scope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main + SupervisorJob())

    private var audioFocusRequest: AudioFocusRequest? = null

    var onPlaybackLogged: ((Song, Long) -> Unit)? = null
    var onPlaybackError: ((String) -> Unit)? = null
    var onPlaybackStateChange: ((Boolean) -> Unit)? = null

    val currentSessionId: Int get() = mediaPlayer?.audioSessionId ?: 0

    private var playStartPositionMs: Long = 0L
    private var lastLoggedSongId: String? = null
    private var lastLoggedPos: Long = 0L
    private var lastLogTime: Long = 0L
    private var lastResumeTimeMs: Long = 0L

    private var baseQueue: List<Song> = emptyList()

    // Dual-tier queue: explicit user queue (Play Next) vs context queue (album/playlist)
    private val explicitQueue: ArrayDeque<Song> = ArrayDeque()

    // Double-tap detection for previous button (1.2 second window)
    private var lastPreviousClickTimeMs: Long = 0L

    // Battery optimization: track app lifecycle to throttle polling when backgrounded
    private var isInForeground = true
    private val foregroundDelayMs = 200L
    private val backgroundDelayMs = 1000L

    fun setForegroundState(foreground: Boolean) {
        isInForeground = foreground
    }

    init {
        startProgressTracker()
    }

    private fun logPlayback(song: Song, pos: Long) {
        if (pos <= 1000L) return
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        if (lastLoggedSongId == song.id && lastLoggedPos == pos && now - lastLogTime < 2000) return
        lastLoggedSongId = song.id
        lastLoggedPos = pos
        lastLogTime = now
        onPlaybackLogged?.invoke(song, pos)
    }

    fun setQueue(songs: List<Song>, startIndex: Int = 0, autoPlay: Boolean = true) {
        if (songs.isEmpty()) return
        val reusingCurrentQueue = songs === _queue.value
        if (!reusingCurrentQueue) {
            originalQueue = songs
            baseQueue = songs
            // Clear explicit queue when starting a new playlist context
            explicitQueue.clear()
        }
        val safeIndex = startIndex.coerceIn(0, songs.size - 1)
        val effective = if (_isShuffle.value && !reusingCurrentQueue) {
            shuffledQueue(songs, safeIndex)
        } else {
            songs
        }
        _queue.value = effective
        _currentIndex.value = safeIndex.coerceIn(0, effective.size - 1)
        val song = effective[safeIndex.coerceIn(0, effective.size - 1)]
        playSong(song, autoPlay)
    }

    /**
     * Keeps the song at [currentIndex] fixed in place and shuffles only the tracks
     * around it, so the playing track's position (and [currentIndex]) stays stable.
     */
    private fun shuffledQueue(queue: List<Song>, currentIndex: Int): List<Song> {
        if (queue.size < 2) return queue
        val safeIndex = currentIndex.coerceIn(0, queue.size - 1)
        val current = queue[safeIndex]
        val rest = queue.filterIndexed { i, _ -> i != safeIndex }.shuffled()
        return rest.toMutableList().apply { add(safeIndex.coerceIn(0, size), current) }
    }

    fun playSong(song: Song, autoPlay: Boolean = true) {
        val prevSong = _currentSong.value
        val prevPos = _currentPosition.value
        if (prevSong != null && prevSong.id != song.id) {
            logPlayback(prevSong, prevPos)
        }
        _currentSong.value = song
        _duration.value = song.durationMs.coerceAtLeast(1000L)
        _currentPosition.value = 0L
        playStartPositionMs = 0L

        requestAudioFocus()
        stopCurrentPlayers()

        if (song.isBuiltIn || song.uriString.isEmpty()) {
            // Built-in procedural track playback
            proceduralSynth.setSpeed(_playbackSpeed.value)
            if (autoPlay) {
                _isPlaying.value = true
                proceduralSynth.startPlayback(
                    trackId = song.id,
                    initialPositionMs = 0L,
                    onProgressUpdate = { pos -> _currentPosition.value = pos },
                    onCompletion = { handleTrackCompletion() }
                )
            } else {
                _isPlaying.value = false
            }
        } else {
            try {
                mediaPlayer = MediaPlayer().apply {
                    setAudioAttributes(
                        AudioAttributes.Builder()
                            .setContentType(AudioAttributes.CONTENT_TYPE_MUSIC)
                            .setUsage(AudioAttributes.USAGE_MEDIA)
                            .build()
                    )
                    setDataSource(context, Uri.parse(song.uriString))
                    setOnPreparedListener { mp ->
                        _duration.value = mp.duration.toLong().coerceAtLeast(1000L)
                        setupAudioEffects(mp.audioSessionId)
                        applyPlaybackSpeed(_playbackSpeed.value)
                        mp.setOnCompletionListener { handleTrackCompletion() }
                        if (autoPlay) {
                            mp.start()
                            _isPlaying.value = true
                        } else {
                            _isPlaying.value = false
                        }
                    }
                    setOnErrorListener { _, what, extra ->
                        _isPlaying.value = false
                        onPlaybackError?.invoke("Playback error ($what,$extra) for ${song.title}")
                        handleTrackCompletionError()
                        true
                    }
                    setOnCompletionListener { handleTrackCompletion() }
                    prepareAsync()
                }
            } catch (e: SecurityException) {
                _isPlaying.value = false
                onPlaybackError?.invoke("Permission denied for ${song.title}")
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                _isPlaying.value = false
                onPlaybackError?.invoke("Cannot play ${song.title}: ${e.message}")
            }
        }
    }

    fun togglePlayPause() {
        Log.d("AbstraAudio", "togglePlayPause called isPlaying=${_isPlaying.value} current=${_currentSong.value?.title} pos=${_currentPosition.value} stack=${Thread.currentThread().stackTrace.take(6).joinToString("->") { it.methodName }}")
        if (_isPlaying.value) {
            pause()
        } else {
            resume()
        }
    }

    fun pause() {
        val song = _currentSong.value
        val pos = _currentPosition.value
        Log.d("AbstraAudio", "pause() song=${song?.title} pos=$pos isPlaying=${_isPlaying.value} caller=${Thread.currentThread().stackTrace[4].methodName}")
        if (song != null) logPlayback(song, pos)
        _isPlaying.value = false
        onPlaybackStateChange?.invoke(false)
        try { mediaPlayer?.let { if (it.isPlaying) it.pause() } } catch (_: Exception) {}
        proceduralSynth.pause()
    }

    fun resume() {
        Log.d("AbstraAudio", "resume() called isPlaying=${_isPlaying.value} song=${_currentSong.value?.title} queueSize=${_queue.value.size} mpExists=${mediaPlayer!=null} mpPlaying=${try{mediaPlayer?.isPlaying}catch(_:Exception){null}}")
        lastResumeTimeMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
        requestAudioFocus()
        val song = _currentSong.value ?: _queue.value.firstOrNull()
        if (song == null) {
            Log.w("AbstraAudio", "resume() no song, abort")
            return
        }

        if (_currentSong.value == null) {
            _currentSong.value = song
        }

        _isPlaying.value = true
        onPlaybackStateChange?.invoke(true)
        Log.d("AbstraAudio", "resume() set _isPlaying=true, applying speed=${_playbackSpeed.value}")
        // Ensure speed applied at resume time (deferred from setPlaybackSpeed while paused)
        applyPlaybackSpeed(_playbackSpeed.value)

        if (song.isBuiltIn || song.uriString.isEmpty()) {
            proceduralSynth.setSpeed(_playbackSpeed.value)
            proceduralSynth.resume(
                trackId = song.id,
                currentPosMs = _currentPosition.value,
                onProgressUpdate = { pos -> _currentPosition.value = pos },
                onCompletion = { handleTrackCompletion() }
            )
            Log.d("AbstraAudio", "resume() procedural resumed")
        } else {
            val mpSnapshot = mediaPlayer
            if (mpSnapshot != null) {
                val isMpPlaying = try { mpSnapshot.isPlaying } catch (_: Exception) { false }
                Log.d("AbstraAudio", "resume() mp exists, isPlaying=$isMpPlaying currentPos=${_currentPosition.value} duration=${_duration.value} mpCurrentPos=${try{mpSnapshot.currentPosition}catch(_:Exception){-1}}")
                if (!isMpPlaying) {
                    try { mpSnapshot.start(); Log.d("AbstraAudio", "resume() mp.start() called success isPlaying=${try{mpSnapshot.isPlaying}catch(_:Exception){null}}") } catch (e: Exception) {
                        Log.e("AbstraAudio", "resume mp.start failed, fallback to playSong", e)
                        playSong(song, autoPlay = true)
                    }
                }
                // Verify after 200ms if still playing
                scope.launch {
                    delay(250)
                    val stillPlaying = try { mpSnapshot.isPlaying } catch (_: Exception) { false }
                    Log.d("AbstraAudio", "resume() 250ms check _isPlaying=${_isPlaying.value} mp.isPlaying=$stillPlaying currentPos=${_currentPosition.value}")
                    if (_isPlaying.value && !stillPlaying && mediaPlayer === mpSnapshot) {
                        Log.w("AbstraAudio", "resume() WARN _isPlaying true but mp not playing - fixing state")
                        _isPlaying.value = false
                    }
                }
            } else {
                Log.d("AbstraAudio", "resume() mp null, calling playSong")
                playSong(song, autoPlay = true)
            }
        }
    }

    fun seekTo(positionMs: Long) {
        val safePos = positionMs.coerceIn(0L, _duration.value)
        _currentPosition.value = safePos
        try { mediaPlayer?.seekTo(safePos.toInt()) } catch (_: Exception) {}
        val song = _currentSong.value
        if (song != null && (song.isBuiltIn || song.uriString.isEmpty()) && _isPlaying.value) {
            proceduralSynth.startPlayback(
                trackId = song.id,
                initialPositionMs = safePos,
                onProgressUpdate = { pos -> _currentPosition.value = pos },
                onCompletion = { handleTrackCompletion() }
            )
        }
    }

    fun next() {
        val q = _queue.value
        if (q.isEmpty()) return
        // Respect ONE_TIME: manual next still wraps but completion handling uses ONE_TIME logic
        val nextIndex = (_currentIndex.value + 1) % q.size
        _currentIndex.value = nextIndex
        playSong(q[nextIndex], true)
    }

    fun previous(forceSkip: Boolean = false) {
        val q = _queue.value
        if (q.isEmpty()) return

        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val isDoubleTap = (now - lastPreviousClickTimeMs) < 1200L // 1.2 second grace window
        lastPreviousClickTimeMs = now

        // If played > 3s AND this is NOT a rapid double-tap, restart current track
        if (!forceSkip && !isDoubleTap && _currentPosition.value > 3000L) {
            _currentPosition.value = 0L // Instantly reflect in UI state
            seekTo(0L)
            return
        }

        // Skip to previous track
        val prevIndex = if (_currentIndex.value - 1 < 0) q.size - 1 else _currentIndex.value - 1
        _currentIndex.value = prevIndex
        playSong(q[prevIndex], true)
    }

    fun toggleShuffle() {
        _isShuffle.value = !_isShuffle.value
        val q = _queue.value
        if (q.isEmpty()) return

        if (_isShuffle.value) {
            baseQueue = q
            originalQueue = q
            _queue.value = shuffledQueue(q, _currentIndex.value)
        } else {
            val current = q[_currentIndex.value.coerceIn(0, q.size - 1)]
            val restored = baseQueue.ifEmpty { originalQueue.ifEmpty { q } }
            _queue.value = restored
            val idx = restored.indexOfFirst { it.id == current.id }
            _currentIndex.value = if (idx != -1) idx else _currentIndex.value.coerceIn(0, restored.size - 1)
            originalQueue = restored
        }
    }

    fun toggleRepeatMode() {
        _repeatMode.value = when (_repeatMode.value) {
            RepeatMode.ONE_TIME -> RepeatMode.REPEAT_ALL
            RepeatMode.REPEAT_ALL -> RepeatMode.REPEAT_ONE
            RepeatMode.REPEAT_ONE -> RepeatMode.ONE_TIME
        }
    }

    fun setPlaybackSpeed(speed: Float) {
        Log.d("AbstraAudio", "setPlaybackSpeed $speed isPlaying=${_isPlaying.value} thread=${Thread.currentThread().name}")
        _playbackSpeed.value = speed
        proceduralSynth.setSpeed(speed)
        // Only touch MediaPlayer if currently playing — setting playbackParams while paused
        // resumes playback on some OEMs (theme/playerStyle change bug).
        if (_isPlaying.value) applyPlaybackSpeed(speed)
    }

    fun updateCurrentSong(updatedSong: Song) {
        if (_currentSong.value?.id == updatedSong.id) {
            _currentSong.value = updatedSong
        }
        // Also update in queue
        _queue.value = _queue.value.map { if (it.id == updatedSong.id) updatedSong else it }
        originalQueue = originalQueue.map { if (it.id == updatedSong.id) updatedSong else it }
    }

    fun insertNextInQueue(song: Song) {
        val currentQ = _queue.value.toMutableList()
        val existingIdx = currentQ.indexOfFirst { it.id == song.id }
        if (existingIdx != -1) {
            currentQ.removeAt(existingIdx)
            // If we removed an item before the insertion point, shift insertion left
            val rawInsert = _currentIndex.value + 1
            val adjustedInsert = if (existingIdx < rawInsert) rawInsert - 1 else rawInsert
            val insertIdx = adjustedInsert.coerceIn(0, currentQ.size)
            currentQ.add(insertIdx, song)
        } else {
            val insertIdx = (_currentIndex.value + 1).coerceIn(0, currentQ.size)
            currentQ.add(insertIdx, song)
        }
        _queue.value = currentQ
        syncOriginalQueueAfterInsert(song)
    }

    private fun syncOriginalQueueAfterInsert(song: Song) {
        if (!_isShuffle.value) {
            originalQueue = _queue.value
            return
        }
        val orig = originalQueue.toMutableList()
        val existingIdx = orig.indexOfFirst { it.id == song.id }
        if (existingIdx != -1) {
            orig.removeAt(existingIdx)
        }
        val currentSongId = _currentSong.value?.id
        val anchor = if (currentSongId != null) orig.indexOfFirst { it.id == currentSongId } else -1
        orig.add((anchor + 1).coerceIn(0, orig.size), song)
        originalQueue = orig
        baseQueue = _queue.value
    }

    fun addToQueue(song: Song) {
        val currentQ = _queue.value.toMutableList()
        if (currentQ.none { it.id == song.id }) {
            currentQ.add(song)
            _queue.value = currentQ
        }
        val orig = originalQueue.toMutableList()
        if (orig.none { it.id == song.id }) {
            orig.add(song)
        }
        originalQueue = orig
        // Also update baseQueue when adding songs to preserve queue when toggling shuffle off
        if (_isShuffle.value) {
            baseQueue = currentQ
        }
    }

    fun removeFromQueue(songId: String) {
        val currentQ = _queue.value.toMutableList()
        val idx = currentQ.indexOfFirst { it.id == songId }
        if (idx != -1) {
            currentQ.removeAt(idx)
            _queue.value = currentQ
            when {
                idx < _currentIndex.value -> _currentIndex.value = (_currentIndex.value - 1).coerceAtLeast(0)
                idx == _currentIndex.value && currentQ.isNotEmpty() -> {
                    val newIdx = idx.coerceIn(0, currentQ.size - 1)
                    _currentIndex.value = newIdx
                    playSong(currentQ[newIdx], _isPlaying.value)
                }
                currentQ.isEmpty() -> {
                    _currentIndex.value = 0
                    _currentSong.value = null
                    _isPlaying.value = false
                    _currentPosition.value = 0L
                    stopCurrentPlayers()
                }
            }
        }
        originalQueue = originalQueue.filterNot { it.id == songId }
        baseQueue = baseQueue.filterNot { it.id == songId }
        if (originalQueue.isEmpty()) _currentIndex.value = 0
    }

    // Master Equalizer toggle & settings
    fun setEqEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        _isEqEnabled.value = enabled
        try {
            equalizer?.enabled = enabled
            bassBoost?.enabled = enabled
            virtualizer?.enabled = enabled
            if (enabled) {
                applyEqPreset(_currentPreset.value)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    fun setGaplessEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        _gaplessEnabled.value = enabled
    }

    fun setCrossfadeSeconds(seconds: Int) {
        _crossfadeSeconds.value = seconds
    }

    fun setPlayTogether(enabled: Boolean) {
        _playTogether.value = enabled
    }

    /**
     * Smoothly fades out volume over 200ms then pauses.
     * Used when audio becomes noisy (headphones/Bluetooth disconnect).
     */
    fun fadeOutAndPause() {
        if (!_isPlaying.value) {
            pause()
            return
        }
        scope.launch {
            val steps = 4
            val stepDelay = 200L / steps
            for (i in steps downTo 1) {
                val volume = i.toFloat() / steps
                try { mediaPlayer?.setVolume(volume, volume) } catch (_: Exception) {}
                proceduralSynth.setVolume(volume)
                delay(stepDelay)
            }
            pause()
            // Restore volume to default after pause
            try { mediaPlayer?.setVolume(1.0f, 1.0f) } catch (_: Exception) {}
            proceduralSynth.setVolume(1.0f)
        }
    }

    fun applyEqPreset(preset: EqPreset) {
        _currentPreset.value = preset
        _customBandLevels.value = preset.bandLevels
        _customBassBoostLevel.value = preset.bassBoost
        _customVirtualizerLevel.value = preset.virtualizer
        if (!_isEqEnabled.value) return

        try {
            equalizer?.let { eq ->
                val numBands = eq.numberOfBands.toInt()
                for (i in 0 until minOf(numBands, preset.bandLevels.size)) {
                    val level = (preset.bandLevels[i] * 100).toShort()
                    eq.setBandLevel(i.toShort(), level)
                }
            }
            bassBoost?.let { bb ->
                if (bb.strengthSupported) {
                    bb.setStrength(preset.bassBoost.toShort())
                }
            }
            virtualizer?.let { virt ->
                if (virt.strengthSupported) {
                    virt.setStrength(preset.virtualizer.toShort())
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    fun setBandLevel(bandIndex: Int, levelDb: Int) {
        val current = _customBandLevels.value.toMutableList()
        if (bandIndex in current.indices) {
            current[bandIndex] = levelDb
            _customBandLevels.value = current
            if (_isEqEnabled.value) {
                try {
                    equalizer?.setBandLevel(bandIndex.toShort(), (levelDb * 100).toShort())
                } catch (_: Exception) {}
            }
        }
    }

    fun setBassBoostStrength(strength: Int) {
        _customBassBoostLevel.value = strength
        if (_isEqEnabled.value) {
            try {
                bassBoost?.setStrength(strength.toShort())
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    fun setVirtualizerStrength(strength: Int) {
        _customVirtualizerLevel.value = strength
        if (_isEqEnabled.value) {
            try {
                virtualizer?.setStrength(strength.toShort())
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    fun applyPreset(preset: EqPreset) = applyEqPreset(preset)
    fun setBassBoost(strength: Int) = setBassBoostStrength(strength)
    fun setVirtualizer(strength: Int) = setVirtualizerStrength(strength)

    private fun handleTrackCompletion() {
        Log.d("AbstraAudio", "handleTrackCompletion() mode=${_repeatMode.value} isPlaying=${_isPlaying.value} pos=${_currentPosition.value}")
        val current = _currentSong.value
        if (current != null) logPlayback(current, _duration.value)
        when (_repeatMode.value) {
            RepeatMode.REPEAT_ONE -> {
                seekTo(0L)
                _currentSong.value?.let { playSong(it, true) }
            }
            RepeatMode.REPEAT_ALL -> {
                next()
            }
            RepeatMode.ONE_TIME -> {
                val q = _queue.value
                if (_currentIndex.value < q.size - 1) {
                    next()
                } else {
                    _isPlaying.value = false
                    _currentPosition.value = 0L
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun handleTrackCompletionError() {
        Log.w("AbstraAudio", "handleTrackCompletionError() triggered")
        // On error, advance using same repeat logic but without logging full duration
        when (_repeatMode.value) {
            RepeatMode.REPEAT_ONE -> { seekTo(0L); _currentSong.value?.let { playSong(it, true) } }
            RepeatMode.REPEAT_ALL -> next()
            RepeatMode.ONE_TIME -> {
                val q = _queue.value
                if (_currentIndex.value < q.size - 1) next() else { _isPlaying.value = false; _currentPosition.value = 0L }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun startProgressTracker() {
        progressJob = scope.launch {
            while (isActive) {
                if (_isPlaying.value) {
                    mediaPlayer?.let { mp ->
                        try {
                            if (mp.isPlaying) {
                                try {
                                    _currentPosition.value = mp.currentPosition.toLong()
                                } catch (_: Exception) {}
                            }
                        } catch (_: IllegalStateException) {
                            // MediaPlayer in invalid state (released / error) - ignore
                        } catch (_: Exception) {}
                    }
                }
                // Throttle to 1000ms when backgrounded, 200ms when in foreground (battery optimization)
                delay(if (isInForeground) foregroundDelayMs else backgroundDelayMs)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun releaseAudioEffects() {
        try { equalizer?.release() } catch (_: Exception) {}
        try { bassBoost?.release() } catch (_: Exception) {}
        try { virtualizer?.release() } catch (_: Exception) {}
        equalizer = null
        bassBoost = null
        virtualizer = null
    }

    private fun setupAudioEffects(sessionId: Int) {
        releaseAudioEffects()
        try {
            equalizer = Equalizer(0, sessionId).apply { enabled = _isEqEnabled.value }
            bassBoost = BassBoost(0, sessionId).apply { enabled = _isEqEnabled.value }
            virtualizer = Virtualizer(0, sessionId).apply { enabled = _isEqEnabled.value }
            if (_isEqEnabled.value) {
                applyEqPreset(_currentPreset.value)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            // If effect creation fails, ensure partial effects are cleaned up
            releaseAudioEffects()
        }
    }

    private fun applyPlaybackSpeed(speed: Float) {
        val wasPlaying = _isPlaying.value
        Log.d("AbstraAudio", "applyPlaybackSpeed $speed wasPlaying=$wasPlaying mpExists=${mediaPlayer!=null}")
        mediaPlayer?.let { mp ->
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
                try {
                    val params = mp.playbackParams ?: PlaybackParams()
                    params.speed = speed
                    mp.playbackParams = params
                    Log.d("AbstraAudio", "applyPlaybackSpeed set params speed=$speed mp.isPlaying=${try{mp.isPlaying}catch(_:Exception){null}} _isPlaying=${_isPlaying.value}")
                    // Guard: playbackParams set can resume paused player on some devices
                    if (!wasPlaying) {
                        try { if (mp.isPlaying) { Log.w("AbstraAudio", "applyPlaybackSpeed guard pausing resumed mp"); mp.pause() } } catch (_: Exception) {}
                        _isPlaying.value = false
                    }
                } catch (e: Exception) { Log.e("AbstraAudio", "applyPlaybackSpeed failed", e) }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun requestAudioFocus() {
        if (_playTogether.value) {
            Log.d("AbstraAudio", "requestAudioFocus skipped playTogether=true")
            return
        }

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            audioFocusRequest = AudioFocusRequest.Builder(AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_GAIN)
                .setAudioAttributes(
                    AudioAttributes.Builder()
                        .setUsage(AudioAttributes.USAGE_MEDIA)
                        .setContentType(AudioAttributes.CONTENT_TYPE_MUSIC)
                        .build()
                )
                .setWillPauseWhenDucked(true)
                .setOnAudioFocusChangeListener { focusChange ->
                    Log.d("AbstraAudio", "onAudioFocusChange $focusChange isPlaying=${_isPlaying.value} msSinceResume=${System.currentTimeMillis()-lastResumeTimeMs}")
                    when (focusChange) {
                        AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_LOSS_TRANSIENT_CAN_DUCK -> {
                            proceduralSynth.setVolume(0.3f)
                            mediaPlayer?.setVolume(0.3f, 0.3f)
                        }
                        AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_GAIN -> {
                            proceduralSynth.setVolume(0.85f)
                            mediaPlayer?.setVolume(1.0f, 1.0f)
                        }
                        AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_LOSS, AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_LOSS_TRANSIENT -> {
                            // Ignore transient loss right after resume (OEM sends spurious LOSS when we request focus while paused)
                            if (System.currentTimeMillis() - lastResumeTimeMs < 700) {
                                Log.w("AbstraAudio", "audioFocus LOSS ignored (within 700ms of resume)")
                                // Don't pause, just duck
                                proceduralSynth.setVolume(0.85f)
                                mediaPlayer?.setVolume(1.0f, 1.0f)
                            } else {
                                Log.w("AbstraAudio", "audioFocus LOSS -> pause()")
                                pause()
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                .build()
            val res = audioFocusRequest?.let { audioManager.requestAudioFocus(it) }
            Log.d("AbstraAudio", "requestAudioFocus result $res")
        }
    }

    private fun stopCurrentPlayers() {
        releaseAudioEffects()
        val mp = mediaPlayer
        if (mp != null) {
            try {
                mp.setOnPreparedListener(null)
                mp.setOnCompletionListener(null)
                mp.setOnErrorListener(null)
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
            try { mp.reset() } catch (_: Exception) {}
            try { mp.release() } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
        mediaPlayer = null
        proceduralSynth.stop()
    }

    fun release() {
        val song = _currentSong.value
        val pos = _currentPosition.value
        if (song != null) logPlayback(song, pos)
        stopCurrentPlayers()
        progressJob?.cancel()
        scope.cancel()
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            try { audioFocusRequest?.let { audioManager.abandonAudioFocusRequest(it) } } catch (_: Exception) {}
        } else {
            try { @Suppress("DEPRECATION") audioManager.abandonAudioFocus(null) } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    companion object {
        val DEFAULT_PRESETS = listOf(
            EqPreset.POP,
            EqPreset.BASS_BOOST,
            EqPreset.VOCAL,
            EqPreset.ELECTRONIC,
            EqPreset.ROCK,
            EqPreset.KAWAII,
            EqPreset.FLAT
        )
    }
}

package dev.abstra.player

import android.content.Context

object PlaybackManager {
    @Volatile
    private var instance: AudioEngine? = null

    fun get(context: Context): AudioEngine {
        return instance ?: synchronized(this) {
            instance ?: AudioEngine(context.applicationContext).also { instance = it }
        }
    }
}



================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/player/ProceduralAudioGenerator.kt
================================================
package dev.abstra.player

import android.media.AudioAttributes
import android.media.AudioFormat
import android.media.AudioTrack
import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import kotlin.math.PI
import kotlin.math.sin

/**
 * Real-time procedural melody and synth audio generator for built-in Pookie & Trap Mix tracks.
 * Allows smooth, pleasant offline playback on any Android device/emulator.
 */
class ProceduralAudioGenerator {
    private var audioTrack: AudioTrack? = null
    private var generatorJob: Job? = null
    private val generatorScope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Default + SupervisorJob())
    @Volatile
    private var isPlaying = false
    private val sampleRate = 44100
    private var volume = 0.85f
    private var speed = 1.0f

    @Synchronized
    fun startPlayback(
        trackId: String,
        initialPositionMs: Long = 0L,
        onProgressUpdate: (Long) -> Unit,
        onCompletion: () -> Unit
    ) {
        stop()

        try {
            val minBuf = AudioTrack.getMinBufferSize(
                sampleRate,
                AudioFormat.CHANNEL_OUT_STEREO,
                AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT
            )
            val bufferSize = if (minBuf > 0) minBuf * 4 else 8192

            audioTrack = AudioTrack.Builder()
                .setAudioAttributes(
                    AudioAttributes.Builder()
                        .setUsage(AudioAttributes.USAGE_MEDIA)
                        .setContentType(AudioAttributes.CONTENT_TYPE_MUSIC)
                        .build()
                )
                .setAudioFormat(
                    AudioFormat.Builder()
                        .setEncoding(AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT)
                        .setSampleRate(sampleRate)
                        .setChannelMask(AudioFormat.CHANNEL_OUT_STEREO)
                        .build()
                )
                .setBufferSizeInBytes(bufferSize)
                .setTransferMode(AudioTrack.MODE_STREAM)
                .build()

            audioTrack?.setVolume(volume)
            audioTrack?.play()
            isPlaying = true
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            isPlaying = true // Proceed with simulated progress if audio device is unavailable
        }

        val melodyNotes = getMelodyForTrack(trackId)
        val chordNotes = getChordsForTrack(trackId)
        val bpm = getBpmForTrack(trackId)

        generatorJob = generatorScope.launch {
            var currentSec = (initialPositionMs / 1000.0).coerceAtLeast(0.0)
            val maxDurationSec = 225.0 // ~3:45
            val chunkSize = 1024
            val buffer = ShortArray(chunkSize * 2) // Stereo

            var sampleIdx = (currentSec * sampleRate).toLong()
            var lastProgressUpdate = 0L

            while (isActive && isPlaying && currentSec < maxDurationSec) {
                for (i in 0 until chunkSize) {
                    val time = sampleIdx.toDouble() / sampleRate
                    val beatIndex = ((time * (bpm / 60.0) * speed).toInt())

                    val melodyFreq = melodyNotes[beatIndex % melodyNotes.size]
                    val chordFreq = chordNotes[(beatIndex / 4) % chordNotes.size]

                    // Soft synthesizer: combination of warm sine fundamentals and subtle harmonics
                    val melodyEnv = (1.0 - ((time * (bpm / 60.0) * speed) % 1.0) * 0.4).coerceIn(0.2, 1.0)
                    val sMelody = sin(2 * PI * melodyFreq * time) * 0.35 * melodyEnv
                    val sChord = (sin(2 * PI * chordFreq * time) + sin(2 * PI * (chordFreq * 1.5) * time) * 0.5) * 0.2
                    val sBass = sin(2 * PI * (chordFreq / 2.0) * time) * 0.25

                    // Trap mix / Lofi kick and hi-hat rhythm
                    val beatFraction = (time * (bpm / 60.0) * speed) % 1.0
                    val isKick = (beatIndex % 2 == 0) && beatFraction < 0.15
                    val kick = if (isKick) sin(2 * PI * 65.0 * (1.0 - beatFraction * 5.0) * time) * 0.3 else 0.0

                    val sampleValue = ((sMelody + sChord + sBass + kick) * 32767 * 0.6).toInt().coerceIn(-32767, 32767)

                    // Stereo panning effect
                    val leftPan = 0.9 + 0.1 * sin(time * 0.5)
                    val rightPan = 0.9 - 0.1 * sin(time * 0.5)

                    buffer[i * 2] = (sampleValue * leftPan).toInt().toShort()
                    buffer[i * 2 + 1] = (sampleValue * rightPan).toInt().toShort()

                    sampleIdx++
                }

                val written = try {
                    audioTrack?.write(buffer, 0, buffer.size) ?: 0
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                    0
                }

                currentSec = sampleIdx.toDouble() / sampleRate
                val nowMs = (currentSec * 1000).toLong()
                if (nowMs - lastProgressUpdate >= 200) {
                    lastProgressUpdate = nowMs
                    withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                        onProgressUpdate(nowMs)
                    }
                }

                if (written <= 0) {
                    // Fallback pacing if track is not consuming audio
                    delay(22)
                }
            }

            if (currentSec >= maxDurationSec && isPlaying) {
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    onCompletion()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun pause() {
        isPlaying = false
        generatorJob?.cancel()
        generatorJob = null
        try {
            audioTrack?.pause()
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    fun resume(trackId: String, currentPosMs: Long, onProgressUpdate: (Long) -> Unit, onCompletion: () -> Unit) {
        startPlayback(trackId, currentPosMs, onProgressUpdate, onCompletion)
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun stop() {
        isPlaying = false
        generatorJob?.cancel()
        generatorJob = null
        try {
            audioTrack?.stop()
            audioTrack?.release()
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        audioTrack = null
    }

    fun release() {
        stop()
        generatorScope.cancel()
    }

    fun setSpeed(newSpeed: Float) {
        speed = newSpeed.coerceIn(0.5f, 2.0f)
    }

    fun setVolume(vol: Float) {
        volume = vol.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        try {
            audioTrack?.setVolume(volume)
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    private fun getMelodyForTrack(trackId: String): List<Double> {
        return when (trackId) {
            "demo_daydreams" -> listOf(523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 880.00, 783.99, 659.25, 587.33, 523.25)
            "demo_strawberry" -> listOf(587.33, 659.25, 739.99, 880.00, 987.77, 880.00, 739.99, 659.25)
            "demo_candy" -> listOf(659.25, 783.99, 987.77, 1046.50, 987.77, 783.99, 659.25, 523.25)
            "demo_pinkmoon" -> listOf(440.00, 523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 659.25, 523.25, 493.88, 440.00)
            "demo_kehna" -> listOf(587.33, 622.25, 698.46, 783.99, 698.46, 622.25, 587.33, 523.25)
            "demo_mehbooba" -> listOf(659.25, 698.46, 783.99, 880.00, 783.99, 698.46, 659.25, 587.33)
            "demo_aapjaisa" -> listOf(523.25, 587.33, 659.25, 783.99, 880.00, 783.99, 659.25, 523.25)
            "demo_zeehale" -> listOf(440.00, 493.88, 523.25, 659.25, 587.33, 523.25, 493.88, 440.00)
            "demo_silsila" -> listOf(493.88, 554.37, 659.25, 739.99, 659.25, 554.37, 493.88, 440.00)
            else -> listOf(523.25, 587.33, 659.25, 783.99, 880.00, 783.99, 659.25, 523.25)
        }
    }

    private fun getChordsForTrack(trackId: String): List<Double> {
        return when (trackId) {
            "demo_daydreams" -> listOf(261.63, 329.63, 392.00, 349.23)
            "demo_strawberry" -> listOf(293.66, 369.99, 440.00, 329.63)
            "demo_pinkmoon" -> listOf(220.00, 261.63, 329.63, 293.66)
            "demo_zeehale" -> listOf(220.00, 196.00, 174.61, 164.81)
            else -> listOf(261.63, 329.63, 392.00, 349.23)
        }
    }

    private fun getBpmForTrack(trackId: String): Double {
        return when (trackId) {
            "demo_daydreams" -> 84.0
            "demo_strawberry" -> 92.0
            "demo_candy" -> 96.0
            "demo_pinkmoon" -> 78.0
            "demo_kehna" -> 118.0
            "demo_mehbooba" -> 122.0
            "demo_aapjaisa" -> 120.0
            "demo_zeehale" -> 140.0
            "demo_silsila" -> 138.0
            else -> 90.0
        }
    }
}



================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/service/MediaSessionManager.kt
================================================
package dev.abstra.service

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.LinearGradient
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Shader
import android.media.MediaMetadataRetriever
import android.os.Bundle
import android.support.v4.media.MediaDescriptionCompat
import android.support.v4.media.MediaMetadataCompat
import android.support.v4.media.session.MediaSessionCompat
import android.support.v4.media.session.PlaybackStateCompat
import dev.abstra.player.AudioEngine
import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap

class MediaSessionManager(
    private val context: Context,
    private val engine: AudioEngine,
    private val onArtLoaded: (Bitmap?) -> Unit,
    private val onStopRequested: () -> Unit
) {
    private val sessionScope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main + SupervisorJob())
    private var mediaSession: MediaSessionCompat? = null
    val sessionToken get() = mediaSession?.sessionToken

    private object ArtCache {
        private const val MAX = 64
        private val cache = object : LinkedHashMap<String, Bitmap>(MAX, 0.75f, true) {
            override fun removeEldestEntry(eldest: MutableMap.MutableEntry<String, Bitmap>?) = size > MAX
        }
        @Volatile private var fallbackBitmap: Bitmap? = null
        fun getFallback(): Bitmap? = fallbackBitmap
        fun setFallback(bmp: Bitmap) { fallbackBitmap = bmp }
        fun get(key: String): Bitmap? = synchronized(this) { cache[key] }
        fun put(key: String, bmp: Bitmap) { synchronized(this) { cache[key] = bmp } }
    }

    fun start() {
        val session = MediaSessionCompat(context, "AbstraPlayer").apply {
            setCallback(object : MediaSessionCompat.Callback() {
                override fun onPlay() { engine.togglePlayPause() }
                override fun onPause() { engine.pause() }
                override fun onSkipToNext() { engine.next() }
                override fun onSkipToPrevious() { engine.previous() }
                override fun onStop() { onStopRequested() }
                override fun onSeekTo(pos: Long) { engine.seekTo(pos) }
                override fun onCustomAction(action: String?, extras: Bundle?) {
                    if (action == ACTION_STOP) onStopRequested()
                }
                override fun onMediaButtonEvent(mediaButtonIntent: android.content.Intent?): Boolean {
                    val ke = mediaButtonIntent?.getParcelableExtra<android.view.KeyEvent>(android.content.Intent.EXTRA_KEY_EVENT) ?: return false
                    if (ke.action == android.view.KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) {
                        when (ke.keyCode) {
                            android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_PLAY_PAUSE -> { engine.togglePlayPause(); return true }
                            android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_NEXT -> { engine.next(); return true }
                            android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_PREVIOUS -> { engine.previous(); return true }
                            android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_STOP -> { onStopRequested(); return true }
                        }
                    }
                    if (ke.action == android.view.KeyEvent.ACTION_UP) return true
                    return false
                }
            })
            isActive = true
        }
        mediaSession = session
        session.setMediaButtonReceiver(null)

        // Register immediate playback state change callback for notification controls
        engine.onPlaybackStateChange = { isPlaying ->
            updatePlaybackStateImmediate(session, isPlaying)
        }

        sessionScope.launch {
            launch {
                engine.currentSong.collect { song ->
                    updateMetadata(session, song)
                    updateQueue(session)
                    updatePlaybackStatePosition(session)
                    song?.let { loadArtAsync(it.uriString) } ?: onArtLoaded(null)
                }
            }
            launch {
                engine.isPlaying.collect { updatePlaybackStatePosition(session) }
            }
            launch {
                engine.currentPosition.collect { updatePlaybackStatePosition(session) }
            }
            launch {
                engine.duration.collect { updatePlaybackStatePosition(session) }
            }
            launch {
                engine.queue.collect { updateQueue(session); updatePlaybackStatePosition(session) }
            }
            launch {
                engine.currentIndex.collect { updateQueue(session); updatePlaybackStatePosition(session) }
            }
        }
    }

    fun updateSessionActiveState() {
        mediaSession?.isActive = engine.currentSong.value != null
    }

    fun stop() {
        sessionScope.cancel()
        engine.onPlaybackStateChange = null
        mediaSession?.release()
        mediaSession = null
    }

    private fun updateMetadata(session: MediaSessionCompat, song: dev.abstra.data.model.Song?) {
        val builder = MediaMetadataCompat.Builder()
        if (song != null) {
            builder.putString(MediaMetadataCompat.METADATA_KEY_TITLE, song.title)
                .putString(MediaMetadataCompat.METADATA_KEY_ARTIST, song.artist)
                .putString(MediaMetadataCompat.METADATA_KEY_ALBUM, song.album)
                .putLong(MediaMetadataCompat.METADATA_KEY_DURATION, engine.duration.value)
                .putLong(MediaMetadataCompat.METADATA_KEY_TRACK_NUMBER, (engine.currentIndex.value + 1).toLong())
                .putLong(MediaMetadataCompat.METADATA_KEY_NUM_TRACKS, engine.queue.value.size.toLong())
            ArtCache.get(song.uriString)?.let { bmp ->
                try {
                    if (!bmp.isRecycled) builder.putBitmap(MediaMetadataCompat.METADATA_KEY_ALBUM_ART, bmp)
                } catch (_: Exception) {}
            }
        } else {
            builder.putString(MediaMetadataCompat.METADATA_KEY_TITLE, "Abstra Player")
                .putString(MediaMetadataCompat.METADATA_KEY_ARTIST, "No song playing")
        }
        try {
            session.setMetadata(builder.build())
        } catch (e: IllegalStateException) {
            // Recycled bitmap race - retry without art
            try {
                val fallback = MediaMetadataCompat.Builder()
                    .putString(MediaMetadataCompat.METADATA_KEY_TITLE, song?.title ?: "Abstra Player")
                    .putString(MediaMetadataCompat.METADATA_KEY_ARTIST, song?.artist ?: "No song playing")
                    .build()
                session.setMetadata(fallback)
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    private var lastQueueHash: Int = 0
    private fun updateQueue(session: MediaSessionCompat) {
        val q = engine.queue.value
        val ci = engine.currentIndex.value
        val hash = q.hashCode() * 31 + ci
        if (hash == lastQueueHash) return
        lastQueueHash = hash
        if (q.isNotEmpty()) {
            val queueItems = q.mapIndexed { idx, song ->
                MediaSessionCompat.QueueItem(
                    MediaDescriptionCompat.Builder()
                        .setTitle(song.title)
                        .setSubtitle(song.artist)
                        .setMediaId(song.id)
                        .build(),
                    idx.toLong()
                )
            }
            session.setQueue(queueItems)
        }
    }

    private var lastPositionUpdate = 0L
    private fun updatePlaybackStatePosition(session: MediaSessionCompat) {
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        // Throttle position updates to ~5Hz to avoid binder flood
        if (now - lastPositionUpdate < 180) return
        lastPositionUpdate = now
        val stateBuilder = PlaybackStateCompat.Builder()
            .setActions(
                PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_PLAY or PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_PAUSE or
                PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_PLAY_PAUSE or PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_SKIP_TO_NEXT or
                PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_SKIP_TO_PREVIOUS or PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_SEEK_TO or
                PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_STOP
            )
        val state = if (engine.isPlaying.value) PlaybackStateCompat.STATE_PLAYING else PlaybackStateCompat.STATE_PAUSED
        stateBuilder.setState(state, engine.currentPosition.value, engine.playbackSpeed.value)
        val ci = engine.currentIndex.value
        if (engine.queue.value.isNotEmpty()) {
            stateBuilder.setActiveQueueItemId(ci.toLong())
        }
        session.setPlaybackState(stateBuilder.build())
    }

    // Immediate playback state update (no throttle) for play/pause button sync in notifications
    private fun updatePlaybackStateImmediate(session: MediaSessionCompat, isPlaying: Boolean) {
        val stateBuilder = PlaybackStateCompat.Builder()
            .setActions(
                PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_PLAY or PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_PAUSE or
                PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_PLAY_PAUSE or PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_SKIP_TO_NEXT or
                PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_SKIP_TO_PREVIOUS or PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_SEEK_TO or
                PlaybackStateCompat.ACTION_STOP
            )
        val state = if (isPlaying) PlaybackStateCompat.STATE_PLAYING else PlaybackStateCompat.STATE_PAUSED
        stateBuilder.setState(state, engine.currentPosition.value, engine.playbackSpeed.value)
        val ci = engine.currentIndex.value
        if (engine.queue.value.isNotEmpty()) {
            stateBuilder.setActiveQueueItemId(ci.toLong())
        }
        session.setPlaybackState(stateBuilder.build())
    }

    // Legacy wrapper for metadata-triggered full refresh
    private fun updatePlaybackState(session: MediaSessionCompat) {
        updateQueue(session)
        updatePlaybackStatePosition(session)
    }

    private fun loadArtAsync(uriString: String) {
        if (uriString.isBlank()) { onArtLoaded(null); return }
        val requestedUri = uriString
        ArtCache.get(uriString)?.let { onArtLoaded(it); return }
        sessionScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            var retriever: MediaMetadataRetriever? = null
            try {
                retriever = MediaMetadataRetriever()
                retriever.setDataSource(context, android.net.Uri.parse(uriString))
                val artBytes = retriever.embeddedPicture
                if (artBytes != null && artBytes.isNotEmpty()) {
                    val opts = android.graphics.BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inSampleSize = 2 }
                    val bmpRaw = android.graphics.BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(artBytes, 0, artBytes.size, opts)
                    val bmp = bmpRaw?.let {
                        if (it.width > 512 || it.height > 512) {
                            android.graphics.Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(it, 512, 512, true).also { scaled ->
                                if (scaled !== it) try { it.recycle() } catch (_: Exception) {}
                            }
                        } else it
                    }
                    if (bmp != null) {
                        ArtCache.put(uriString, bmp)
                        withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                            if (engine.currentSong.value?.uriString == requestedUri) onArtLoaded(bmp)
                        }
                        return@launch
                    }
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
            } finally {
                try { retriever?.release() } catch (_: Exception) {}
            }
            val fallback = ArtCache.getFallback() ?: createGradientBitmap().also { ArtCache.setFallback(it) }
            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                if (engine.currentSong.value?.uriString == requestedUri) onArtLoaded(fallback)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun createGradientBitmap(): Bitmap {
        val bmp = Bitmap.createBitmap(128, 128, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        val canvas = Canvas(bmp)
        val paint = Paint().apply {
            shader = LinearGradient(0f, 0f, 128f, 128f, intArrayOf(0xFFFF99C2.toInt(), 0xFFE14E86.toInt()), null, Shader.TileMode.CLAMP)
        }
        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, 128f, 128f, paint)
        return bmp
    }

    companion object {
        const val ACTION_STOP = "dev.abstra.STOP"
    }
}



================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/service/MusicPlaybackService.kt
================================================
package dev.abstra.service

import android.app.*
import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.IntentFilter
import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.media.AudioManager
import android.os.Binder
import android.os.Build
import android.os.IBinder
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import dev.abstra.MainActivity
import dev.abstra.R
import dev.abstra.player.AudioEngine
import kotlinx.coroutines.*

class MusicPlaybackService : Service() {
    private val binder = LocalBinder()
    var audioEngine: AudioEngine? = null
    private var mediaSessionManager: MediaSessionManager? = null
    private val serviceScope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main + SupervisorJob())
    private var notificationBitmap: Bitmap? = null

    inner class LocalBinder : Binder() {
        fun getService(): MusicPlaybackService = this@MusicPlaybackService
    }

    private var becomingNoisyReceiver: BroadcastReceiver? = null

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        createNotificationChannel()
        registerBecomingNoisyReceiver()
        // Promote to foreground immediately with placeholder to avoid ANR / MIUI kill
        // (audioEngine may be null until MainActivity binds)
        try { startForegroundWithPlaceholder() } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    private fun startForegroundWithPlaceholder() {
        val contentIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this, 0,
            Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java).apply { flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP },
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT
        )
        val placeholder = NotificationCompat.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setContentTitle("Abstra Player")
            .setContentText("Ready to play")
            .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_stat_music)
            .setContentIntent(contentIntent)
            .setOngoing(false)
            .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_LOW)
            .setVisibility(NotificationCompat.VISIBILITY_PUBLIC)
            .build()
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, placeholder, ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_MEDIA_PLAYBACK)
        } else {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, placeholder)
        }
    }

    private fun registerBecomingNoisyReceiver() {
        val filter = IntentFilter(AudioManager.ACTION_AUDIO_BECOMING_NOISY)
        val receiver = object : BroadcastReceiver() {
            override fun onReceive(context: Context?, intent: Intent?) {
                if (intent?.action == AudioManager.ACTION_AUDIO_BECOMING_NOISY) {
                    // Bluetooth / headset disconnected — fade out then pause for smooth experience
                    audioEngine?.fadeOutAndPause()
                    updateNotification()
                }
            }
        }
        becomingNoisyReceiver = receiver
        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
                registerReceiver(receiver, filter, Context.RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED)
            } else {
                @Suppress("UnspecifiedRegisterReceiverFlag")
                registerReceiver(receiver, filter)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            try { registerReceiver(receiver, filter) } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    private fun unregisterBecomingNoisyReceiver() {
        becomingNoisyReceiver?.let {
            try { unregisterReceiver(it) } catch (_: Exception) {}
            becomingNoisyReceiver = null
        }
    }

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder = binder

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        when (intent?.action) {
            ACTION_PLAY_PAUSE -> audioEngine?.togglePlayPause()
            ACTION_NEXT -> audioEngine?.next()
            ACTION_PREV -> audioEngine?.previous()
            ACTION_STOP -> {
                audioEngine?.pause()
                mediaSessionManager?.stop()
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.N) {
                    stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
                } else {
                    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                    stopForeground(true)
                }
                stopSelf()
                return START_NOT_STICKY
            }
        }
        updateNotification()
        return START_STICKY
    }

    fun initSession(engine: AudioEngine) {
        if (mediaSessionManager != null) return
        audioEngine = engine
        mediaSessionManager = MediaSessionManager(
            context = this,
            engine = engine,
            onArtLoaded = { bmp ->
                // Do NOT recycle - bmp is also cached in ArtCache and still used for MediaSession metadata
                // Let GC handle old bitmap; recycling would poison the cache and crash with "Can't parcel a recycled bitmap"
                notificationBitmap = bmp
                updateNotification()
            },
            onStopRequested = {
                engine.pause()
                val stopIntent = Intent(this, MusicPlaybackService::class.java).apply { action = ACTION_STOP }
                try { startService(stopIntent) } catch (_: Exception) {}
            }
        ).also { it.start() }

        serviceScope.launch {
            launch {
                engine.currentSong.collect { updateNotification() }
            }
            launch {
                engine.isPlaying.collect { updateNotification() }
            }
        }
        updateNotification()
    }

    fun updateNotification() {
        val engine = audioEngine
        if (engine == null) {
            // Keep placeholder foreground alive until engine is ready
            try { startForegroundWithPlaceholder() } catch (_: Exception) {}
            return
        }
        val song = engine.currentSong.value
        val isPlaying = engine.isPlaying.value
        val title = song?.title ?: "Abstra Player"
        val artist = song?.artist ?: "No song playing"

        val contentIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this, 0,
            Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java).apply { flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP },
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT
        )

        val prevIntent = PendingIntent.getService(
            this, 1,
            Intent(this, MusicPlaybackService::class.java).apply { action = ACTION_PREV },
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT
        )
        val playPauseIntent = PendingIntent.getService(
            this, 2,
            Intent(this, MusicPlaybackService::class.java).apply { action = ACTION_PLAY_PAUSE },
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT
        )
        val nextIntent = PendingIntent.getService(
            this, 3,
            Intent(this, MusicPlaybackService::class.java).apply { action = ACTION_NEXT },
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT
        )
        val stopIntent = PendingIntent.getService(
            this, 4,
            Intent(this, MusicPlaybackService::class.java).apply { action = ACTION_STOP },
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT
        )

        val playPauseIcon = if (isPlaying) R.drawable.ic_pause else R.drawable.ic_play_arrow
        val playPauseText = if (isPlaying) "Pause" else "Play"

        val mediaStyle = androidx.media.app.NotificationCompat.MediaStyle()
            .setMediaSession(mediaSessionManager?.sessionToken)
            .setShowActionsInCompactView(0, 1, 2)
            .setShowCancelButton(true)
            .setCancelButtonIntent(stopIntent)

        val notification = NotificationCompat.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setContentTitle(title)
            .setContentText(artist)
            .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_stat_music)
            .setContentIntent(contentIntent)
            .addAction(R.drawable.ic_skip_previous, "Prev", prevIntent)
            .addAction(playPauseIcon, playPauseText, playPauseIntent)
            .addAction(R.drawable.ic_skip_next, "Next", nextIntent)
            .addAction(R.drawable.ic_close, "Close", stopIntent)
            .setStyle(mediaStyle)
            .setOngoing(isPlaying)
            .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_LOW)
            .setVisibility(NotificationCompat.VISIBILITY_PUBLIC)
            .setLargeIcon(notificationBitmap)
            .build()

        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification, ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_MEDIA_PLAYBACK)
            } else {
                startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    private fun createNotificationChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(
                CHANNEL_ID, "Music Playback", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW
            ).apply {
                description = "Shows now playing music controls"
                setShowBadge(false)
            }
            val manager = getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager
            manager.createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        unregisterBecomingNoisyReceiver()
        mediaSessionManager?.stop()
        serviceScope.cancel()
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    companion object {
        const val CHANNEL_ID = "abstra_playback_channel"
        const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 101
        const val ACTION_PLAY_PAUSE = "dev.abstra.ACTION_PLAY_PAUSE"
        const val ACTION_NEXT = "dev.abstra.ACTION_NEXT"
        const val ACTION_PREV = "dev.abstra.ACTION_PREV"
        const val ACTION_STOP = "dev.abstra.ACTION_STOP"
    }
}

package dev.abstra.ui.components

import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.items
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag

import dev.abstra.data.db.FolderRuleEntity
