@Composable
fun RenameSongDialog(
    song: Song,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onConfirm: (newTitle: String, newArtist: String) -> Unit
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    var title by remember { mutableStateOf(song.title) }
    var artist by remember { mutableStateOf(song.artist) }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Text(
                text = "Rename Song",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                color = colors.textHeading
            )
        },
        text = {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                Text(
                    text = "Edit track details below:",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                    color = colors.textMuted
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = title,
                    onValueChange = { title = it },
                    label = { Text("Song Title") },
                    singleLine = true,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .testTag("rename_title_input"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = colors.primary,
                        unfocusedBorderColor = colors.border
                    )
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = artist,
                    onValueChange = { artist = it },
                    label = { Text("Artist Name") },
                    singleLine = true,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .testTag("rename_artist_input"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = colors.primary,
                        unfocusedBorderColor = colors.border
                    )
                )
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    if (title.isNotBlank()) {
                        onConfirm(title.trim(), artist.trim().ifBlank { "Unknown Artist" })
                    }
                },
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = colors.primary),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("rename_confirm_button")
            ) {
                Text("Save", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("Cancel", color = colors.textMuted)
            }
        },
        containerColor = colors.bgSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
    )
}

@Composable
fun DeleteSongConfirmDialog(
    song: Song,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onConfirm: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Text(
                text = "Delete Track?",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                color = colors.destructive
            )
        },
        text = {
            Text(
                text = "Are you sure you want to permanently delete '${song.title}' by ${song.artist} from device storage and library?",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                color = colors.textBody
            )
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = onConfirm,
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = colors.destructive),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("delete_confirm_button")
            ) {
                Text("Delete", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("Cancel", color = colors.textMuted)
            }
        },
        containerColor = colors.bgSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
    )
}

@Composable
fun SongInfoDialog(
    song: Song,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Text(
                text = "Track Information",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                color = colors.textHeading
            )
        },
        text = {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(vertical = 4.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
            ) {
                InfoRow("Title", song.title)
                InfoRow("Artist", song.artist)
                InfoRow("Album", song.album)
                InfoRow("Duration", song.durationFormatted)
                InfoRow("Folder", song.folderName)
                InfoRow("Genre/Tag", song.tag)
                InfoRow("Source", if (song.isBuiltIn) "Built-in Synthesized Audio" else "Device Storage")
                if (song.path.isNotBlank()) {
                    InfoRow("File Path", song.path)
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = onDismiss,
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = colors.primary),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
            ) {
                Text("Close", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        containerColor = colors.bgSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
    )
}

@Composable
private fun InfoRow(label: String, value: String) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
        Text(
            text = label,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
            color = colors.primaryStrong
        )
        Text(
            text = value,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
            color = colors.textHeading
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun FolderRulesDialog(
    folderRules: List<FolderRuleEntity>,
    isWhitelistMode: Boolean,
    onToggleWhitelistMode: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    onRemoveRule: (folderName: String) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Text(
                text = "Folder Blacklist & Whitelist",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                color = colors.textHeading
            )
        },
        text = {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                        .padding(12.dp),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            text = "Whitelist Mode",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                            color = colors.textHeading
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = if (isWhitelistMode) "Only playing songs from whitelisted folders" else "Playing all folders except blacklisted",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            color = colors.textMuted
                        )
                    }
                    Switch(
                        checked = isWhitelistMode,
                        onCheckedChange = onToggleWhitelistMode,
                        colors = SwitchDefaults.colors(
                            checkedThumbColor = Color.White,
                            checkedTrackColor = colors.primary
                        )
                    )
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "Configured Folder Rules (${folderRules.size})",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                    color = colors.textHeading
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                if (folderRules.isEmpty()) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(vertical = 16.dp),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = "No folder rules active. All non-blacklisted folders will be scanned.",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            color = colors.textMuted
                        )
                    }
                } else {
                    LazyColumn(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .heightIn(max = 240.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                    ) {
                        items(folderRules, key = { it.folderName }) { rule ->
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                    .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                                    .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                            ) {
                                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = rule.folderName,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                        color = colors.textHeading
                                    )
                                    Text(
                                        text = if (rule.isBlacklisted) "Blacklisted (Hidden from library)" else "Whitelisted",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                        color = if (rule.isBlacklisted) colors.destructive else colors.primaryStrong
                                    )
                                }
                                IconButton(
                                    onClick = { onRemoveRule(rule.folderName) },
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Filled.Delete,
                                        contentDescription = "Remove rule",
                                        tint = colors.destructive,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = onDismiss,
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = colors.primary),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
            ) {
                Text("Done", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        containerColor = colors.bgSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
    )
}

@Composable
fun CreatePlaylistDialog(
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onConfirm: (name: String) -> Unit
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    var playlistName by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Text(
                text = "New Playlist",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                color = colors.textHeading
            )
        },
        text = {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                Text(
                    text = "Enter a name for your custom playlist:",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                    color = colors.textMuted
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = playlistName,
                    onValueChange = { playlistName = it },
                    label = { Text("Playlist Name") },
                    placeholder = { Text("e.g. Kawaii Chill, Workout Mix") },
                    singleLine = true,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .testTag("create_playlist_input"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = colors.primary,
                        unfocusedBorderColor = colors.border
                    )
                )
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    if (playlistName.isNotBlank()) {
                        onConfirm(playlistName.trim())
                        onDismiss()
                    }
                },
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = colors.primary),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("create_playlist_confirm_button")
            ) {
                Text("Create", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("Cancel", color = colors.textMuted)
            }
        },
        containerColor = colors.bgSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
    )
}

@Composable
fun AddToPlaylistDialog(
    song: Song,
    playlists: List<Playlist>,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onSelectPlaylist: (Long) -> Unit,
    onCreateNewPlaylist: (String) -> Unit
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    var showCreateNew by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var newPlaylistName by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Text(
                text = "Add to Playlist",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                color = colors.textHeading
            )
        },
        text = {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                Text(
                    text = "Add '${song.title}' to:",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                    color = colors.textMuted
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                if (showCreateNew) {
                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = newPlaylistName,
                        onValueChange = { newPlaylistName = it },
                        label = { Text("New Playlist Name") },
                        singleLine = true,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                        colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                            focusedBorderColor = colors.primary,
                            unfocusedBorderColor = colors.border
                        )
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                    Button(
                        onClick = {
                            if (newPlaylistName.isNotBlank()) {
                                onCreateNewPlaylist(newPlaylistName.trim())
                                showCreateNew = false
                                newPlaylistName = ""
                            }
                        },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = colors.primary),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                    ) {
                        Text("Create Playlist", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    }
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                } else {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                            .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                            .clickable { showCreateNew = true }
                            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Filled.Add,
                            contentDescription = "Create new",
                            tint = colors.primaryStrong,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "+ Create New Playlist",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                            color = colors.primaryStrong
                        )
                    }
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                }

                if (playlists.isEmpty() && !showCreateNew) {
                    Text(
                        text = "No custom playlists yet.",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        color = colors.textMuted,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 8.dp)
                    )
                } else {
                    LazyColumn(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .heightIn(max = 200.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                    ) {
                        items(playlists, key = { it.id }) { playlist ->
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                    .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                                    .clickable {
                                        onSelectPlaylist(playlist.id)
                                        onDismiss()
                                    }
                                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Filled.QueueMusic,
                                    contentDescription = "Playlist",
                                    tint = colors.primary,
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = playlist.name,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                        color = colors.textHeading
                                    )
                                    Text(
                                        text = "${playlist.songCount} songs",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                        color = colors.textMuted
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("Cancel", color = colors.textMuted)
            }
        },
        containerColor = colors.bgSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
    )
}

/**
 * Player UI Design Presets Selector Dialog matching all 6 designs from the image
 */
@Composable
fun PlayerStyleSelectionDialog(
    currentStyle: PlayerDesignStyle,
    onSelectStyle: (PlayerDesignStyle) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                BowRibbonIcon(size = 28.dp)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "POOKIE PLAYER VARIANTS",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                        letterSpacing = 1.sp,
                        fontSize = 17.sp
                    ),
                    color = colors.textHeading
                )
            }
        },
        text = {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "Pick your favorite player UI design preset:",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                    color = colors.textMuted
                )

                PlayerDesignStyle.values().forEach { style ->
                    val isSelected = currentStyle == style
                    Surface(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                            .clickable {
                                onSelectStyle(style)
                                onDismiss()
                            }
                            .border(
                                width = if (isSelected) 2.dp else 1.dp,
                                color = if (isSelected) colors.primaryStrong else colors.border,
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                            ),
                        color = if (isSelected) colors.surfaceAlt else colors.bgSurface
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .size(36.dp)
                                    .clip(CircleShape)
                                    .background(if (isSelected) colors.primaryStrong else colors.surfaceAlt),
                                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = style.presetNumber,
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                        fontSize = 11.sp
                                    ),
                                    color = if (isSelected) Color.White else colors.textHeading
                                )
                            }
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                Text(
                                    text = style.displayName,
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                    color = colors.textHeading
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = style.description,
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(fontSize = 11.sp),
                                    color = colors.textMuted,
                                    maxLines = 1
                                )
                            }
                            if (isSelected) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Filled.CheckCircle,
                                    contentDescription = "Selected",
                                    tint = colors.primaryStrong,
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("Done", color = colors.primaryStrong, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        containerColor = colors.bgSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
    )
}

package dev.abstra.ui.components

import androidx.compose.foundation.Canvas
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.size
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.geometry.CornerRadius
import androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset
import androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Size
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Path
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.StrokeCap
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.drawscope.DrawScope
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.drawscope.Stroke
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp

/**
 * Clean 4-point sparkle vector star illustration
 */
@Composable
fun SparkleStarIcon(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    size: Dp = 18.dp,
    color: Color = Color(0xFFF06FA0)
) {
    Canvas(modifier = modifier.size(size)) {
        val w = this.size.width
        val h = this.size.height
        val cx = w / 2f
        val cy = h / 2f
        val radius = w * 0.45f

        val path = Path().apply {
            moveTo(cx, cy - radius)
            cubicTo(cx, cy, cx, cy, cx + radius, cy)
            cubicTo(cx, cy, cx, cy, cx, cy + radius)
            cubicTo(cx, cy, cx, cy, cx - radius, cy)
            cubicTo(cx, cy, cx, cy, cx, cy - radius)
            close()
        }
        drawPath(path, color)
    }
}

/**
 * Stylized glossy vector heart illustration
 */
@Composable
fun HeartIllustration(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    size: Dp = 20.dp,
    color: Color = Color(0xFFFF4D7E),
    withBorder: Boolean = false,
    borderColor: Color = Color.White
) {
    Canvas(modifier = modifier.size(size)) {
        val w = this.size.width
        val h = this.size.height
        val path = Path().apply {
            moveTo(w * 0.5f, h * 0.85f)
            cubicTo(w * 0.1f, h * 0.55f, 0f, h * 0.25f, w * 0.25f, h * 0.12f)
            cubicTo(w * 0.42f, h * 0.05f, w * 0.5f, h * 0.3f, w * 0.5f, h * 0.3f)
            cubicTo(w * 0.5f, h * 0.3f, w * 0.58f, h * 0.05f, w * 0.75f, h * 0.12f)
            cubicTo(w * 1.0f, h * 0.25f, w * 0.9f, h * 0.55f, w * 0.5f, h * 0.85f)
            close()
        }
        drawPath(path, color)
        if (withBorder) {
            drawPath(path, borderColor, style = Stroke(width = 2.5f))
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Cute ribbon bow decoration for Pookie player header and frames
 */
@Composable
fun BowRibbonIcon(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    size: Dp = 32.dp,
    color: Color = Color(0xFFFFB3D1),
    knotColor: Color = Color(0xFFE14E86)
) {
    Canvas(modifier = modifier.size(size)) {
        val w = this.size.width
        val h = this.size.height

        // Left loop
        val leftLoop = Path().apply {
            moveTo(w * 0.5f, h * 0.45f)
            cubicTo(w * 0.15f, h * 0.05f, w * 0.02f, h * 0.45f, w * 0.5f, h * 0.55f)
            close()
        }
        drawPath(leftLoop, color)
        drawPath(leftLoop, knotColor, style = Stroke(width = 2f))

        // Right loop
        val rightLoop = Path().apply {
            moveTo(w * 0.5f, h * 0.45f)
            cubicTo(w * 0.85f, h * 0.05f, w * 0.98f, h * 0.45f, w * 0.5f, h * 0.55f)
            close()
        }
        drawPath(rightLoop, color)
        drawPath(rightLoop, knotColor, style = Stroke(width = 2f))

        // Left ribbon tail
        val leftTail = Path().apply {
            moveTo(w * 0.45f, h * 0.55f)
            lineTo(w * 0.18f, h * 0.95f)
            lineTo(w * 0.35f, h * 0.85f)
            lineTo(w * 0.48f, h * 0.6f)
            close()
        }
        drawPath(leftTail, color)
        drawPath(leftTail, knotColor, style = Stroke(width = 1.5f))

        // Right ribbon tail
        val rightTail = Path().apply {
            moveTo(w * 0.55f, h * 0.55f)
            lineTo(w * 0.82f, h * 0.95f)
            lineTo(w * 0.65f, h * 0.85f)
            lineTo(w * 0.52f, h * 0.6f)
            close()
        }
        drawPath(rightTail, color)
        drawPath(rightTail, knotColor, style = Stroke(width = 1.5f))

        // Center knot circle
        drawCircle(
            color = knotColor,
            radius = w * 0.13f,
            center = Offset(w * 0.5f, h * 0.5f)
        )
    }
}

/**
 * Floating 3D glossy heart badge
 */
@Composable
fun FloatingHeartBadge(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    size: Dp = 38.dp,
    fillColor: Color = Color(0xFFFFB3D1),
    borderColor: Color = Color.White
) {
    Canvas(modifier = modifier.size(size)) {
        val w = this.size.width
        val h = this.size.height

        val heartPath = Path().apply {
            val ox = w * 0.08f
            val oy = h * 0.08f
            val sw = w * 0.84f
            val sh = h * 0.84f
            moveTo(ox + sw * 0.5f, oy + sh * 0.85f)
            cubicTo(ox + sw * 0.1f, oy + sh * 0.55f, ox, oy + sh * 0.25f, ox + sw * 0.25f, oy + sh * 0.12f)
            cubicTo(ox + sw * 0.42f, oy + sh * 0.05f, ox + sw * 0.5f, oy + sh * 0.3f, ox + sw * 0.5f, oy + sh * 0.3f)
            cubicTo(ox + sw * 0.5f, oy + sh * 0.3f, ox + sw * 0.58f, oy + sh * 0.05f, ox + sw * 0.75f, oy + sh * 0.12f)
            cubicTo(ox + sw * 1.0f, oy + sh * 0.25f, ox + sw * 0.9f, oy + sh * 0.55f, ox + sw * 0.5f, oy + sh * 0.85f)
            close()
        }

        drawPath(heartPath, fillColor)
        drawPath(heartPath, borderColor, style = Stroke(width = 3f))

        // Glossy highlight
        drawCircle(
            color = Color(0x99FFFFFF),
            radius = w * 0.12f,
            center = Offset(w * 0.38f, h * 0.35f)
        )
    }
}

/**
 * Realistic Metallic Paperclip Illustration for Polaroid Frame
 */
@Composable
fun PaperclipIllustration(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    size: Dp = 36.dp,
    color: Color = Color(0xFFC090A5)
) {
    Canvas(modifier = modifier.size(size)) {
        val w = this.size.width
        val h = this.size.height
        val stroke = w * 0.12f

        val path = Path().apply {
            moveTo(w * 0.32f, h * 0.85f)
            lineTo(w * 0.32f, h * 0.25f)
            cubicTo(w * 0.32f, h * 0.08f, w * 0.72f, h * 0.08f, w * 0.72f, h * 0.25f)
            lineTo(w * 0.72f, h * 0.75f)
            cubicTo(w * 0.72f, h * 0.92f, w * 0.48f, h * 0.92f, w * 0.48f, h * 0.75f)
            lineTo(w * 0.48f, h * 0.38f)
        }
        drawPath(path, color, style = Stroke(width = stroke, cap = StrokeCap.Round))
    }
}

/**
 * Translucent Pastel Washi Tape Strip
 */
@Composable
fun WashiTapeSticker(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    width: Dp = 54.dp,
    height: Dp = 22.dp,
    color: Color = Color(0xBBFF99C2)
) {
    Canvas(modifier = modifier.size(width, height)) {
        val w = this.size.width
        val h = this.size.height

        val path = Path().apply {
            moveTo(0f, h * 0.2f)
            lineTo(w * 0.08f, 0f)
            lineTo(w * 0.92f, 0f)
            lineTo(w, h * 0.2f)
            lineTo(w * 0.96f, h)
            lineTo(w * 0.04f, h)
            close()
        }
        drawPath(path, color)
        // subtle tape pattern stripes
        for (i in 1..4) {
            drawLine(
                color = Color(0x33FFFFFF),
                start = Offset(w * (i * 0.2f), 0f),
                end = Offset(w * (i * 0.2f + 0.1f), h),
                strokeWidth = 2f
            )
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/ui/components/MarqueeText.kt
================================================
package dev.abstra.ui.components

import androidx.compose.animation.core.*
import androidx.compose.animation.core.RepeatMode as AnimRepeatMode
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.Text
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.text.TextStyle
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalDensity

@Composable
fun MarqueeText(
    text: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    textStyle: TextStyle = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
    color: Color = Color.Unspecified,
    speed: Int = 12000
) {
    val density = LocalDensity.current
    val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "marquee")
    val baseOffset by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0f,
        targetValue = -1000f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(speed, easing = LinearEasing),
            repeatMode = AnimRepeatMode.Restart
        ),
        label = "offset"
    )

    Text(
        text = text,
        modifier = modifier.offset(x = baseOffset.dp),
        style = textStyle.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
        color = color,
        overflow = TextOverflow.Clip
    )
}


================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/ui/components/MiniPlayer.kt
================================================
package dev.abstra.ui.components

import androidx.compose.animation.AnimatedVisibility
import androidx.compose.animation.core.Spring
import androidx.compose.animation.core.animate
import androidx.compose.animation.core.spring
import androidx.compose.animation.core.tween
import androidx.compose.animation.slideInVertically
import androidx.compose.animation.slideOutVertically
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.basicMarquee
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.gestures.detectHorizontalDragGestures
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Pause
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.PlayArrow
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.SkipNext
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.shadow
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.graphicsLayer
import androidx.compose.ui.hapticfeedback.HapticFeedbackType
import androidx.compose.ui.input.pointer.pointerInput
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalHapticFeedback
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import dev.abstra.data.model.Song
import dev.abstra.ui.theme.LocalAbstraColors
import kotlin.math.abs

@Composable
fun MiniPlayer(
    currentSong: Song?,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    onPlayPause: () -> Unit,
    onNext: () -> Unit,
    onPrevious: () -> Unit,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    val haptic = LocalHapticFeedback.current
    var swipeOffsetX by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(0f) }
    var snapBack by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var hasTriggeredHaptic by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(snapBack) {
        if (snapBack) {
            animate(
                swipeOffsetX, 0f,
                animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = Spring.DampingRatioMediumBouncy, stiffness = Spring.StiffnessMediumLow)
            ) { value, _ -> swipeOffsetX = value }
            snapBack = false
            hasTriggeredHaptic = false
        }
    }

    AnimatedVisibility(
        visible = currentSong != null,
        enter = slideInVertically(initialOffsetY = { it }),
        exit = slideOutVertically(targetOffsetY = { it }),
        modifier = modifier
    ) {
        if (currentSong == null) return@AnimatedVisibility

        Surface(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                .shadow(8.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), ambientColor = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.18f))
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                .clickable { onClick() }
                .border(1.5.dp, colors.border, RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                .graphicsLayer {
                    translationX = swipeOffsetX
                    alpha = 1f - (abs(swipeOffsetX) / 400f).coerceIn(0f, 0.25f)
                }
                .pointerInput(Unit) {
                    detectHorizontalDragGestures(
                        onDragEnd = {
                            val threshold = 64f
                            when {
                                swipeOffsetX > threshold -> {
                                    haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.TextHandleMove)
                                    onPrevious()
                                }
                                swipeOffsetX < -threshold -> {
                                    haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.TextHandleMove)
                                    onNext()
                                }
                            }
                            snapBack = true
                        },
                        onDragCancel = { snapBack = true },
                        onHorizontalDrag = { change, dragAmount ->
                            change.consume()
                            // Damped resistance past threshold
                            val resistance = if (abs(swipeOffsetX) > 80f) 0.45f else 1f
                            swipeOffsetX += dragAmount * resistance
                            if (!hasTriggeredHaptic && abs(swipeOffsetX) > 64f) {
                                haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.TextHandleMove)
                                hasTriggeredHaptic = true
                            } else if (abs(swipeOffsetX) < 40f) {
                                hasTriggeredHaptic = false
                            }
                        }
                    )
                }
                .testTag("mini_player_bar"),
            color = colors.bgSurface,
            tonalElevation = 6.dp
        ) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                // Mini Thumbnail Artwork
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(46.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                ) {
                    ThematicAlbumArt(
                        trackId = currentSong.id,
                        title = currentSong.title,
                        artist = currentSong.artist,
                        uriString = currentSong.uriString,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        cornerRadius = 12.dp
                    )
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

                // Title & Artist
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = currentSong.title,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            fontSize = 14.sp
                        ),
                        color = colors.textHeading,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        modifier = Modifier.basicMarquee()
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = currentSong.artist,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontSize = 12.sp),
                        color = colors.textBody,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        modifier = Modifier.basicMarquee()
                    )
                }

                // Play / Pause Button
                IconButton(
                    onClick = onPlayPause,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(40.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(colors.primary)
                        .testTag("mini_play_pause_button")
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = if (isPlaying) Icons.Filled.Pause else Icons.Filled.PlayArrow,
                        contentDescription = if (isPlaying) "Pause" else "Play",
                        tint = Color.White,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
                    )
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))

                // Next Button
                IconButton(
                    onClick = onNext,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(36.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .testTag("mini_next_button")
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Filled.SkipNext,
                        contentDescription = "Next Track",
                        tint = colors.primaryStrong,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// 01. ROTATING CD CENTERPIECE
// ==========================================
@Composable
fun RotatingCdCenterpiece(
    song: Song?,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    rotation: Float,
    colors: AbstraColors,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .testTag("preset_rotating_cd"),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        // Rotating CD Body
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize(0.92f)
                .rotate(if (isPlaying) rotation else 0f)
                .shadow(16.dp, shape = CircleShape, spotColor = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.4f))
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(
                    Brush.radialGradient(
                        colors = listOf(
                            Color(0xFFFFD6E7),
                            Color(0xFFFFBBD7),
                            Color(0xFFFFE0ED),
                            Color(0xFFF799C0),
                            Color(0xFFFFD6E7)
                        )
                    )
                )
                .border(3.5.dp, Color(0xFFFFFFFF), CircleShape),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            // Metallic / Holographic rainbow light sheen streaks
            Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                val w = size.width
                val h = size.height
                val cx = w / 2f
                val cy = h / 2f

                // Concentric data grooves
                drawCircle(
                    color = Color(0x33FFFFFF),
                    radius = w * 0.42f,
                    center = Offset(cx, cy),
                    style = Stroke(width = 1.5f)
                )
                drawCircle(
                    color = Color(0x28E14E86),
                    radius = w * 0.38f,
                    center = Offset(cx, cy),
                    style = Stroke(width = 1f)
                )
                drawCircle(
                    color = Color(0x33FFFFFF),
                    radius = w * 0.34f,
                    center = Offset(cx, cy),
                    style = Stroke(width = 1.5f)
                )

                // Rainbow iridescent sheen triangle fans
                val sheenPath1 = Path().apply {
                    moveTo(cx, cy)
                    lineTo(cx + w * 0.48f, cy - h * 0.25f)
                    lineTo(cx + w * 0.35f, cy - h * 0.45f)
                    close()
                }
                drawPath(sheenPath1, Color(0x44FFFFFF))

                val sheenPath2 = Path().apply {
                    moveTo(cx, cy)
                    lineTo(cx - w * 0.48f, cy + h * 0.25f)
                    lineTo(cx - w * 0.35f, cy + h * 0.45f)
                    close()
                }
                drawPath(sheenPath2, Color(0x44FFFFFF))
            }

            // Outer transparent hub ring
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize(0.62f)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(Color(0x33FFFFFF))
                    .border(2.dp, Color(0x66FFFFFF), CircleShape),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                // Center Album Artwork Label
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize(0.72f)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .border(2.5.dp, colors.primary, CircleShape),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    ThematicAlbumArt(
                        trackId = song?.id ?: "demo",
                        title = song?.title ?: "daydreams",
                        artist = song?.artist ?: "lovelyy",
                        uriString = song?.uriString ?: "",
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        cornerRadius = 999.dp,
                        showDetailsOnLarge = false
                    )

                    // Spindle Hole
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(20.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(Color(0xFFFFF0F5))
                            .border(2.dp, Color(0xFFE14E86), CircleShape)
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // Cute Bow on top-left edge of the CD
        BowRibbonIcon(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.TopStart)
                .offset(x = 6.dp, y = 6.dp)
                .size(46.dp)
        )

        // Cute Cassette Sticker on bottom-right edge ("♥ mix")
        CassetteTapeSticker(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.BottomEnd)
                .offset(x = 6.dp, y = 6.dp)
        )
    }
}

// ==========================================
// 03. HEART WINDOW CENTERPIECE
// ==========================================
@Composable
fun HeartWindowCenterpiece(
    song: Song?,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    colors: AbstraColors,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "heartPulse")
    val pulseScale by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.98f,
        targetValue = if (isPlaying) 1.02f else 0.98f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(1200, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
            repeatMode = AnimRepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "pulse"
    )

    val heartShape = remember {
        GenericShape { size, _ ->
            val w = size.width
            val h = size.height
            moveTo(w * 0.5f, h * 0.88f)
            cubicTo(w * 0.05f, h * 0.55f, 0f, h * 0.22f, w * 0.25f, h * 0.08f)
            cubicTo(w * 0.42f, h * 0.02f, w * 0.5f, h * 0.28f, w * 0.5f, h * 0.28f)
            cubicTo(w * 0.5f, h * 0.28f, w * 0.58f, h * 0.02f, w * 0.75f, h * 0.08f)
            cubicTo(w * 1.0f, h * 0.22f, w * 0.95f, h * 0.55f, w * 0.5f, h * 0.88f)
            close()
        }
    }

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .scale(pulseScale)
            .testTag("preset_heart_window"),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        // Background Floating Sparkles around heart
        SparkleStarIcon(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.TopStart)
                .offset(x = 12.dp, y = 14.dp),
            size = 18.dp,
            color = Color(0xFFFF99C2)
        )
        SparkleStarIcon(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.TopEnd)
                .offset(x = (-12).dp, y = 18.dp),
            size = 16.dp,
            color = Color(0xFFFF99C2)
        )
        SparkleStarIcon(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.BottomStart)
                .offset(x = 18.dp, y = (-24).dp),
            size = 14.dp,
            color = Color(0xFFFFD1E3)
        )

        // Outer Puffy Pastel Pink Heart Bezel
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize(0.92f)
                .aspectRatio(1f)
                .shadow(16.dp, shape = heartShape, spotColor = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                .clip(heartShape)
                .background(
                    Brush.radialGradient(
                        colors = listOf(
                            Color(0xFFFFEBF2),
                            Color(0xFFFFCBE0),
                            Color(0xFFFF85B3)
                        )
                    )
                )
                .padding(14.dp),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            // Inner Layered Heart Cutout with Album Art
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .clip(heartShape)
                    .border(2.5.dp, Color.White, heartShape),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                ThematicAlbumArt(
                    trackId = song?.id ?: "demo",
                    title = song?.title ?: "daydreams",
                    artist = song?.artist ?: "lovelyy",
                    uriString = song?.uriString ?: "",
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    cornerRadius = 0.dp,
                    showDetailsOnLarge = false
                )
            }
        }

        // Cute Mini Heart Gem Badge on bottom-right
        FloatingHeartBadge(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.BottomEnd)
                .offset(x = (-6).dp, y = (-12).dp),
            size = 38.dp,
            fillColor = Color(0xFFFF4D7E),
            borderColor = Color.White
        )
    }
}

// ==========================================
// 04. VINYL RECORD CENTERPIECE
// ==========================================
@Composable
fun VinylRecordCenterpiece(
    song: Song?,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    rotation: Float,
    colors: AbstraColors,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .testTag("preset_vinyl_record"),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        // Floating Music Notes in top-left
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.TopStart)
                .offset(x = 8.dp, y = 6.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Text(
                text = "♪",
                color = colors.primaryStrong,
                fontSize = 20.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
            SparkleStarIcon(size = 12.dp, color = colors.primary)
        }

        // Spinning Black Vinyl Record LP — GPU-accelerated via graphicsLayer (battery optimized)
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize(0.90f)
                .graphicsLayer {
                    rotationZ = if (isPlaying) rotation else 0f
                }
                .shadow(16.dp, shape = CircleShape, spotColor = Color(0x88000000))
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(Color(0xFF19161E))
                .border(4.dp, Color(0xFF332B3A), CircleShape),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            // Vinyl Grooves & Sheen
            Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                val w = size.width
                val h = size.height
                val cx = w / 2f
                val cy = h / 2f
                val r = w / 2f

                // Spiral / Concentric Grooves
                val grooveRadii = listOf(0.92f, 0.86f, 0.80f, 0.74f, 0.68f, 0.62f)
                grooveRadii.forEach { factor ->
                    drawCircle(
                        color = Color(0x3344384E),
                        radius = r * factor,
                        center = Offset(cx, cy),
                        style = Stroke(width = 1.5f)
                    )
                }

                // Glossy Vinyl Light Reflections (4-point gloss)
                val reflection1 = Path().apply {
                    moveTo(cx, cy)
                    lineTo(cx + w * 0.45f, cy - h * 0.2f)
                    lineTo(cx + w * 0.35f, cy - h * 0.4f)
                    close()
                }
                drawPath(reflection1, Color(0x28FFFFFF))

                val reflection2 = Path().apply {
                    moveTo(cx, cy)
                    lineTo(cx - w * 0.45f, cy + h * 0.2f)
                    lineTo(cx - w * 0.35f, cy + h * 0.4f)
                    close()
                }
                drawPath(reflection2, Color(0x28FFFFFF))
            }

            // Center Pink Label
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize(0.52f)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(Color(0xFFFFB3D1))
                    .border(2.5.dp, colors.primaryStrong, CircleShape),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                ThematicAlbumArt(
                    trackId = song?.id ?: "demo",
                    title = song?.title ?: "daydreams",
                    artist = song?.artist ?: "lovelyy",
                    uriString = song?.uriString ?: "",
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    cornerRadius = 999.dp,
                    showDetailsOnLarge = false
                )

                // Center Spindle Hole
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(16.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(Color(0xFF19161E))
                        .border(1.5.dp, Color.White, CircleShape)
                )
            }
        }

        // Turntable Tonearm Stylus in Top-Right
        TurntableArmIllustration(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.TopEnd)
                .offset(x = 6.dp, y = (-4).dp)
                .size(110.dp)
        )

        // Cute Plush Teddy Bear in Bottom-Left
        MascotBearIllustration(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.BottomStart)
                .offset(x = 2.dp, y = 4.dp)
                .size(62.dp)
        )

        // Floating Heart Gem in Bottom-Right
        FloatingHeartBadge(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.BottomEnd)
                .offset(x = 6.dp, y = 2.dp),
            size = 32.dp,
            fillColor = Color(0xFFFF85B3)
        )
    }
}

// 05. TV SCREEN CENTERPIECE

@Composable
fun TvScreenCenterpiece(
    song: Song?,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    colors: AbstraColors,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .testTag("preset_tv_screen"),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        // Vintage 90s Pink CRT TV Cabinet
        Surface(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth(0.94f)
                .fillMaxHeight(0.92f)
                .shadow(16.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp), spotColor = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.4f))
                .border(3.dp, Color(0xFFF3C4D8), RoundedCornerShape(26.dp)),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
            color = Color(0xFFFFE0ED)
        ) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(12.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                // Curved Convex CRT TV Bubble Screen
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                        .shadow(8.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                        .background(Color(0xFF231C26))
                        .border(3.dp, Color(0xFFF7B0CD), RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    ThematicAlbumArt(
                        trackId = song?.id ?: "demo",
                        title = song?.title ?: "daydreams",
                        artist = song?.artist ?: "lovelyy",
                        uriString = song?.uriString ?: "",
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        cornerRadius = 20.dp,
                        showDetailsOnLarge = false
                    )

                    // CRT Glass Scanlines & Curved Reflection
                    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                        val w = size.width
                        val h = size.height

                        // Top-left curved glass reflection
                        val glassGlare = Path().apply {
                            moveTo(w * 0.1f, h * 0.15f)
                            cubicTo(w * 0.4f, h * 0.1f, w * 0.7f, h * 0.25f, w * 0.85f, h * 0.4f)
                            lineTo(w * 0.75f, h * 0.45f)
                            cubicTo(w * 0.5f, h * 0.2f, w * 0.3f, h * 0.22f, w * 0.1f, h * 0.3f)
                            close()
                        }
                        drawPath(glassGlare, Color(0x33FFFFFF))
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))

                // Right TV Control Panel (Dials, Buttons, Speaker Slots, LED)
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .width(36.dp)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .background(Color(0xFFFFCCE0))
                        .padding(vertical = 6.dp),
                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                ) {
                    // Top Rotary Dial 1
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(24.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(Color(0xFFF799C0))
                            .border(1.5.dp, Color.White, CircleShape),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .width(3.dp)
                                .height(12.dp)
                                .background(Color.White)
                        )
                    }

                    // Rotary Dial 2
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(24.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(Color(0xFFF799C0))
                            .border(1.5.dp, Color.White, CircleShape),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .width(3.dp)
                                .height(12.dp)
                                .background(Color.White)
                        )
                    }

                    // Speaker Grille Slots
                    Column(
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp),
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                    ) {
                        repeat(4) {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .width(20.dp)
                                    .height(2.5.dp)
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(1.dp))
                                    .background(Color(0xFFE14E86).copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    // Power LED indicator
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(8.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(if (isPlaying) Color(0xFF4ADE80) else Color(0xFFFF4D7E))
                            .border(1.dp, Color.White, CircleShape)
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // Ribbon Bow on Top-Left of TV
        BowRibbonIcon(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.TopStart)
                .offset(x = 4.dp, y = 2.dp)
                .size(42.dp)
        )

        // Plush Teddy Bear in Bottom-Left
        MascotBearIllustration(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.BottomStart)
                .offset(x = 0.dp, y = 8.dp)
                .size(56.dp)
        )
    }
}

// ==========================================
// 06. CLOUD FRAME CENTERPIECE
// ==========================================
@Composable
fun CloudFrameCenterpiece(
    song: Song?,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    colors: AbstraColors,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val cloudShape = remember {
        GenericShape { size, _ ->
            val w = size.width
            val h = size.height

            moveTo(w * 0.25f, h * 0.78f)
            // Bottom edge
            cubicTo(w * 0.4f, h * 0.82f, w * 0.6f, h * 0.82f, w * 0.75f, h * 0.78f)
            // Right-bottom bump
            cubicTo(w * 0.92f, h * 0.75f, w * 0.98f, h * 0.60f, w * 0.92f, h * 0.45f)
            // Right-top bump
            cubicTo(w * 0.95f, h * 0.28f, w * 0.82f, h * 0.15f, w * 0.68f, h * 0.18f)
            // Top-middle bump
            cubicTo(w * 0.58f, h * 0.08f, w * 0.42f, h * 0.08f, w * 0.32f, h * 0.18f)
            // Left-top bump
            cubicTo(w * 0.18f, h * 0.15f, w * 0.05f, h * 0.28f, w * 0.08f, h * 0.45f)
            // Left-bottom bump
            cubicTo(w * 0.02f, h * 0.60f, w * 0.08f, h * 0.75f, w * 0.25f, h * 0.78f)
            close()
        }
    }

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .testTag("preset_cloud_frame"),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        // Dangling / Hanging Stars & Hearts beneath the cloud
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                .offset(y = 2.dp)
                .fillMaxWidth(0.7f),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top
        ) {
            DanglingItem(length = 24.dp, isHeart = false)
            DanglingItem(length = 32.dp, isHeart = true)
            DanglingItem(length = 20.dp, isHeart = false)
            DanglingItem(length = 28.dp, isHeart = true)
            DanglingItem(length = 18.dp, isHeart = false)
        }

        // Fluffy Puffy Cloud Outer Bezel
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize(0.92f)
                .shadow(16.dp, shape = cloudShape, spotColor = Color(0x44FFB3D1))
                .clip(cloudShape)
                .background(
                    Brush.verticalGradient(
                        colors = listOf(
                            Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                            Color(0xFFFFEFF6),
                            Color(0xFFFFD6E7)
                        )
                    )
                )
                .padding(14.dp),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {

                    uriString = song?.uriString ?: "",
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    cornerRadius = 0.dp,
                    showDetailsOnLarge = false
                )
            }
        }

        // Golden Crescent Moon on Top-Left Cloud Curve
        CrescentMoonIllustration(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.TopStart)
                .offset(x = 10.dp, y = 6.dp)
                .size(34.dp)
        )

        // Sleepy Teddy Bear resting on Top-Right Cloud Puff

                .align(Alignment.TopEnd)
                .offset(x = 6.dp, y = 2.dp)
                .size(52.dp)
        )

@Composable
private fun DanglingItem(
    length: Dp,
    isHeart: Boolean,
    color: Color = Color(0xFFFF99C2)
) {
    Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .width(1.5.dp)
                .height(length)
                .background(color.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
        )
        if (isHeart) {
            HeartIllustration(size = 10.dp, color = color)
        } else {
            SparkleStarIcon(size = 10.dp, color = Color(0xFFFFE066))
        }
    }
}

package dev.abstra.ui.components

@Composable
fun CassetteTapeSticker(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier

        val w = size.width

        // Cassette main body

            center = Offset(20f, 15f)
        )

        // Spool window
        drawRoundRect(
            color = Color(0xFF4A1528),
            topLeft = Offset(16f, h * 0.56f),
            size = Size(w - 32f, h * 0.32f),
            cornerRadius = CornerRadius(5f, 5f)
        )

        // Left and Right Spools
        drawCircle(
            color = Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
            radius = 6f,
            center = Offset(26f, h * 0.72f)
        )
        drawCircle(
            color = Color(0xFF4A1528),
            radius = 3f,

        )

        drawCircle(
            color = Color(0xFFFFFFFF),

        )
        drawCircle(
            color = Color(0xFF4A1528),
            radius = 3f,
            center = Offset(w - 26f, h * 0.72f)
        )
    }
}

/**
 * Interactive scrubber bar with Heart slider thumb
 */
@Composable
fun HeartScrubber(
    currentPositionMs: Long,
    durationMs: Long,
    onSeek: (Long) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    val density = LocalDensity.current
    var isDragging by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var dragFraction by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(0f) }

    val safeDuration = durationMs.coerceAtLeast(1L)
    val playbackFraction = (currentPositionMs.toFloat() / safeDuration).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    val displayFraction = if (isDragging) dragFraction else playbackFraction

    val elapsedText = formatMs(if (isDragging) (dragFraction * safeDuration).toLong() else currentPositionMs)
    val durationText = formatMs(safeDuration)

    Column(modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("heart_scrubber_container").semantics { contentDescription = "Seek bar, ${formatMs(currentPositionMs)} of ${formatMs(safeDuration)}" }) {
        BoxWithConstraints(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(36.dp)
                .pointerInput(Unit) {
                    detectTapGestures { offset ->
                        val newFraction = (offset.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                        onSeek((newFraction * safeDuration).toLong())
                    }
                }
                .pointerInput(Unit) {
                    detectHorizontalDragGestures(
                        onDragStart = { offset ->
                            isDragging = true
                            dragFraction = (offset.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                        },
                        onDragEnd = {
                            isDragging = false
                            onSeek((dragFraction * safeDuration).toLong())
                        },
                        onDragCancel = { isDragging = false },
                        onHorizontalDrag = { change, dragAmount ->
                            change.consume()
                            val newX = (dragFraction * size.width) + dragAmount
                            dragFraction = (newX / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                        }
                    )
                },
            contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
        ) {
            val totalWidthPx = constraints.maxWidth.toFloat()
            val thumbXOffset = (displayFraction * totalWidthPx).coerceIn(0f, totalWidthPx)
            val thumbXDp = with(density) { thumbXOffset.toDp() }

            // Background Track
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(6.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                    .background(colors.border)
            )

            // Progress Track
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .width(thumbXDp)
                    .height(6.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                    .background(colors.primary)
            )

            // Heart Drag Thumb
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .offset(x = (thumbXDp - 11.dp).coerceAtLeast(0.dp))
                    .size(22.dp)
                    .testTag("scrubber_heart_thumb"),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                    val w = size.width
                    val h = size.height
                    val heartPath = createHeartPath(w, h, 0.9f)
                    drawPath(heartPath, colors.accentHeart)
                    drawPath(heartPath, Color.White, style = Stroke(width = 2.5f))
                }
            }
        }

        // Time labels
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 4.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
        ) {
            Text(
                text = elapsedText,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                color = colors.textMuted
            )
            Text(
                text = durationText,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                color = colors.textMuted
            )
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Animated background confetti with sparkles & hearts — throttled when not visible
 */
@Composable
fun ConfettiBackground(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    isEnabled: Boolean = true,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    Box(modifier = modifier.background(colors.bgBase)) {
        if (isEnabled) {
            val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "confetti")
            val animOffset by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
                initialValue = 0f,
                targetValue = 360f,
                animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
                    animation = tween(12000, easing = LinearEasing),
                    repeatMode = AnimRepeatMode.Restart
                ),
                label = "drift"
            )
            Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                val w = size.width
                val h = size.height

                val particles = listOf(
                    Triple(0.10f, 0.06f, 0.02f),
                    Triple(0.90f, 0.10f, 0.015f),
                    Triple(0.06f, 0.42f, 0.018f),
                    Triple(0.94f, 0.62f, 0.022f),
                    Triple(0.22f, 0.88f, 0.016f),
                    Triple(0.80f, 0.92f, 0.02f),
                    Triple(0.50f, 0.04f, 0.012f)
                )

                particles.forEach { (xNorm, yNorm, scale) ->
                    val floatY = h * yNorm + sin((animOffset + xNorm * 100) * (PI / 180.0)).toFloat() * 12f
                    val floatX = w * xNorm
                    drawSparkle(floatX, floatY, w * scale, colors.border.copy(alpha = 0.45f))
                }
            }
        }
        content()
    }
}

private fun DrawScope.drawSparkle(cx: Float, cy: Float, radius: Float, color: Color) {
    val path = Path().apply {
        moveTo(cx, cy - radius)
        cubicTo(cx, cy, cx, cy, cx + radius, cy)
        cubicTo(cx, cy, cx, cy, cx, cy + radius)
        cubicTo(cx, cy, cx, cy, cx - radius, cy)
        cubicTo(cx, cy, cx, cy, cx, cy - radius)
        close()
    }
    drawPath(path, color)
}

private fun createHeartPath(width: Float, height: Float, scale: Float = 1.0f): Path {
    val w = width * scale
    val h = height * scale
    val ox = (width - w) / 2f
    val oy = (height - h) / 2f

    return Path().apply {
        moveTo(ox + w * 0.5f, oy + h * 0.85f)
        cubicTo(ox + w * 0.1f, oy + h * 0.55f, ox, oy + h * 0.25f, ox + w * 0.25f, oy + h * 0.12f)
        cubicTo(ox + w * 0.42f, oy + h * 0.05f, ox + w * 0.5f, oy + h * 0.3f, ox + w * 0.5f, oy + h * 0.3f)
        cubicTo(ox + w * 0.5f, oy + h * 0.3f, ox + w * 0.58f, oy + h * 0.05f, ox + w * 0.75f, oy + h * 0.12f)
        cubicTo(ox + w * 1.0f, oy + h * 0.25f, ox + w * 0.9f, oy + h * 0.55f, ox + w * 0.5f, oy + h * 0.85f)
        close()
    }
}

private fun formatMs(ms: Long): String {
    val totalSeconds = (ms / 1000).coerceAtLeast(0)
    val mins = totalSeconds / 60
    val secs = totalSeconds % 60
    return "%02d:%02d".format(mins, secs)
}
