package dev.abstra.ui.components

import androidx.compose.animation.core.animateFloatAsState
import androidx.compose.animation.core.spring
import androidx.compose.foundation.ExperimentalFoundationApi
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.combinedClickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Favorite
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.MoreVert
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.PlayArrow
import androidx.compose.material.icons.outlined.FavoriteBorder
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.scale
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import dev.abstra.data.model.Song
import dev.abstra.data.model.UiDensity
import dev.abstra.ui.theme.LocalAbstraColors

@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
fun SongListItem(
    song: Song,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    isCurrentSong: Boolean,
    density: UiDensity,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    onFavoriteToggle: () -> Unit,
    onOptionsClick: () -> Unit,
    onLongClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    var heartPressed by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val heartScale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (heartPressed) 1.35f else 1.0f,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.4f),
        label = "heartScale",
        finishedListener = { heartPressed = false }
    )

    val rowHeight = when (density) {
        UiDensity.COMPACT -> 56.dp
        UiDensity.MEDIUM -> 68.dp
        UiDensity.COMFORTABLE -> 80.dp
    }

    val thumbSize = when (density) {
        UiDensity.COMPACT -> 44.dp
        UiDensity.MEDIUM -> 52.dp
        UiDensity.COMFORTABLE -> 62.dp
    }

    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(rowHeight)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
            .background(if (isCurrentSong) colors.surfaceAlt.copy(alpha = 0.6f) else Color.Transparent)
            .combinedClickable(
                onClick = onClick,
                onLongClick = onLongClick
            )
            .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
            .testTag("song_item_${song.id}"),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        // Thumbnail Artwork
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(thumbSize)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                .background(colors.surfaceAlt),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            ThematicAlbumArt(
                trackId = song.id,
                title = song.title,
                artist = song.artist,
                uriString = song.uriString,
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                cornerRadius = 14.dp
            )
            if (isCurrentSong && isPlaying) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .matchParentSize()
                        .background(Color(0x66000000)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Filled.PlayArrow,
                        contentDescription = "Playing",
                        tint = Color.White,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

        // Title and Subtitle Info
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
        ) {
            Text(
                text = song.title,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                    fontWeight = if (isCurrentSong) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.SemiBold,
                    fontSize = 14.5.sp
                ),
                color = if (isCurrentSong) colors.primaryStrong else colors.textHeading,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))

            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "${song.artist} • ${song.durationFormatted}",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontSize = 12.sp),
                    color = colors.textBody,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))

        // Trailing Heart Favorite Button
        IconButton(
            onClick = {
                heartPressed = true
                onFavoriteToggle()
            },
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(38.dp)
                .scale(heartScale)
                .testTag("favorite_button_${song.id}")
        ) {
            Icon(
                imageVector = if (song.isFavorite) Icons.Filled.Favorite else Icons.Outlined.FavoriteBorder,
                contentDescription = "Favorite",
                tint = if (song.isFavorite) colors.accentHeart else colors.textMuted.copy(alpha = 0.8f),
                modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
            )
        }

        // 3-dots More Actions Button
        IconButton(
            onClick = onOptionsClick,
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(38.dp)
                .testTag("options_button_${song.id}")
        ) {
            Icon(
                imageVector = Icons.Filled.MoreVert,
                contentDescription = "Song options",
                tint = colors.textMuted,
                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

            ) {

                        .size(54.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                        .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                ) {
                    ThematicAlbumArt(
                        trackId = song.id,
                        title = song.title,
                        artist = song.artist,
                        uriString = song.uriString,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        cornerRadius = 14.dp
                    )

                        color = colors.textHeading,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "${song.artist} • ${song.folderName}",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        color = colors.textMuted,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                }
            }

            HorizontalDivider(color = colors.border.copy(alpha = 0.5f), thickness = 1.dp)
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

            // Action Items
            OptionRowItem(
                icon = Icons.Filled.SkipNext,
                title = "Play Next",
                subtitle = "Queue track immediately after current song",
                onClick = onPlayNext
            )

            OptionRowItem(
                icon = Icons.Filled.QueueMusic,
                title = "Add to Queue",
                subtitle = "Add to the end of upcoming songs",
                onClick = onAddToQueue
            )

            OptionRowItem(
                icon = Icons.Filled.PlaylistAdd,
                title = "Add to Playlist",
                subtitle = "Save to custom or favorites playlist",
                onClick = onAddToPlaylist
            )

            OptionRowItem(
                icon = if (song.isFavorite) Icons.Filled.Favorite else Icons.Outlined.FavoriteBorder,
                title = if (song.isFavorite) "Remove from Favorites" else "Add to Favorites",
                iconTint = if (song.isFavorite) colors.accentHeart else colors.textHeading,
                onClick = onToggleFavorite
            )

            OptionRowItem(
                icon = Icons.Filled.Edit,
                title = "Rename Song",
                subtitle = "Change track title and artist name",
                onClick = onRename
            )

            OptionRowItem(
                icon = Icons.Filled.Share,
                title = "Share Song",
                subtitle = "Share song details with friends",
                onClick = onShare
            )

            OptionRowItem(
                icon = Icons.Filled.FolderOff,
                title = "Blacklist Folder (${song.folderName})",
                subtitle = "Exclude all tracks from this folder",
                onClick = onBlacklistFolder
            )

            OptionRowItem(
                icon = Icons.Filled.Info,
                title = "Song Details",
                subtitle = "View file path, size, bitrate & duration",
                onClick = onSongInfo
            )

            OptionRowItem(
                icon = Icons.Filled.Delete,
                title = "Delete Song",
                subtitle = "Remove track from your library",
                iconTint = Color(0xFFE53935),
                textColor = Color(0xFFE53935),
                onClick = onDelete
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun OptionRowItem(
    icon: ImageVector,
    title: String,
    subtitle: String? = null,
    iconTint: Color? = null,
    textColor: Color? = null,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    val effectiveIconTint = iconTint ?: colors.primaryStrong
    val effectiveTextColor = textColor ?: colors.textHeading

    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
            .clickable { onClick() }
            .padding(vertical = 10.dp, horizontal = 6.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(36.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(colors.surfaceAlt),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Icon(
                imageVector = icon,
                contentDescription = title,
                tint = effectiveIconTint,
                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
            )
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(14.dp))

        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge.copy(
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                    fontSize = 14.5.sp
                ),
                color = effectiveTextColor
            )
            if (subtitle != null) {
                Text(
                    text = subtitle,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(fontSize = 11.5.sp),
                    color = colors.textMuted
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

package dev.abstra.ui.components

import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.media.MediaMetadataRetriever
import androidx.palette.graphics.Palette
import androidx.compose.foundation.Canvas
import androidx.compose.foundation.Image
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.MusicNote
import androidx.compose.material3.Icon
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.Text
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.*
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.asImageBitmap
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.asAndroidBitmap
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.drawscope.DrawScope
import androidx.compose.ui.layout.ContentScale
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import dev.abstra.ui.theme.LocalAbstraColors
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Semaphore
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

/** Data class for palette colors extracted from album art */
private data class PaletteColors(
    val dominant: Int,
    val vibrant: Int,
    val lightVibrant: Int,
    val darkVibrant: Int,
    val lightMuted: Int,
    val muted: Int
)

private object EmbeddedArtCache {
    private const val MAX_ENTRIES = 64
    @JvmField val decodeSemaphore = Semaphore(3)
    private val cache = object : LinkedHashMap<String, ImageBitmap>(MAX_ENTRIES, 0.75f, true) {
        override fun removeEldestEntry(eldest: MutableMap.MutableEntry<String, ImageBitmap>?): Boolean =
            size > MAX_ENTRIES
    }

    fun get(key: String): ImageBitmap? = synchronized(this) { cache[key] }
    fun put(key: String, bitmap: ImageBitmap) {
        synchronized(this) { cache[key] = bitmap }
    }
    fun clear() = synchronized(this) { cache.clear() }
}

/**
 * High quality, scalable album art thumbnail and artwork generator.
 * Works seamlessly in small song list rows (44dp), mini players (48dp), and full player heroes (260dp).
 */
@Composable
fun ThematicAlbumArt(
    trackId: String,
    title: String,
    artist: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    uriString: String = "",
    cornerRadius: Dp = 12.dp,
    showDetailsOnLarge: Boolean = true
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    val isDemoTrack = trackId.startsWith("demo_")
    val cacheKey = if (uriString.isNotBlank() && !isDemoTrack) uriString else null
    var embeddedBitmap by remember(trackId, uriString) { mutableStateOf(EmbeddedArtCache.get(cacheKey ?: "")) }

    // Asynchronously try to load embedded album art — sampled decode to avoid OOM, limited concurrency
    LaunchedEffect(trackId, uriString) {
        val key = cacheKey
        if (key != null && embeddedBitmap == null) {
            var retriever: MediaMetadataRetriever? = null
            var loaded: ImageBitmap? = null
            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                EmbeddedArtCache.decodeSemaphore.acquire()
                try {
                    retriever = MediaMetadataRetriever()
                    retriever.setDataSource(context, android.net.Uri.parse(uriString))
                    val artBytes = retriever.embeddedPicture
                    if (artBytes != null && artBytes.isNotEmpty()) {
                        val opts = BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inSampleSize = 2 }
                        val bitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(artBytes, 0, artBytes.size, opts)
                        if (bitmap != null) {
                            val scaled = if (bitmap.width > 300 || bitmap.height > 300) {
                                android.graphics.Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(bitmap, 300, 300, true).also {
                                    if (it !== bitmap) try { bitmap.recycle() } catch (_: Exception) {}
                                }
                            } else bitmap
                            val imageBitmap = scaled.asImageBitmap()
                            EmbeddedArtCache.put(key, imageBitmap)
                            loaded = imageBitmap
                        }
                    }
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                } finally {
                    try { retriever?.release() } catch (_: Exception) {}
                    EmbeddedArtCache.decodeSemaphore.release()
                }
            }
            if (loaded != null) embeddedBitmap = loaded
        }
    }

    // Palette colors extracted from album art
    var paletteColors by remember(trackId) { mutableStateOf<PaletteColors?>(null) }

    // Extract palette colors when bitmap is loaded
    LaunchedEffect(embeddedBitmap) {
        embeddedBitmap?.let { bmp ->
            withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
                try {
                    val androidBmp = bmp.asAndroidBitmap()
                    val palette = Palette.from(androidBmp).generate()
                    paletteColors = PaletteColors(
                        dominant = palette.getDominantColor(0xFF1A1A1A.toInt()),
                        vibrant = palette.getVibrantColor(0xFF1A1A1A.toInt()),
                        lightVibrant = palette.getLightVibrantColor(0xFF1A1A1A.toInt()),
                        darkVibrant = palette.getDarkVibrantColor(0xFF1A1A1A.toInt()),
                        lightMuted = palette.getLightMutedColor(0xFF1A1A1A.toInt()),
                        muted = palette.getMutedColor(0xFF1A1A1A.toInt())
                    )
                } catch (_: Exception) {}
            }
        }
    }

    // Gradient theme - use palette colors when available, otherwise fallback to hash-based colors
    val gradientBrush = remember(trackId, title, paletteColors) {
        paletteColors?.let { palette ->
            Brush.verticalGradient(
                listOf(
                    Color(palette.lightVibrant),
                    Color(palette.vibrant),
                    Color(palette.darkVibrant)
                )
            )
        } ?: run {
            val hash = (trackId + title).hashCode()
            when (kotlin.math.abs(hash) % 5) {
                0 -> Brush.verticalGradient(
                    listOf(Color(0xFFFF99C2), Color(0xFFFF6EA7), Color(0xFFE14E86))
                )
                1 -> Brush.linearGradient(
                    listOf(Color(0xFF8A3B66), Color(0xFFD85D8B), Color(0xFFFF9EAA))
                )
                2 -> Brush.linearGradient(
                    listOf(Color(0xFF6B429A), Color(0xFFA259B4), Color(0xFFFF82AA))
                )
                3 -> Brush.verticalGradient(
                    listOf(Color(0xFFFF9E80), Color(0xFFFF5252), Color(0xFFD81B60))
                )
                else -> Brush.verticalGradient(
                    listOf(Color(0xFF8054C8), Color(0xFFFF71CE), Color(0xFF01CDFE))
                )
            }
        }
    }

    BoxWithConstraints(
        modifier = modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(cornerRadius))
            .background(gradientBrush)
            .testTag("album_art_$trackId"),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        val isLarge = maxWidth > 90.dp
        val isTiny = maxWidth < 40.dp

        if (embeddedBitmap != null) {
            Image(
                bitmap = embeddedBitmap!!,
                contentDescription = "$title album art",
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
            )
        } else {
            // Decorative background patterns
            Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                val w = size.width
                val h = size.height

                drawCircle(
                    color = Color(0x33FFFFFF),
                    radius = w * 0.4f,
                    center = Offset(w * 0.5f, h * 0.45f)
                )

                if (isLarge) {
                    drawCircle(
                        color = Color(0x22FFFFFF),
                        radius = w * 0.35f,
                        center = Offset(w * 0.5f, h * 0.5f),
                        style = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.drawscope.Stroke(width = 2f)
                    )
                    drawCircle(
                        color = Color(0x18FFFFFF),
                        radius = w * 0.25f,
                        center = Offset(w * 0.5f, h * 0.5f),
                        style = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.drawscope.Stroke(width = 2f)
                    )
                    drawSparkle(w * 0.2f, h * 0.2f, 14f, Color.White)
                    drawSparkle(w * 0.8f, h * 0.25f, 12f, Color.White)
                    drawSparkle(w * 0.3f, h * 0.8f, 10f, Color(0xFFFFD1E3))
                } else if (!isTiny) {
                    drawSparkle(w * 0.25f, h * 0.25f, 6f, Color.White)
                    drawSparkle(w * 0.75f, h * 0.3f, 5f, Color.White)
                }
            }

            if (isLarge && showDetailsOnLarge) {
                Column(
                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(52.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(Color(0x44FFFFFF)),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        HeartIllustration(size = 28.dp, color = Color.White, withBorder = false)
                    }
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = title,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                        color = Color.White,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = artist,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        color = Color(0xFFFFF0F5),
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                    )
                }
            } else {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(if (isTiny) 20.dp else 26.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(Color(0x38FFFFFF)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Filled.MusicNote,
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = Color.White,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(if (isTiny) 12.dp else 16.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

private fun DrawScope.drawSparkle(cx: Float, cy: Float, radius: Float, color: Color) {
    val path = Path().apply {
        moveTo(cx, cy - radius)
        cubicTo(cx, cy, cx, cy, cx + radius, cy)
        cubicTo(cx, cy, cx, cy, cx + radius, cy)
        cubicTo(cx, cy, cx, cy, cx - radius, cy)
        cubicTo(cx, cy, cx, cy, cx, cy - radius)
        close()
    }
    drawPath(path, color)
}

package dev.abstra.ui.components

import androidx.compose.animation.animateColorAsState
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Folder
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.MusicNote
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.QueueMusic
import androidx.compose.material3.Icon
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.Text
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import dev.abstra.ui.theme.LocalAbstraColors
import dev.abstra.ui.viewmodel.LibraryTab

@Composable
fun TopSegmentedTabs(
    selectedTab: LibraryTab,
    onTabSelected: (LibraryTab) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    val tabs = listOf(
        Triple(LibraryTab.SONGS, "Songs", Icons.Filled.MusicNote),
        Triple(LibraryTab.PLAYLISTS, "Playlists", Icons.Filled.QueueMusic),
        Triple(LibraryTab.FOLDERS, "Folders", Icons.Filled.Folder)
    )

    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
            .testTag("top_segmented_tabs"),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        tabs.forEach { (tab, title, icon) ->
            val isSelected = selectedTab == tab
            val bgColor by animateColorAsState(
                targetValue = if (isSelected) colors.primary else colors.surfaceAlt.copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                label = "tabBg"
            )
            val textColor by animateColorAsState(
                targetValue = if (isSelected) Color.White else colors.textHeading,
                label = "tabText"
            )
            val borderColor by animateColorAsState(
                targetValue = if (isSelected) colors.primaryStrong else colors.border,
                label = "tabBorder"
            )

            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(1f)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(22.dp))
                    .background(bgColor)
                    .border(1.2.dp, borderColor, RoundedCornerShape(22.dp))
                    .clickable { onTabSelected(tab) }
                    .padding(vertical = 10.dp),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = icon,
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = textColor,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = title,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge.copy(
                            fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                            fontSize = 13.sp
                        ),
                        color = textColor
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

package dev.abstra.ui.screens

import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn

import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.shadow
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight

import dev.abstra.ui.components.*
import dev.abstra.ui.theme.LocalAbstraColors
import dev.abstra.ui.viewmodel.MusicViewModel

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun EqualizerScreen(
    viewModel: MusicViewModel,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    val context = LocalContext.current

    val isEqEnabled by viewModel.audioEngine.isEqEnabled.collectAsState()
    val bandLevels by viewModel.audioEngine.bandLevels.collectAsState()
    val bassBoostLevel by viewModel.audioEngine.bassBoostLevel.collectAsState()
    val virtualizerLevel by viewModel.audioEngine.virtualizerLevel.collectAsState()
    val activePreset: EqPreset by viewModel.audioEngine.activePreset.collectAsState()
    val showSaveEqDialog by viewModel.showSaveEqDialog.collectAsState()

    val bandFreqLabels = listOf("60 Hz", "230 Hz", "910 Hz", "3.6 kHz", "14 kHz")

    ConfettiBackground(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize().statusBarsPadding().navigationBarsPadding()
    ) {
        Scaffold(
            containerColor = Color.Transparent,
            topBar = {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = onBack,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(40.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                                .testTag("eq_back_button")
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "Back", tint = colors.textHeading)
                        }
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                        Column {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "AUDIO EQUALIZER",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                                        letterSpacing = 1.sp
                                    ),
                                    color = colors.textHeading
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                SparkleStarIcon(size = 14.dp, color = colors.primary)
                            }
                            Text(
                                text = "Fine-tune crystal highs & punchy bass",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                color = colors.textMuted
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    Switch(
                        checked = isEqEnabled,
                        onCheckedChange = { viewModel.setEqEnabled(it) },
                        colors = SwitchDefaults.colors(
                            checkedThumbColor = Color.White,
                            checkedTrackColor = colors.primary
                        ),
                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("eq_master_switch")
                    )
                }
            }
        ) { paddingValues ->
            LazyColumn(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(paddingValues)
                    .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 8.dp, bottom = 32.dp)
            ) {
                // Preset Selector Chips
                item {
                    Column {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = "SOUND PRESETS",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold),
                                color = colors.primaryStrong
                            )

                            TextButton(onClick = { viewModel.setShowSaveEqDialog(true) }) {
                                Icon(Icons.Filled.BookmarkAdd, contentDescription = null, tint = colors.primaryStrong, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                Text("Save Preset", color = colors.primaryStrong, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 12.sp)
                            }
                        }

                        LazyRow(
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                        ) {
                            items(EqPreset.values()) { preset ->
                                val isSelected = activePreset == preset
                                FilterChip(
                                    selected = isSelected,
                                    onClick = { viewModel.audioEngine.applyPreset(preset) },
                                    label = {
                                        Text(
                                            text = preset.displayName,
                                            fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
                                        )
                                    },
                                    colors = FilterChipDefaults.filterChipColors(
                                        selectedContainerColor = colors.primary,
                                        selectedLabelColor = Color.White,
                                        containerColor = colors.surfaceAlt,
                                        labelColor = colors.textHeading
                                    ),
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                                    border = null
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // 5-Band Equalizer Card
                item {
                    Surface(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .shadow(6.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), spotColor = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.25f))
                            .border(1.5.dp, colors.border, RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
                        color = colors.bgSurface
                    ) {
                        Column(
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp),
                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                            ) {
                                Text("+12 dB", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall, color = colors.textMuted)
                                Text("FREQUENCY RESPONSE", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold), color = colors.primaryStrong)
                                Text("-12 dB", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall, color = colors.textMuted)
                            }

                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                            // 5 Interactive Vertical Band Sliders
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .height(200.dp),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceEvenly,
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                bandLevels.forEachIndexed { index, level ->
                                    val label = bandFreqLabels.getOrElse(index) { "Band $index" }
                                    Column(
                                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                                    ) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = if (level > 0) "+$level" else "$level",
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                            color = if (level != 0) colors.primaryStrong else colors.textMuted
                                        )
                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))

                                        // Slider with value from -12 to 12
                                        Slider(
                                            value = level.toFloat(),
                                            onValueChange = { newVal ->
                                                viewModel.audioEngine.setBandLevel(index, newVal.toInt())
                                            },
                                            valueRange = -12f..12f,
                                            steps = 23,
                                            enabled = isEqEnabled,
                                            colors = SliderDefaults.colors(
                                                thumbColor = colors.primaryStrong,
                                                activeTrackColor = colors.primary,
                                                inactiveTrackColor = colors.surfaceAlt
                                            ),
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .height(120.dp)
                                                .testTag("eq_band_slider_$index")
                                        )

                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                        Text(
                                            text = label,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontSize = 10.sp),
                                            color = colors.textHeading,
                                            maxLines = 1
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Bass Boost & Virtualizer Cards
                item {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                    ) {
                        // Bass Boost Card
                        Surface(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .shadow(4.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                                .border(1.5.dp, colors.border, RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                            color = colors.bgSurface
                        ) {
                            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(14.dp)) {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Text("Bass Boost", style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold), color = colors.textHeading)
                                    Text("${bassBoostLevel}%", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold), color = colors.primaryStrong)
                                }
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                Slider(
                                    value = bassBoostLevel.toFloat(),
                                    onValueChange = { viewModel.audioEngine.setBassBoost(it.toInt()) },
                                    valueRange = 0f..100f,
                                    enabled = isEqEnabled,
                                    colors = SliderDefaults.colors(
                                        thumbColor = colors.primaryStrong,
                                        activeTrackColor = colors.primary
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        // 3D Virtualizer Card
                        Surface(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .shadow(4.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                                .border(1.5.dp, colors.border, RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                            color = colors.bgSurface
                        ) {
                            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(14.dp)) {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Text("3D Sound", style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold), color = colors.textHeading)
                                    Text("${virtualizerLevel}%", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold), color = colors.primaryStrong)
                                }
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                Slider(
                                    value = virtualizerLevel.toFloat(),
                                    onValueChange = { viewModel.audioEngine.setVirtualizer(it.toInt()) },
                                    valueRange = 0f..100f,
                                    enabled = isEqEnabled,
                                    colors = SliderDefaults.colors(
                                        thumbColor = colors.primaryStrong,
                                        activeTrackColor = colors.primary
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Save Custom Preset Dialog
        if (showSaveEqDialog) {
            var presetName by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
            AlertDialog(
                onDismissRequest = { viewModel.setShowSaveEqDialog(false) },
                title = { Text("Save Equalizer Preset", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = colors.textHeading) },
                text = {
                    Column {
                        Text("Name your custom EQ profile:", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = colors.textMuted)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                        OutlinedTextField(
                            value = presetName,
                            onValueChange = { presetName = it },
                            placeholder = { Text("e.g. My Acoustic Boost") },
                            singleLine = true,
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                        )
                    }
                },
                confirmButton = {
                    Button(
                        onClick = {
                            if (presetName.isNotBlank()) {
                                viewModel.saveCustomEq(presetName.trim(), bandLevels, bassBoostLevel, virtualizerLevel)
                                viewModel.setShowSaveEqDialog(false)
                                Toast.makeText(context, "Saved preset '$presetName'!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            }
                        },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = colors.primary)
                    ) {
                        Text("Save Profile", color = Color.White)
                    }
                },
                dismissButton = {
                    TextButton(onClick = { viewModel.setShowSaveEqDialog(false) }) {
                        Text("Cancel", color = colors.textMuted)
                    }
                },
                containerColor = colors.bgSurface,
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

package dev.abstra.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.itemsIndexed
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.rememberLazyListState
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.material3.pulltorefresh.PullToRefreshBox
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.shadow
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import dev.abstra.ui.components.*
import dev.abstra.ui.theme.LocalAbstraColors
import dev.abstra.ui.viewmodel.MusicViewModel

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun FolderDetailScreen(
    viewModel: MusicViewModel,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    listState: androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyListState? = null
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    val folderName by viewModel.selectedFolder.collectAsState()
    val allSongs by viewModel.allSongs.collectAsState()
    val currentSong by viewModel.currentSong.collectAsState()
    val isPlaying by viewModel.audioEngine.isPlaying.collectAsState()

    val folderSongs = remember(folderName, allSongs) {
        if (folderName == null) emptyList()
        else allSongs.filter { it.folderName.equals(folderName, ignoreCase = true) }
    }

    ConfettiBackground(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize().statusBarsPadding().navigationBarsPadding()
    ) {
        Scaffold(
            containerColor = Color.Transparent,
            topBar = {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    IconButton(
                        onClick = onBack,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(40.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                            .testTag("folder_back_button")
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "Back", tint = colors.textHeading)
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            text = folderName ?: "Folder",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold),
                            color = colors.textHeading,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "${folderSongs.size} songs in directory",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            color = colors.textMuted
                        )
                    }

                    if (folderSongs.isNotEmpty()) {
                        Button(
                            onClick = {
                                viewModel.playSongFromList(folderSongs, folderSongs.first())
                            },
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = colors.primary),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.PlayArrow, contentDescription = "Play All", tint = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                            Text("Play All", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        }
                    }
                }
            },
            bottomBar = {
                MiniPlayer(
                    currentSong = currentSong,
                    isPlaying = isPlaying,
                    onPlayPause = { viewModel.audioEngine.togglePlayPause() },
                    onNext = { viewModel.audioEngine.next() },
                    onPrevious = { viewModel.audioEngine.previous() },
                    onClick = { viewModel.openFullPlayer() }
                )
            }
        ) { paddingValues ->
            if (folderSongs.isEmpty()) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .padding(paddingValues),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                        Text("No songs in this folder", color = colors.textHeading, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        Text("Folder may be filtered by blacklist/whitelist rules", color = colors.textMuted, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall)
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                        TextButton(onClick = onBack) { Text("Back to folders") }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                val isRefreshing by viewModel.isRefreshing.collectAsState()
                val listStateInternal = listState ?: rememberLazyListState()
                PullToRefreshBox(
                    isRefreshing = isRefreshing,
                    onRefresh = { viewModel.refreshLibrary() },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .padding(paddingValues)
                ) {
                    LazyColumn(
                        state = listStateInternal,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxSize()
                            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 8.dp, bottom = 80.dp)
                    ) {
                        itemsIndexed(folderSongs, key = { _, s -> s.id }, contentType = { _, _ -> "folderSong" }) { index, song ->
                        val isCurrent = currentSong?.id == song.id
                        Surface(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .shadow(if (isCurrent) 6.dp else 2.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                                .clickable { viewModel.playSongFromList(folderSongs, song) }
                                .border(
                                    width = if (isCurrent) 1.5.dp else 1.dp,
                                    color = if (isCurrent) colors.primaryStrong else colors.border,
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                                ),
                            color = if (isCurrent) colors.surfaceAlt else colors.bgSurface
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .size(42.dp)
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                                ) {
                                    ThematicAlbumArt(
                                        trackId = song.id,
                                        title = song.title,
                                        artist = song.artist,
                                        uriString = song.uriString,
                                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                                    )
                                }

                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

                                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = song.title,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                        color = if (isCurrent) colors.primaryStrong else colors.textHeading,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                    Text(
                                        text = "${song.artist} • ${song.durationFormatted}",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                        color = colors.textMuted
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/ui/screens/FullPlayerScreen.kt
