package dev.abstra.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.animation.AnimatedVisibility
import androidx.compose.animation.core.*
import androidx.compose.animation.core.RepeatMode as AnimRepeatMode
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.basicMarquee
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.gestures.detectDragGestures
import androidx.compose.foundation.gestures.detectHorizontalDragGestures
import androidx.compose.foundation.gestures.detectTapGestures
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.itemsIndexed
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.*
import androidx.compose.material.icons.outlined.FavoriteBorder
import androidx.compose.material.icons.outlined.Repeat
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.alpha
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.scale
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.shadow
import androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.graphicsLayer
import androidx.compose.ui.hapticfeedback.HapticFeedbackType
import androidx.compose.ui.input.pointer.pointerInput
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalHapticFeedback
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.IntOffset
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.roundToInt
import dev.abstra.data.model.PlayerDesignStyle
import dev.abstra.data.model.RepeatMode
import dev.abstra.ui.components.*
import dev.abstra.ui.theme.LocalAbstraColors
import dev.abstra.ui.viewmodel.ActiveScreen
import dev.abstra.ui.viewmodel.MusicViewModel

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun FullPlayerScreen(
    viewModel: MusicViewModel,
    onClose: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    val context = LocalContext.current

    val currentSong by viewModel.currentSong.collectAsState()
    val isPlaying by viewModel.audioEngine.isPlaying.collectAsState()
    val currentPosition by viewModel.audioEngine.currentPosition.collectAsState()
    val duration by viewModel.audioEngine.duration.collectAsState()
    val queue by viewModel.audioEngine.queue.collectAsState()
    val currentIndex by viewModel.audioEngine.currentIndex.collectAsState()
    val repeatMode by viewModel.audioEngine.repeatMode.collectAsState()
    val isShuffle by viewModel.audioEngine.isShuffle.collectAsState()
    val playbackSpeed by viewModel.audioEngine.playbackSpeed.collectAsState()
    val showLyrics by viewModel.showLyrics.collectAsState()
    val playerDesignStyle by viewModel.playerDesignStyle.collectAsState()
    val showPlayerStyleDialog by viewModel.showPlayerStyleDialog.collectAsState()

    var showQueueSheet by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showSpeedDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var heartPressed by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    var coverSwipeX by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(0f) }
    var coverSnapBack by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var coverHapticDone by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var playerDragY by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(0f) }
    var playerSnapBack by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(coverSnapBack) {
        if (coverSnapBack) {
            androidx.compose.animation.core.animate(
                coverSwipeX, 0f, animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.7f, stiffness = 420f)
            ) { value, _ -> coverSwipeX = value }
            coverSnapBack = false
            coverHapticDone = false
        }
    }
    LaunchedEffect(playerSnapBack) {
        if (playerSnapBack) {
            androidx.compose.animation.core.animate(
                playerDragY, 0f, animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.8f, stiffness = 380f)
            ) { value, _ -> playerDragY = value }
            playerSnapBack = false
        }
    }

    val heartScale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (heartPressed) 1.35f else 1.0f,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.4f),
        label = "heartScale",
        finishedListener = { heartPressed = false }
    )

    // Vinyl rotation — battery-optimized using infiniteTransition and graphicsLayer
    // This runs on GPU during draw phase, no recomposition required
    val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "vinylSpin")
    val spinRotation by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0f,
        targetValue = 360f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(durationMillis = 8000, easing = LinearEasing),
            repeatMode = AnimRepeatMode.Restart
        ),
        label = "rotation"
    )

    ConfettiBackground(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize().statusBarsPadding().navigationBarsPadding()
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                .testTag("full_player_screen")
                .offset { IntOffset(0, playerDragY.roundToInt()) }
                .alpha((1f - (playerDragY / 800f).coerceIn(0f, 0.5f)))
                .pointerInput(Unit) {
                    detectDragGestures(
                        onDragEnd = {
                            if (playerDragY > 200f) { onClose() }
                            playerSnapBack = true
                        },
                        onDragCancel = { playerSnapBack = true },
                        onDrag = { change, dragAmount ->
                            change.consume()
                            playerDragY = (playerDragY + dragAmount.y).coerceAtLeast(0f)
                        }
                    )
                },
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
        ) {
            // Top Bar: Bow Icon, Title with Sparkles, Preset Style Switcher & Collapse
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(vertical = 4.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                IconButton(
                    onClick = onClose,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(38.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                        .testTag("player_close_button")
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Filled.KeyboardArrowDown,
                        contentDescription = "Collapse Player",
                        tint = colors.textHeading,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(26.dp)
                    )
                }

                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                ) {
                    SparkleStarIcon(size = 14.dp, color = colors.primary)
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "ABSTRA",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                            letterSpacing = 2.sp,
                            fontSize = 15.sp
                        ),
                        color = colors.textHeading,
                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                    SparkleStarIcon(size = 14.dp, color = colors.primary)
                }

                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
                ) {
                    IconButton(
                        onClick = { viewModel.setShowPlayerStyleDialog(true) },
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(38.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                            .testTag("player_style_switcher_button")
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Filled.Palette,
                            contentDescription = "Player Design",
                            tint = colors.primaryStrong,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                        )
                    }

                    IconButton(
                        onClick = {
                            currentSong?.let { viewModel.shareSong(context, it) }
                        },
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(38.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                            .testTag("player_share_button")
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Filled.Share,
                            contentDescription = "Share Track",
                            tint = colors.textHeading,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // Fixed Center Card Container: Preset Artwork Hub & Controls
            Surface(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .weight(1f)
                    .padding(vertical = 4.dp)
                    .shadow(16.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(32.dp), spotColor = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.35f))
                .border(2.dp, colors.border, RoundedCornerShape(32.dp)),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(32.dp),
                color = colors.bgSurface
            ) {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .padding(horizontal = 18.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceEvenly
                ) {
                    val haptic = LocalHapticFeedback.current
                    // Dynamic Centerpiece matching all 6 PlayerDesignStyle options from the image
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .sizeIn(maxWidth = 230.dp, maxHeight = 230.dp)
                            .aspectRatio(1f)
                            .testTag("player_centerpiece_container")
                            .graphicsLayer {
                                translationX = coverSwipeX
                                val progress = (abs(coverSwipeX) / 220f).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                                alpha = 1f - progress * 0.22f
                                scaleX = 1f - progress * 0.06f
                                scaleY = 1f - progress * 0.06f
                            }
                            .pointerInput(Unit) {
                                detectTapGestures(
                                    onDoubleTap = {
                                        viewModel.audioEngine.togglePlayPause()
                                    }
                                )
                            }
                            .pointerInput(Unit) {
                                detectHorizontalDragGestures(
                                    onDragEnd = {
                                        val threshold = 72f
                                        when {
                                            coverSwipeX > threshold -> {
                                                // Swiped right -> Previous (intuitive)
                                                haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.TextHandleMove)
                                                viewModel.audioEngine.previous()
                                            }
                                            coverSwipeX < -threshold -> {
                                                // Swiped left -> Next
                                                haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.TextHandleMove)
                                                viewModel.audioEngine.next()
                                            }
                                        }
                                        coverSnapBack = true
                                    },
                                    onDragCancel = { coverSnapBack = true },
                                    onHorizontalDrag = { change, dragAmount ->
                                        change.consume()
                                        val resistance = if (abs(coverSwipeX) > 90f) 0.5f else 1f
                                        coverSwipeX += dragAmount * resistance
                                        if (!coverHapticDone && abs(coverSwipeX) > 72f) {
                                            haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.TextHandleMove)
                                            coverHapticDone = true
                                        } else if (abs(coverSwipeX) < 36f) {
                                            coverHapticDone = false
                                        }
                                    }
                                )
                            },
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        when (playerDesignStyle) {
                            PlayerDesignStyle.ROTATING_CD -> {
                                RotatingCdCenterpiece(
                                    song = currentSong,
                                    isPlaying = isPlaying,
                                    rotation = spinRotation,
                                    colors = colors
                                )
                            }
                            PlayerDesignStyle.POLAROID_FRAME -> {
                                PolaroidCenterpiece(
                                    song = currentSong,
                                    isPlaying = isPlaying,
                                    colors = colors
                                )
                            }
                            PlayerDesignStyle.HEART_WINDOW -> {
                                HeartWindowCenterpiece(
                                    song = currentSong,
                                    isPlaying = isPlaying,
                                    colors = colors
                                )
                            }
                            PlayerDesignStyle.VINYL_RECORD -> {
                                VinylRecordCenterpiece(
                                    song = currentSong,
                                    isPlaying = isPlaying,
                                    rotation = spinRotation,
                                    colors = colors
                                )
                            }
                            PlayerDesignStyle.TV_SCREEN -> {
                                TvScreenCenterpiece(
                                    song = currentSong,
                                    isPlaying = isPlaying,
                                    colors = colors
                                )
                            }
                            PlayerDesignStyle.CLOUD_FRAME -> {
                                CloudFrameCenterpiece(
                                    song = currentSong,
                                    isPlaying = isPlaying,
                                    colors = colors
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Song Info: Title (e.g. "daydreams"), Artist (e.g. "lovelyy")
                    Column(
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = currentSong?.title ?: "No track selected",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                                fontSize = 21.sp
                            ),
                            color = colors.textHeading,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                            modifier = Modifier.basicMarquee()
                        )

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))

                        Text(
                            text = currentSong?.artist ?: "Select a song from your library",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                                fontSize = 14.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                            ),
                            color = colors.textMuted,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                            modifier = Modifier.basicMarquee()
                        )
                    }

                    // Scrubber Bar with Heart Thumb & Timestamps (01:23 / 03:45)
                    HeartScrubber(
                        currentPositionMs = currentPosition,
                        durationMs = duration,
                        onSeek = { viewModel.audioEngine.seekTo(it) },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                    )

                    // Main Controls matching the exact icons in the image: Shuffle, Prev, Big Play/Pause, Next, Repeat
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(horizontal = 4.dp),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        // Shuffle Toggle
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = { viewModel.audioEngine.toggleShuffle() },
                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("player_shuffle_button")
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Filled.Shuffle,
                                contentDescription = "Shuffle",
                                tint = if (isShuffle) colors.primaryStrong else colors.textMuted,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                            )
                        }

                        // Previous Button
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = { viewModel.audioEngine.previous() },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(48.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                                .border(1.dp, colors.border, CircleShape)
                                .testTag("player_prev_button")
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Filled.SkipPrevious,
                                contentDescription = "Previous",
                                tint = colors.primaryStrong,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(26.dp)
                            )
                        }

                        // Big Hero Play / Pause Button
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(66.dp)
                                .shadow(10.dp, shape = CircleShape, spotColor = colors.primary)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(colors.primary)
                                .border(3.dp, colors.primaryStrong, CircleShape)
                                .clickable { viewModel.audioEngine.togglePlayPause() }
                                .testTag("player_play_pause_button"),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = if (isPlaying) Icons.Filled.Pause else Icons.Filled.PlayArrow,
                                contentDescription = if (isPlaying) "Pause" else "Play",
                                tint = Color.White,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(34.dp)
                            )
                        }

                        // Next Button
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = { viewModel.audioEngine.next() },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(48.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                                .border(1.dp, colors.border, CircleShape)
                                .testTag("player_next_button")
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Filled.SkipNext,
                                contentDescription = "Next",
                                tint = colors.primaryStrong,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(26.dp)
                            )
                        }

                        // Repeat Mode Toggle
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = { viewModel.audioEngine.toggleRepeatMode() },
                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("player_repeat_button")
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = when (repeatMode) {
                                    RepeatMode.REPEAT_ONE -> Icons.Filled.RepeatOne
                                    RepeatMode.REPEAT_ALL -> Icons.Filled.Repeat
                                    RepeatMode.ONE_TIME -> Icons.Outlined.Repeat
                                },
                                contentDescription = "Repeat",
                                tint = if (repeatMode != RepeatMode.ONE_TIME) colors.primaryStrong else colors.textMuted,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    // Secondary Tools: Speed, Lyrics, Favorite, Equalizer, Queue
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(horizontal = 4.dp),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        // Speed Pill
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                                .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                                .clickable { showSpeedDialog = true }
                                .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 5.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = "${playbackSpeed}x",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                color = colors.textHeading
                            )
                        }

                        // Lyrics Toggle Pill
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                                .background(if (showLyrics) colors.primary else colors.surfaceAlt)
                                .clickable { viewModel.toggleLyrics() }
                                .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 5.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = "lyrics",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    fontSize = 11.sp
                                ),
                                color = if (showLyrics) Color.White else colors.textHeading
                            )
                        }

                        // Heart Favorite Button
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = {
                                heartPressed = true
                                currentSong?.let { viewModel.toggleFavorite(it) }
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(36.dp)
                                .scale(heartScale)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                                .border(1.dp, colors.border, CircleShape)
                                .testTag("player_favorite_toggle")
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = if (currentSong?.isFavorite == true) Icons.Filled.Favorite else Icons.Outlined.FavoriteBorder,
                                contentDescription = "Favorite",
                                tint = if (currentSong?.isFavorite == true) colors.accentHeart else colors.textMuted,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                            )
                        }

                        // Equalizer Shortcut
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = { viewModel.navigateTo(ActiveScreen.EQUALIZER) },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(36.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                                .border(1.dp, colors.border, CircleShape)
                                .testTag("player_eq_shortcut")
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Filled.GraphicEq,
                                contentDescription = "Equalizer",
                                tint = colors.primaryStrong,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                            )
                        }

                        // Up Next Queue Button
                        Button(
                            onClick = { showQueueSheet = true },
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = colors.primary),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
                            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 5.dp),
                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("player_queue_toggle_button")
                        ) {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Filled.QueueMusic,
                                    contentDescription = "Queue",
                                    tint = Color.White,
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(15.dp)
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = "Up Next (${queue.size})",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                    color = Color.White
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Synced / Plain Lyrics View if expanded
            AnimatedVisibility(visible = showLyrics) {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(vertical = 4.dp),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = colors.surfaceAlt.copy(alpha = 0.95f))
                ) {
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp),
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = currentSong?.lyrics ?: "(No lyrics available for this track)",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                                lineHeight = 22.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                            ),
                            color = colors.textHeading,
                            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Slide-up Queue Bottom Sheet
        if (showQueueSheet) {
            ModalBottomSheet(
                onDismissRequest = { showQueueSheet = false },
                containerColor = colors.bgSurface,
                dragHandle = {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .padding(vertical = 10.dp)
                            .width(40.dp)
                            .height(4.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(2.dp))
                            .background(colors.border)
                    )
                }
            ) {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
                        .padding(bottom = 24.dp)
                        .testTag("queue_bottom_sheet")
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(vertical = 8.dp),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Filled.QueueMusic,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = colors.primaryStrong,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Upcoming Queue (${queue.size} tracks)",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                color = colors.textHeading
                            )
                        }

                        IconButton(onClick = { showQueueSheet = false }) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.Close, contentDescription = "Close", tint = colors.textMuted)
                        }
                    }

                    HorizontalDivider(color = colors.border.copy(alpha = 0.5f), thickness = 1.dp)
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                    if (queue.isEmpty()) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(vertical = 32.dp),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Text("Queue is empty", color = colors.textMuted)
                        }
                    } else {
                        LazyColumn(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .heightIn(max = 380.dp),
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
                        ) {
                            itemsIndexed(queue, key = { index, song -> "${song.id}_$index" }) { index, song ->
                                val isCurrent = index == currentIndex
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                                        .background(if (isCurrent) colors.surfaceAlt else Color.Transparent)
                                        .clickable {
                                            viewModel.audioEngine.setQueue(queue, startIndex = index, autoPlay = true)
                                            showQueueSheet = false
                                        }
                                        .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
                                        .testTag("queue_item_$index"),
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    if (isCurrent) {
                                        Icon(
                                            imageVector = Icons.Filled.PlayArrow,
                                            contentDescription = "Current",
                                            tint = colors.primaryStrong,
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                                        )
                                    } else {
                                        Text(
                                            text = "${index + 1}",
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                            color = colors.textMuted,
                                            modifier = Modifier.width(18.dp),
                                            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                                        )
                                    }

                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))

                                    Box(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .size(38.dp)
                                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                            .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                                    ) {
                                        ThematicAlbumArt(
                                            trackId = song.id,
                                            title = song.title,
                                            artist = song.artist,
                                            uriString = song.uriString,
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                            cornerRadius = 8.dp
                                        )
                                    }

                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

                                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = song.title,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                                                fontWeight = if (isCurrent) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium
                                            ),
                                            color = if (isCurrent) colors.primaryStrong else colors.textHeading,
                                            maxLines = 1,
                                            modifier = Modifier.basicMarquee()
                                        )
                                        Text(
                                            text = song.artist,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                            color = colors.textMuted,
                                            maxLines = 1,
                                            modifier = Modifier.basicMarquee()
                                        )
                                    }

                                    IconButton(
                                        onClick = { viewModel.audioEngine.removeFromQueue(song.id) },
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(
                                            imageVector = Icons.Filled.Close,
                                            contentDescription = "Remove",
                                            tint = colors.textMuted,
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Speed Selector Dialog
        if (showSpeedDialog) {
            AlertDialog(
                onDismissRequest = { showSpeedDialog = false },
                title = {
                    Text(
                        text = "Playback Speed",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                        color = colors.textHeading
                    )
                },
                text = {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                        val speeds = listOf(0.5f, 0.75f, 1.0f, 1.25f, 1.5f, 2.0f)
                        speeds.forEach { speed ->
                            val isSelected = playbackSpeed == speed
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                    .background(if (isSelected) colors.surfaceAlt else Color.Transparent)
                                    .clickable {
                                        viewModel.setPlaybackSpeed(speed)
                                        showSpeedDialog = false
                                    }
                                    .padding(vertical = 12.dp, horizontal = 16.dp),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "${speed}x",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge.copy(
                                        fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
                                    ),
                                    color = if (isSelected) colors.primaryStrong else colors.textHeading
                                )
                                if (isSelected) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Filled.Check,
                                        contentDescription = "Selected",
                                        tint = colors.primaryStrong,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                },
                confirmButton = {
                    TextButton(onClick = { showSpeedDialog = false }) {
                        Text("Close", color = colors.primaryStrong)
                    }
                },
                containerColor = colors.bgSurface,
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
            )
        }

        // Player UI Style Selection Dialog
        if (showPlayerStyleDialog) {
            PlayerStyleSelectionDialog(
                currentStyle = playerDesignStyle,
                onSelectStyle = { viewModel.setPlayerDesignStyle(it) },
                onDismiss = { viewModel.setShowPlayerStyleDialog(false) }
            )
        }
    }
}

package dev.abstra.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.itemsIndexed
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.shadow
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import dev.abstra.data.model.Song
import dev.abstra.ui.components.*
import dev.abstra.ui.theme.LocalAbstraColors
import dev.abstra.ui.viewmodel.MusicViewModel

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun PlaylistDetailScreen(
    viewModel: MusicViewModel,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    val playlist by viewModel.selectedPlaylist.collectAsState()
    val allSongs by viewModel.allSongs.collectAsState()
    val playlists by viewModel.playlists.collectAsState()
    val currentSong by viewModel.currentSong.collectAsState()
    val isPlaying by viewModel.audioEngine.isPlaying.collectAsState()
    val showAddSongsToPlaylistDialog by viewModel.showAddSongsToPlaylistDialog.collectAsState()

    var playlistSongs by remember { mutableStateOf<List<Song>>(emptyList()) }

    LaunchedEffect(playlist) {
        val p = playlist ?: return@LaunchedEffect
        
        if (p.isSmartFavorites) {
            viewModel.allSongs.collect { songs ->
                playlistSongs = songs.filter { it.isFavorite }
            }
        } else {
            viewModel.repository.getSongsForPlaylist(p.id).collect { songs ->
                playlistSongs = songs
            }
        }
    }

    ConfettiBackground(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize().statusBarsPadding().navigationBarsPadding()
    ) {
        Scaffold(
            containerColor = Color.Transparent,
            topBar = {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    IconButton(
                        onClick = onBack,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(40.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                            .testTag("playlist_back_button")
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "Back", tint = colors.textHeading)
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            text = playlist?.name ?: "Playlist",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold),
                            color = colors.textHeading,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "${playlistSongs.size} tracks",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            color = colors.textMuted
                        )
                    }

                    if (playlist?.isSmartFavorites == false) {
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = { viewModel.setShowAddSongsToPlaylistDialog(true) },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(36.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(colors.primary)
                                .testTag("add_songs_to_playlist_button")
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.Add, contentDescription = "Add Songs", tint = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                        }
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    }

                    if (playlistSongs.isNotEmpty()) {
                        Button(
                            onClick = {
                                viewModel.playSongFromList(playlistSongs, playlistSongs.first())
                            },
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = colors.primary),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.PlayArrow, contentDescription = "Play All", tint = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                            Text("Play All", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        }
                    }
                }
            },
            bottomBar = {
                MiniPlayer(
                    currentSong = currentSong,
                    isPlaying = isPlaying,
                    onPlayPause = { viewModel.audioEngine.togglePlayPause() },
                    onNext = { viewModel.audioEngine.next() },
                    onPrevious = { viewModel.audioEngine.previous() },
                    onClick = { viewModel.openFullPlayer() }
                )
            }
        ) { paddingValues ->
            if (playlistSongs.isEmpty()) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .padding(paddingValues),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                        BowRibbonIcon(size = 40.dp)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "No songs in this playlist yet",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                            color = colors.textHeading
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "Tap the + button above to add songs!",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            color = colors.textMuted
                        )
                    }
                }
            } else {
                LazyColumn(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .padding(paddingValues)
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 8.dp, bottom = 80.dp)
                ) {
                    itemsIndexed(playlistSongs, key = { _, s -> s.id }) { index, song ->
                        val isCurrent = currentSong?.id == song.id
                        Surface(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .shadow(if (isCurrent) 6.dp else 2.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                                .clickable { viewModel.playSongFromList(playlistSongs, song) }
                                .border(
                                    width = if (isCurrent) 1.5.dp else 1.dp,
                                    color = if (isCurrent) colors.primaryStrong else colors.border,
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                                ),
                            color = if (isCurrent) colors.surfaceAlt else colors.bgSurface
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .size(42.dp)
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                                ) {
                                    ThematicAlbumArt(
                                        trackId = song.id,
                                        title = song.title,
                                        artist = song.artist,
                                        uriString = song.uriString,
                                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                                    )
                                }

                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

                                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = song.title,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                        color = if (isCurrent) colors.primaryStrong else colors.textHeading,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                    Text(
                                        text = "${song.artist} • ${song.durationFormatted}",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                        color = colors.textMuted
                                    )
                                }

                                if (playlist?.isSmartFavorites == false) {
                                    IconButton(
                                        onClick = {
                                            playlist?.id?.let { pId ->
                                                viewModel.removeSongFromPlaylist(pId, song.id)
                                            }
                                        }
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(Icons.Filled.RemoveCircleOutline, contentDescription = "Remove from playlist", tint = colors.textMuted)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Add Songs to Playlist Dialog
        if (showAddSongsToPlaylistDialog) {
            AddSongsToPlaylistDialog(
                allSongs = allSongs,
                existingSongIds = playlistSongs.map { it.id }.toSet(),
                onDismiss = { viewModel.setShowAddSongsToPlaylistDialog(false) },
                onConfirm = { selectedIds ->
                    playlist?.id?.let { pId ->
                        viewModel.addSongsToPlaylist(pId, selectedIds)
                    }
                }
            )
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun AddSongsToPlaylistDialog(
    allSongs: List<Song>,
    existingSongIds: Set<String>,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onConfirm: (List<String>) -> Unit
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current
    var selectedIds by remember { mutableStateOf(setOf<String>()) }
    var searchQuery by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    val filteredSongs = remember(allSongs, searchQuery, existingSongIds) {
        val available = allSongs.filter { !existingSongIds.contains(it.id) }
        if (searchQuery.isBlank()) available
        else available.filter {
            it.title.contains(searchQuery, ignoreCase = true) ||
            it.artist.contains(searchQuery, ignoreCase = true)
        }
    }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Text(
                text = "Add Songs to Playlist",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                color = colors.textHeading
            )
        },
        text = {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = searchQuery,
                    onValueChange = { searchQuery = it },
                    placeholder = { Text("Search songs...", color = colors.textMuted) },
                    leadingIcon = { Icon(Icons.Filled.Search, contentDescription = null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)) },
                    singleLine = true,
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = colors.primary,
                        unfocusedBorderColor = colors.border
                    ),
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                if (selectedIds.isNotEmpty()) {
                    Text(
                        text = "${selectedIds.size} songs selected",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                        color = colors.primaryStrong
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                }

                if (filteredSongs.isEmpty()) {
                    Text(
                        text = "No more songs available to add",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        color = colors.textMuted,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 16.dp)
                    )
                } else {
                    LazyColumn(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .heightIn(max = 300.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
                    ) {
                        items(filteredSongs.size) { index ->
                            val song = filteredSongs[index]
                            val isSelected = selectedIds.contains(song.id)
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                    .background(if (isSelected) colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.1f) else Color.Transparent)
                                    .clickable {
                                        selectedIds = if (isSelected) {
                                            selectedIds - song.id
                                        } else {
                                            selectedIds + song.id
                                        }
                                    }
                                    .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Checkbox(
                                    checked = isSelected,
                                    onCheckedChange = { checked ->
                                        selectedIds = if (checked) {
                                            selectedIds + song.id
                                        } else {
                                            selectedIds - song.id
                                        }
                                    },
                                    colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(checkedColor = colors.primary)
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = song.title,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                        color = colors.textHeading,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                    Text(
                                        text = song.artist,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                        color = colors.textMuted,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    onConfirm(selectedIds.toList())
                    onDismiss()
                },
                enabled = selectedIds.isNotEmpty(),
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = colors.primary),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
            ) {
                Text("Add ${selectedIds.size} Songs", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("Cancel", color = colors.textMuted)
            }
        },
        containerColor = colors.bgSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
    )
}

                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Filled.Restore, contentDescription = null, tint = colors.primaryStrong, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                Text("Restore from Backup (Soon)", color = colors.primaryStrong, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            }
                            Text(
                                "Your library is automatically backed up by Android. Manual file export will be available in a future update.",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                color = colors.textMuted
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Crossfade Dialog
        if (showCrossfadeDialog) {
            AlertDialog(
                onDismissRequest = { showCrossfadeDialog = false },
                title = { Text("Crossfade Overlap", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = colors.textHeading) },
                text = {
                    Column {
                        listOf(0, 2, 4, 6, 8, 10).forEach { sec ->
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .clickable {
                                        viewModel.setCrossfade(sec)
                                        showCrossfadeDialog = false
                                    }
                                    .padding(vertical = 10.dp),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Text(if (sec == 0) "Off (0 seconds)" else "$sec seconds", color = colors.textHeading)
                                if (crossfadeSeconds == sec) {
                                    Icon(Icons.Filled.Check, contentDescription = null, tint = colors.primaryStrong)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                },
                confirmButton = {
                    TextButton(onClick = { showCrossfadeDialog = false }) {
                        Text("Done", color = colors.primaryStrong)
                    }
                },
                containerColor = colors.bgSurface,
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SectionHeader(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    colors: dev.abstra.ui.theme.AbstraColors
) {
    Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 4.dp)) {
        Text(
            text = title,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold, letterSpacing = 1.sp),
            color = colors.primaryStrong
        )
        Text(
            text = subtitle,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
            color = colors.textMuted
        )
    }
}

import androidx.compose.foundation.background
