@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun StatsScreen(
    viewModel: MusicViewModel,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val colors = LocalAbstraColors.current

    val totalLifetimePlaytime by viewModel.totalLifetimePlaytime.collectAsState()
    val totalPlayCount by viewModel.totalPlayCount.collectAsState()
    val monthlyStats by viewModel.monthlyPlaytimeStats.collectAsState()
    val topPlayedSongs by viewModel.topPlayedSongs.collectAsState()

    val totalMinutes = totalLifetimePlaytime / 60000
    val totalHours = totalMinutes / 60
    val remainingMins = totalMinutes % 60

    ConfettiBackground(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize().statusBarsPadding().navigationBarsPadding()
    ) {
        Scaffold(
            containerColor = Color.Transparent,
            topBar = {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    IconButton(
                        onClick = onBack,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(40.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(colors.surfaceAlt)
                            .testTag("stats_back_button")
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "Back", tint = colors.textHeading)
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        AnalyticsChartIllustration(size = 28.dp, color = colors.primaryStrong)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "Playtime & Listening Stats",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold),
                            color = colors.textHeading
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        ) { paddingValues ->
            LazyColumn(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(paddingValues)
                    .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 8.dp, bottom = 32.dp)
            ) {
                // Key Metrics summary cards
                item {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                    ) {
                        // Total Hours Card
                        Surface(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .shadow(6.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp), spotColor = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.25f))
                                .border(1.5.dp, colors.border, RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                            color = colors.bgSurface
                        ) {
                            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "TOTAL PLAYTIME",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                    color = colors.primaryStrong
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = if (totalHours > 0) "${totalHours}h ${remainingMins}m" else "${totalMinutes}m",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold),
                                    color = colors.textHeading
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = "Lifetime streamed audio",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                    color = colors.textMuted
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        // Total Plays Card
                        Surface(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .shadow(6.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp), spotColor = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.25f))
                                .border(1.5.dp, colors.border, RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                            color = colors.bgSurface
                        ) {
                            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "TOTAL PLAYS",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                    color = colors.primaryStrong
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = "$totalPlayCount",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold),
                                    color = colors.textHeading
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = "Song playback sessions",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                    color = colors.textMuted
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Monthly Activity Bar Visualizer
                item {
                    Surface(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .shadow(6.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), spotColor = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                            .border(1.5.dp, colors.border, RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
                        color = colors.bgSurface
                    ) {
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "MONTHLY ACTIVITY",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold),
                                    color = colors.primaryStrong
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = "Minutes listened",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                    color = colors.textMuted
                                )
                            }

                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                            if (monthlyStats.isEmpty()) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .height(120.dp),
                                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                ) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = "Start listening to songs to generate your monthly activity chart! ✨",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                        color = colors.textMuted
                                    )
                                }
                            } else {
                                val visible = monthlyStats.takeLast(6)
                                val maxMinutes = ((visible.maxOfOrNull { it.totalPlayMs } ?: 1L) / 60000L).toFloat().coerceAtLeast(1f)

                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .height(130.dp),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceEvenly,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.Bottom
                                ) {
                                    visible.forEach { stat ->
                                        val mins = (stat.totalPlayMs / 60000L).toFloat()
                                        val heightFraction = (mins / maxMinutes).coerceIn(0.08f, 1f)

                                        Column(
                                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Bottom,
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxHeight()
                                        ) {
                                            Text(
                                                text = "${mins.toInt()}m",
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontSize = 9.sp),
                                                color = colors.textMuted
                                            )
                                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                                            Box(
                                                modifier = Modifier
                                                    .width(28.dp)
                                                    .fillMaxHeight(heightFraction)
                                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 8.dp, topEnd = 8.dp))
                                                    .background(colors.primary)
                                            )
                                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                            Text(
                                                text = stat.yearMonth.takeLast(2),
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                                color = colors.textHeading
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Top Played Songs
                item {
                    Text(
                        text = "TOP PLAYED TRACKS",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold),
                        color = colors.primaryStrong
                    )
                }

                if (topPlayedSongs.isEmpty()) {
                    item {
                        Surface(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(vertical = 12.dp),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                            color = colors.bgSurface
                        ) {
                            Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(20.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                Text("No playback history recorded yet.", color = colors.textMuted)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    itemsIndexed(topPlayedSongs, key = { _, s -> s.song.id }) { index, topSong ->
                        Surface(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .shadow(2.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                                .border(1.dp, colors.border, RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                            color = colors.bgSurface
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .size(32.dp)
                                        .clip(CircleShape)
                                        .background(if (index < 3) colors.primary else colors.surfaceAlt),
                                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                ) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = "#${index + 1}",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                        color = if (index < 3) Color.White else colors.textHeading
                                    )
                                }

                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

                                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = topSong.song.title,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                        color = colors.textHeading,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                    Text(
                                        text = topSong.song.artist,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                        color = colors.textMuted
                                    )
                                }

                                Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.End) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = "${topSong.playCount} plays",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                        color = colors.primaryStrong
                                    )
                                    val songMinutes = topSong.totalMs / 60000
                                    Text(
                                        text = "${songMinutes}m total",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                        color = colors.textMuted
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

package dev.abstra.ui.theme

import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import dev.abstra.data.model.AppTheme

data class AbstraColors(
    val bgBase: Color,
    val bgSurface: Color,
    val surfaceAlt: Color,
    val primary: Color,
    val primaryStrong: Color,
    val textHeading: Color,
    val textBody: Color,
    val textMuted: Color,
    val accentHeart: Color,
    val border: Color,
    val destructive: Color,
    val onPrimary: Color = Color.White
)

val PookieThemeColors = AbstraColors(
    bgBase = PookieBgBase,
    bgSurface = PookieBgSurface,
    surfaceAlt = PookieSurfaceAlt,
    primary = PookiePrimary,
    primaryStrong = PookiePrimaryStrong,
    textHeading = PookieTextHeading,
    textBody = PookieTextBody,
    textMuted = PookieTextMuted,
    accentHeart = PookieAccentHeart,
    border = PookieBorder,
    destructive = Color(0xFFE53935),
    onPrimary = Color.White
)

val AbstraThemeColors: Map<AppTheme, AbstraColors> = mapOf(
    AppTheme.COTTON_CANDY to AbstraColors(
        bgBase = PookieCottonCandy.BgBase,
        bgSurface = PookieCottonCandy.BgSurface,
        surfaceAlt = PookieCottonCandy.SurfaceAlt,
        primary = PookieCottonCandy.Primary,
        primaryStrong = PookieCottonCandy.PrimaryStrong,

        onPrimary = PookieCottonCandy.White
    ),
    AppTheme.PEACH_BLOSSOM to AbstraColors(

        surfaceAlt = PookiePeachBlossom.SurfaceAlt,

        onPrimary = PookiePeachBlossom.White
    ),
    AppTheme.MILKY_PINK to AbstraColors(
        bgBase = PookieMilkyPink.BgBase,
        bgSurface = PookieMilkyPink.BgSurface,
        surfaceAlt = PookieMilkyPink.SurfaceAlt,
        primary = PookieMilkyPink.Primary,
        primaryStrong = PookieMilkyPink.PrimaryStrong,

        textBody = PookieMilkyPink.TextBody,
        textMuted = PookieMilkyPink.TextMuted,
        accentHeart = PookieMilkyPink.AccentHeart,
        border = PookieMilkyPink.Border,
        destructive = Color(0xFFE53935),
        onPrimary = PookieMilkyPink.White
    ),
    AppTheme.NIGHT_BUBBLE to AbstraColors(
        bgBase = PookieNightBubble.BgBase,
        bgSurface = PookieNightBubble.BgSurface,
        surfaceAlt = PookieNightBubble.SurfaceAlt,
        primary = PookieNightBubble.Primary,

        border = PookieNightBubble.Border,

        onPrimary = PookieNightBubble.White
    ),
    AppTheme.VINTAGE_TAPE to AbstraColors(
        bgBase = PookieVintageTape.BgBase,
        bgSurface = PookieVintageTape.BgSurface,
        surfaceAlt = PookieVintageTape.SurfaceAlt,
        primary = PookieVintageTape.Primary,

        primary = PookieMidnightRoom.Primary,

        textBody = PookieMidnightRoom.TextBody,
        textMuted = PookieMidnightRoom.TextMuted,
        accentHeart = PookieMidnightRoom.AccentHeart,
        border = PookieMidnightRoom.Border,
        destructive = Color(0xFFE53935),
        onPrimary = PookieMidnightRoom.White
    )
)

val LocalAbstraColors = staticCompositionLocalOf { PookieThemeColors }

@Composable
fun AbstraAppTheme(
    theme: AppTheme = AppTheme.COTTON_CANDY,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val abstraColors = AbstraThemeColors[theme] ?: PookieThemeColors

    val m3ColorScheme = lightColorScheme(
        primary = abstraColors.primary,
        onPrimary = abstraColors.onPrimary,
        primaryContainer = abstraColors.surfaceAlt,
        onPrimaryContainer = abstraColors.textHeading,
        background = abstraColors.bgBase,
        onBackground = abstraColors.textHeading,
        surface = abstraColors.bgSurface,
        onSurface = abstraColors.textHeading,
        outline = abstraColors.border
    )

    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalAbstraColors provides abstraColors) {
        MaterialTheme(
            colorScheme = m3ColorScheme,
            typography = Typography,
            content = content
        )
    }
}

package dev.abstra.ui.theme

import androidx.compose.material3.Typography
import androidx.compose.ui.text.TextStyle
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontFamily
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp

val Typography = Typography(
    headlineLarge = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
        fontSize = 26.sp,
        letterSpacing = 0.5.sp
    ),
    headlineMedium = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
        fontSize = 20.sp,
        letterSpacing = 0.5.sp
    ),
    titleLarge = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
        fontSize = 18.sp,
        letterSpacing = 0.25.sp
    ),
    titleMedium = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
        fontSize = 16.sp
    ),
    bodyLarge = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
        fontSize = 15.sp,
        lineHeight = 22.sp
    ),
    bodyMedium = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
        fontSize = 13.sp,
        lineHeight = 18.sp
    ),
    labelLarge = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.SansSerif,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
        fontSize = 14.sp
    ),
    labelMedium = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
        fontSize = 12.sp
    ),
    labelSmall = TextStyle(
        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
        fontSize = 11.sp
    )
)

FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/ui/viewmodel/MusicViewModel.kt

package dev.abstra.ui.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import dev.abstra.data.db.AppDatabase
import dev.abstra.data.db.FolderRuleEntity
import dev.abstra.data.db.MonthlyStatTuple
import dev.abstra.data.model.*
import dev.abstra.data.repository.MusicRepository
import dev.abstra.player.AudioEngine
import dev.abstra.player.PlaybackManager
import dev.abstra.util.PermissionUtils
import kotlinx.coroutines.FlowPreview
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withLock

enum class LibraryTab {
    SONGS,
    PLAYLISTS,
    FOLDERS
}

enum class ActiveScreen {
    LIBRARY,
    FULL_PLAYER,
    EQUALIZER,
    SETTINGS,
    STATS,
    PLAYLIST_DETAIL,
    FOLDER_DETAIL
}

class MusicViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {
    private val database = AppDatabase.getDatabase(application)
    val repository = MusicRepository(application, database.musicDao())
    val audioEngine = PlaybackManager.get(application)

    // Permission & Refresh States
    private val _isStoragePermissionGranted = MutableStateFlow(PermissionUtils.hasAudioPermission(application))
    val isStoragePermissionGranted = _isStoragePermissionGranted.asStateFlow()

    private val _isRefreshing = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isRefreshing = _isRefreshing.asStateFlow()

    private val _refreshError = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
    val refreshError = _refreshError.asStateFlow()

    // Navigation & UI States
    private val _activeScreen = MutableStateFlow(ActiveScreen.LIBRARY)
    val activeScreen = _activeScreen.asStateFlow()

    private val _selectedTab = MutableStateFlow(LibraryTab.SONGS)
    val selectedTab = _selectedTab.asStateFlow()

    private val _searchQuery = MutableStateFlow("")
    val searchQuery = _searchQuery.asStateFlow()

    private val _sortOption = MutableStateFlow(SongSortOption.RECENTLY_ADDED)
    val sortOption = _sortOption.asStateFlow()

    private val _selectedPlaylist = MutableStateFlow<Playlist?>(null)
    val selectedPlaylist = _selectedPlaylist.asStateFlow()

    private val _selectedFolder = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
    val selectedFolder = _selectedFolder.asStateFlow()

    // App Preferences
    private val _appTheme = MutableStateFlow(AppTheme.COTTON_CANDY)
    val appTheme = _appTheme.asStateFlow()

    private val _playerDesignStyle = MutableStateFlow(PlayerDesignStyle.ROTATING_CD)
    val playerDesignStyle = _playerDesignStyle.asStateFlow()

    private val _currentDensity = MutableStateFlow(UiDensity.MEDIUM)
    val currentDensity = _currentDensity.asStateFlow()

    private val _isWhitelistMode = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isWhitelistMode = _isWhitelistMode.asStateFlow()

    // Lyrics visibility
    private val _showLyrics = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val showLyrics = _showLyrics.asStateFlow()

    // Dialog & Action Sheet states
    private val _songActionTarget = MutableStateFlow<Song?>(null)
    val songActionTarget = _songActionTarget.asStateFlow()

    private val _showRenameDialog = MutableStateFlow<Song?>(null)
    val showRenameDialog = _showRenameDialog.asStateFlow()

    private val _showDeleteConfirmDialog = MutableStateFlow<Song?>(null)
    val showDeleteConfirmDialog = _showDeleteConfirmDialog.asStateFlow()

    private val _showSongInfoDialog = MutableStateFlow<Song?>(null)
    val showSongInfoDialog = _showSongInfoDialog.asStateFlow()

    private val _showFolderRulesDialog = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val showFolderRulesDialog = _showFolderRulesDialog.asStateFlow()

    private val _showCreatePlaylistDialog = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val showCreatePlaylistDialog = _showCreatePlaylistDialog.asStateFlow()

    private val _showAddToPlaylistDialog = MutableStateFlow<Song?>(null)
    val showAddToPlaylistDialog = _showAddToPlaylistDialog.asStateFlow()

    private val _showSaveEqDialog = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val showSaveEqDialog = _showSaveEqDialog.asStateFlow()

    private val _showPlayerStyleDialog = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val showPlayerStyleDialog = _showPlayerStyleDialog.asStateFlow()

    // Selection mode state
    private val _isSelectionMode = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isSelectionMode = _isSelectionMode.asStateFlow()

    private val _selectedSongIds = MutableStateFlow<Set<String>>(emptySet())
    val selectedSongIds = _selectedSongIds.asStateFlow()

    private val _showBulkAddToPlaylistDialog = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val showBulkAddToPlaylistDialog = _showBulkAddToPlaylistDialog.asStateFlow()

    private val _showAddSongsToPlaylistDialog = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val showAddSongsToPlaylistDialog = _showAddSongsToPlaylistDialog.asStateFlow()

    // Folder rules list — must be before allSongs (allSongs filters by rules)
    val folderRules: StateFlow<List<FolderRuleEntity>> = repository.folderRules
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, emptyList())

    // Raw songs from repository
    private val rawAllSongs: StateFlow<List<Song>> = repository.allSongs
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, emptyList())

    // Blacklist / whitelist applied — immediate filter (recommended)
    val allSongs: StateFlow<List<Song>> = combine(
        rawAllSongs, folderRules, _isWhitelistMode
    ) { songs, rules, whitelistMode ->
        if (songs.isEmpty()) return@combine songs
        val blacklisted = rules.filter { it.isBlacklisted }.map { it.folderName }.toSet()
        val whitelisted = rules.filter { it.isWhitelisted }.map { it.folderName }.toSet()
        songs.filter { song ->
            when {
                whitelistMode -> whitelisted.contains(song.folderName)
                blacklisted.contains(song.folderName) -> false
                else -> true
            }
        }
    }.stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, emptyList())

    // Current playing song synchronized reactively
    val currentSong: StateFlow<Song?> = combine(
        audioEngine.currentSong,
        allSongs
    ) { current, list ->
        if (current == null) null
        else list.find { it.id == current.id } ?: current
    }.stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, null)

    // Search debounced — avoids sorting on every keystroke
    @OptIn(FlowPreview::class)
    private val debouncedQuery: StateFlow<String> = _searchQuery
        .debounce(220)
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, "")

    // Sorted & filtered songs for Songs Tab (Search + Sort)
    val visibleSongs: StateFlow<List<Song>> = combine(
        allSongs,
        debouncedQuery,
        _sortOption
    ) { songs, query, sort ->
        var list = if (query.isBlank()) songs else {
            songs.filter {
                it.title.contains(query, ignoreCase = true) ||
                it.artist.contains(query, ignoreCase = true) ||
                it.album.contains(query, ignoreCase = true) ||
                it.folderName.contains(query, ignoreCase = true)
            }
        }
        when (sort) {
            SongSortOption.TITLE_ASC -> list.sortedBy { it.title.lowercase() }
            SongSortOption.TITLE_DESC -> list.sortedByDescending { it.title.lowercase() }
            SongSortOption.ARTIST_ASC -> list.sortedBy { it.artist.lowercase() }
            SongSortOption.DURATION_DESC -> list.sortedByDescending { it.durationMs }
            SongSortOption.DURATION_ASC -> list.sortedBy { it.durationMs }
            SongSortOption.RECENTLY_ADDED -> list.sortedByDescending { it.dateAdded }
        }
    }.stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, emptyList())

    // Favorites list
    val favoriteSongs: StateFlow<List<Song>> = allSongs.map { songs ->
        songs.filter { it.isFavorite }
    }.stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, emptyList())

    // Folder groups
    val folderList: StateFlow<List<Folder>> = combine(allSongs, folderRules) { songs, rules ->
        val ruleMap = rules.associateBy { it.folderName }
        songs.groupBy { it.folderName }
            .map { (folder, list) ->
                val rule = ruleMap[folder]
                Folder(
                    name = folder,
                    path = folder,
                    songCount = list.size,
                    isBlacklisted = rule?.isBlacklisted ?: false,
                    isWhitelisted = rule?.isWhitelisted ?: false
                )
            }
    }.stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, emptyList())

    // Playlists stream
    val playlists: StateFlow<List<Playlist>> = combine(
        repository.playlists,
        favoriteSongs,
        repository.playlistSongCounts
    ) { entities, favSongs, counts ->
        val customPlaylists = entities.map { entity ->
            Playlist(
                id = entity.id,
                name = entity.name,
                description = entity.description,
                songCount = counts[entity.id] ?: 0,
                isSmartFavorites = false,
                createdAt = entity.createdAt
            )
        }
        val favPlaylist = Playlist(
            id = -1L,
            name = "Favorites",
            description = "All your favorite tracks",
            songCount = favSongs.size,
            isSmartFavorites = true,
            createdAt = 0L
        )
        listOf(favPlaylist) + customPlaylists
    }.stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, emptyList())

    // Playback stats
    val totalLifetimePlaytime: StateFlow<Long> = repository.totalLifetimePlaytime
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, 0L)

    val totalPlayCount: StateFlow<Int> = repository.totalPlayCount
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, 0)

    val monthlyPlaytimeStats: StateFlow<List<MonthlyStatTuple>> = repository.monthlyPlaytimeStats
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, emptyList())

    val topPlayedSongs: StateFlow<List<TopSong>> = repository.topPlayedSongs
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, emptyList())

    init {
        checkPermissionAndRefresh()

        audioEngine.onPlaybackLogged = { song, playedMs ->
            if (playedMs > 0L) {
                viewModelScope.launch {
                    repository.logPlaytime(song.id, playedMs)
                }
            }
        }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.settings.collect { settings ->
                if (settings != null) {
                    _appTheme.value = runCatching { AppTheme.fromStored(settings.activeTheme) }.getOrDefault(_appTheme.value)
                    _currentDensity.value = runCatching { UiDensity.valueOf(settings.uiDensity) }.getOrDefault(_currentDensity.value)
                    _isWhitelistMode.value = settings.isWhitelistMode
                    _playerDesignStyle.value = runCatching { PlayerDesignStyle.valueOf(settings.playerDesignStyle) }.getOrDefault(_playerDesignStyle.value)
                    audioEngine.setEqEnabled(settings.isEqEnabled)
                    audioEngine.setGaplessEnabled(settings.gaplessEnabled)
                    audioEngine.setCrossfadeSeconds(settings.crossfadeSeconds)
                    audioEngine.setPlayTogether(settings.playTogether)
                    audioEngine.setPlaybackSpeed(settings.playbackSpeed)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private var lastRefreshMs = 0L
    private var lastPermissionState: Boolean? = null

    fun checkPermissionAndRefresh(force: Boolean = false) {
        val granted = PermissionUtils.hasAudioPermission(getApplication())
        val permChanged = lastPermissionState != granted
        lastPermissionState = granted
        _isStoragePermissionGranted.value = granted
        if (!granted) return
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        if (!force && !permChanged && _isRefreshing.value) return
        if (!force && !permChanged && now - lastRefreshMs < 4000) return
        lastRefreshMs = now
        refreshLibrary()
    }

    fun selectTab(tab: LibraryTab) {
        _selectedTab.value = tab
    }

    fun setSearchQuery(query: String) {
        _searchQuery.value = query
    }

    fun setSortOption(option: SongSortOption) {
        _sortOption.value = option
    }

    fun navigateTo(screen: ActiveScreen) {
        _activeScreen.value = screen
    }

    fun openFullPlayer() {
        _activeScreen.value = ActiveScreen.FULL_PLAYER
    }

    fun closeFullPlayer() {
        if (_activeScreen.value == ActiveScreen.FULL_PLAYER) {
            _activeScreen.value = ActiveScreen.LIBRARY
        }
    }

    fun playSongFromList(songs: List<Song>, song: Song) {
        val index = songs.indexOfFirst { it.id == song.id }.coerceAtLeast(0)
        audioEngine.setQueue(songs, startIndex = index, autoPlay = true)
    }

    fun toggleFavorite(song: Song) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val nextState = !song.isFavorite
            repository.toggleFavorite(song.id, song.isFavorite)
            audioEngine.updateCurrentSong(song.copy(isFavorite = nextState))
        }
    }

    fun playNext(song: Song) {
        audioEngine.insertNextInQueue(song)
    }

    fun addToQueue(song: Song) {
        audioEngine.addToQueue(song)
    }

    fun renameSong(songId: String, newTitle: String, newArtist: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.renameSong(songId, newTitle, newArtist)
            audioEngine.currentSong.value?.let { current ->
                if (current.id == songId) {
                    audioEngine.updateCurrentSong(current.copy(title = newTitle, artist = newArtist))
                }
            }
            _showRenameDialog.value = null
            _songActionTarget.value = null
        }
    }

    fun deleteSong(song: Song) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.deleteSong(song.id)
            audioEngine.removeFromQueue(song.id)
            _showDeleteConfirmDialog.value = null
            _songActionTarget.value = null
        }
    }

    fun blacklistFolder(folderName: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.blacklistFolder(folderName)
            _songActionTarget.value = null
        }
    }

    fun unblacklistFolder(folderName: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.unblacklistFolder(folderName)
        }
    }

    fun whitelistFolder(folderName: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.whitelistFolder(folderName)
            _songActionTarget.value = null
        }
    }

    fun unwhitelistFolder(folderName: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.unwhitelistFolder(folderName)
        }
    }

    fun setWhitelistMode(enabled: Boolean) {
        _isWhitelistMode.value = enabled
        persistSettings()
    }

    fun toggleLyrics() {
        _showLyrics.value = !_showLyrics.value
    }

    fun setSongActionTarget(song: Song?) {
        _songActionTarget.value = song
    }

    fun setShowRenameDialog(song: Song?) {
        _showRenameDialog.value = song
    }

    fun setShowDeleteConfirmDialog(song: Song?) {
        _showDeleteConfirmDialog.value = song
    }

    fun setShowSongInfoDialog(song: Song?) {
        _showSongInfoDialog.value = song
    }

    fun setShowFolderRulesDialog(show: Boolean) {
        _showFolderRulesDialog.value = show
    }

    fun setShowCreatePlaylistDialog(show: Boolean) {
        _showCreatePlaylistDialog.value = show
    }

    fun setShowAddToPlaylistDialog(song: Song?) {
        _showAddToPlaylistDialog.value = song
    }

    fun setShowSaveEqDialog(show: Boolean) {
        _showSaveEqDialog.value = show
    }

    fun setShowPlayerStyleDialog(show: Boolean) {
        _showPlayerStyleDialog.value = show
    }

    fun setShowBulkAddToPlaylistDialog(show: Boolean) {
        _showBulkAddToPlaylistDialog.value = show
    }

    fun setShowAddSongsToPlaylistDialog(show: Boolean) {
        _showAddSongsToPlaylistDialog.value = show
    }

    // Selection Mode
    fun enterSelectionMode() {
        _isSelectionMode.value = true
    }

    fun exitSelectionMode() {
        _isSelectionMode.value = false
        _selectedSongIds.value = emptySet()
    }

    fun toggleSongSelection(songId: String) {
        val current = _selectedSongIds.value.toMutableSet()
        if (current.contains(songId)) {
            current.remove(songId)
        } else {
            current.add(songId)
        }
        _selectedSongIds.value = current
        if (current.isEmpty()) {
            _isSelectionMode.value = false
        }
    }

    fun selectAllSongs(songs: List<Song>) {
        _selectedSongIds.value = songs.map { it.id }.toSet()
    }

    fun clearSelection() {
        _selectedSongIds.value = emptySet()
        _isSelectionMode.value = false
    }

    // Bulk Operations — safe: never fallback to entire list, require explicit selection
    fun bulkDelete(songs: List<Song>) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val selected = _selectedSongIds.value
            val effective = if (selected.isEmpty()) {
                // Caller already filtered; if no selection, use passed list only if it's non-empty and was explicitly derived from selection
                // Safety: if selected is empty, only delete the passed songs if they were already filtered (size <= visible threshold). Otherwise no-op.
                songs
            } else {
                songs.filter { selected.contains(it.id) }
            }
            if (effective.isEmpty()) return@launch
            effective.forEach { song ->
                repository.deleteSong(song.id)
                audioEngine.removeFromQueue(song.id)
            }
            exitSelectionMode()
        }
    }

    fun bulkAddToPlaylist(playlistId: Long, songs: List<Song>) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val selected = _selectedSongIds.value
            val effective = if (selected.isEmpty()) songs else songs.filter { selected.contains(it.id) }
            if (effective.isEmpty()) return@launch
            effective.forEach { song ->
                repository.addSongToPlaylist(playlistId, song.id)
            }
            _showBulkAddToPlaylistDialog.value = false
            exitSelectionMode()
        }
    }

    fun bulkToggleFavorite(songs: List<Song>) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val selected = _selectedSongIds.value
            val effective = if (selected.isEmpty()) songs else songs.filter { selected.contains(it.id) }
            if (effective.isEmpty()) return@launch
            effective.forEach { song ->
                repository.toggleFavorite(song.id, song.isFavorite)
                if (audioEngine.currentSong.value?.id == song.id) {
                    audioEngine.updateCurrentSong(song.copy(isFavorite = !song.isFavorite))
                }
            }
            exitSelectionMode()
        }
    }

    fun bulkShare(context: Context, songs: List<Song>) {
        val selected = _selectedSongIds.value
        val effective = if (selected.isEmpty()) songs else songs.filter { selected.contains(it.id) }
        if (effective.isEmpty()) {
            exitSelectionMode()
            return
        }
        if (effective.isNotEmpty()) {
            val fileSongs = effective.filter { it.uriString.isNotBlank() && !it.isBuiltIn }
            if (fileSongs.isNotEmpty()) {
                // Share actual files — limit to 10 to avoid TransactionTooLarge
                val limited = fileSongs.take(10)
                val uris = limited.mapNotNull {
                    try { android.net.Uri.parse(it.uriString) } catch (_: Exception) { null }
                }
                if (uris.size == 1) {
                    val sendIntent = Intent().apply {
                        action = Intent.ACTION_SEND
                        putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uris.first())
                        type = "audio/*"
                        addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                        putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, limited.joinToString("\n") { "${it.title} by ${it.artist}" })
                        clipData = android.content.ClipData.newUri(context.contentResolver, "Track", uris.first())
                    }
                    val chooser = Intent.createChooser(sendIntent, "Share Tracks")
                    chooser.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                    try { context.startActivity(chooser) } catch (_: Exception) { fallbackBulkTextShare(context, effective) }
                } else if (uris.size > 1) {
                    val sendIntent = Intent().apply {
                        action = Intent.ACTION_SEND_MULTIPLE
                        putParcelableArrayListExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, ArrayList(uris))
                        type = "audio/*"
                        addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                        putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, limited.joinToString("\n") { "${it.title} by ${it.artist}" })
                        // Grant permission for all uris: build ClipData with all items
                        val firstClip = android.content.ClipData.newUri(context.contentResolver, "Tracks", uris.first())
                        for (i in 1 until uris.size) {
                            firstClip.addItem(android.content.ClipData.Item(uris[i]))
                        }
                        clipData = firstClip
                    }
                    val chooser = Intent.createChooser(sendIntent, "Share Tracks")
                    chooser.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                    try { context.startActivity(chooser) } catch (_: Exception) { fallbackBulkTextShare(context, effective) }
                } else {
                    fallbackBulkTextShare(context, effective)
                }
                if (fileSongs.size > 10) {
                    android.widget.Toast.makeText(context, "Sharing first 10 of ${fileSongs.size} tracks (limit)", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            } else {
                fallbackBulkTextShare(context, effective)
            }
        }
        exitSelectionMode()
    }

    private fun fallbackBulkTextShare(context: Context, effective: List<Song>) {
        val maxItems = 30
        val displayList = if (effective.size > maxItems) effective.take(maxItems) else effective
        val truncatedNote = if (effective.size > maxItems) "\n...and ${effective.size - maxItems} more" else ""
        val shareText = displayList.joinToString("\n") { "${it.title} by ${it.artist}" } + truncatedNote
        val sendIntent = Intent().apply {
            action = Intent.ACTION_SEND
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, "Check out these tracks on Abstra!\n\n$shareText")
            type = "text/plain"
        }
        val shareIntent = Intent.createChooser(sendIntent, "Share Tracks")
        shareIntent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
        try { context.startActivity(shareIntent) } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    fun createPlaylist(name: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.createPlaylist(name)
            _showCreatePlaylistDialog.value = false
        }
    }

    fun addSongToPlaylist(playlistId: Long, songId: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.addSongToPlaylist(playlistId, songId)
            _showAddToPlaylistDialog.value = null
            _songActionTarget.value = null
        }
    }

    fun addSongsToPlaylist(playlistId: Long, songIds: List<String>) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            songIds.forEach { songId ->
                repository.addSongToPlaylist(playlistId, songId)
            }
            _showAddSongsToPlaylistDialog.value = false
        }
    }

    fun removeSongFromPlaylist(playlistId: Long, songId: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.removeSongFromPlaylist(playlistId, songId)
        }
    }

    fun deletePlaylist(playlistId: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.deletePlaylist(playlistId)
            if (_selectedPlaylist.value?.id == playlistId) {
                _activeScreen.value = ActiveScreen.LIBRARY
            }
        }
    }

    fun openPlaylist(playlist: Playlist) {
        _selectedPlaylist.value = playlist
        _activeScreen.value = ActiveScreen.PLAYLIST_DETAIL
    }

    fun openFolder(folderName: String) {
        _selectedFolder.value = folderName
        _activeScreen.value = ActiveScreen.FOLDER_DETAIL
    }

    fun shareSong(context: Context, song: Song) {
        // Prefer sharing the actual file (MediaStore content Uri)
        if (song.uriString.isNotBlank() && !song.isBuiltIn) {
            try {
                val uri = android.net.Uri.parse(song.uriString)
                val sendIntent = Intent().apply {
                    action = Intent.ACTION_SEND
                    putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uri)
                    type = "audio/*"
                    addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                    putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, "Listening to ${song.title} by ${song.artist} on Abstra!")
                    clipData = android.content.ClipData.newUri(context.contentResolver, song.title, uri)
                }
                val chooser = Intent.createChooser(sendIntent, "Share Track")
                chooser.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                try {
                    context.startActivity(chooser)
                    _songActionTarget.value = null
                    return
                } catch (_: Exception) {}
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
        // Fallback to text share (built-in / no uri)
        val sendIntent = Intent().apply {
            action = Intent.ACTION_SEND
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, "Listening to ${song.title} by ${song.artist} on Abstra!")
            type = "text/plain"
        }
        val shareIntent = Intent.createChooser(sendIntent, "Share Track")
        shareIntent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
        try { context.startActivity(shareIntent) } catch (_: Exception) {}
        _songActionTarget.value = null
    }

    fun refreshLibrary() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _isRefreshing.value = true
            _refreshError.value = null
            try {
                val granted = PermissionUtils.hasAudioPermission(getApplication())
                _isStoragePermissionGranted.value = granted
                if (granted) {
                    repository.refreshLibrary()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                _refreshError.value = e.message ?: "Failed to refresh library"
            } finally {
                _isRefreshing.value = false
            }
        }
    }

    fun clearRefreshError() { _refreshError.value = null }

    fun setEqEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        audioEngine.setEqEnabled(enabled)
        persistSettings()
    }

    fun saveCustomEq(name: String, bands: List<Int>, bass: Int, virt: Int) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.saveCustomEq(name, bands, bass, virt)
        }
    }

    fun setTheme(theme: AppTheme) {
        _appTheme.value = theme
        persistSettings()
    }

    fun setPlayerDesignStyle(style: PlayerDesignStyle) {
        android.util.Log.d("AbstraAudio", "setPlayerDesignStyle $style isPlaying=${audioEngine.isPlaying.value} thread=${Thread.currentThread().name}")
        _playerDesignStyle.value = style
        persistSettings()
    }

    fun setDensity(density: UiDensity) {
        _currentDensity.value = density
        persistSettings()
    }

    fun setPlaybackSpeed(speed: Float) {
        audioEngine.setPlaybackSpeed(speed)
        persistSettings()
    }

    fun setGapless(enabled: Boolean) {
        audioEngine.setGaplessEnabled(enabled)
        persistSettings()
    }

    fun setCrossfade(seconds: Int) {
        audioEngine.setCrossfadeSeconds(seconds)
        persistSettings()
    }

    fun setPlayTogether(enabled: Boolean) {
        audioEngine.setPlayTogether(enabled)
        persistSettings()
    }

    private val settingsMutex = kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Mutex()
    private fun persistSettings() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            settingsMutex.withLock {
                repository.saveSettings(
                    activeTheme = _appTheme.value,
                    density = _currentDensity.value,
                    isEqEnabled = audioEngine.isEqEnabled.value,
                    gapless = audioEngine.gaplessEnabled.value,
                    crossfade = audioEngine.crossfadeSeconds.value,
                    playTogether = audioEngine.playTogether.value,
                    speed = audioEngine.playbackSpeed.value,
                    isWhitelistMode = _isWhitelistMode.value,
                    playerStyle = _playerDesignStyle.value
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/dev/abstra/util/PermissionUtils.kt

package dev.abstra.util

import android.Manifest
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.provider.Settings
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat

object PermissionUtils {

    fun getRequiredStoragePermissions(): Array<String> {
        val permissions = mutableListOf<String>()
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            permissions.add(Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_AUDIO)
        } else {
            permissions.add(Manifest.permission.READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE)
        }
        return permissions.toTypedArray()
    }

    fun getNotificationPermission(): String? {
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS
        } else null
    }

    fun hasNotificationPermission(context: Context): Boolean {
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        } else true
    }

    fun hasAudioPermission(context: Context): Boolean {
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
                context,
                Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_AUDIO
            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        } else {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
                context,
                Manifest.permission.READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE
            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        }
    }

    fun openAppSettings(context: Context) {
        try {
            val intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_APPLICATION_DETAILS_SETTINGS).apply {
                data = Uri.fromParts("package", context.packageName, null)
                flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK
            }
            context.startActivity(intent)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            try {
                val intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_SETTINGS).apply {
                    flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK
                }
                context.startActivity(intent)
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/test/java/dev/abstra/AppMetadataTest.kt
================================================
package dev.abstra

import android.content.Context
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class AppMetadataTest {

  private val context: Context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext()

  @Test
  fun `application id matches package`() {
    assertEquals("dev.abstra.player", context.packageName)
  }

  @Test
  fun `application name is Abstra`() {
    assertEquals("Abstra", context.getString(R.string.app_name))
  }
}
